## Becuma of the White Skin / 白肤贝库玛

### I / I

===Original===
There are more worlds than one, and in many ways they are unlike each other. But joy and sorrow, or, in other words, good and evil, are not absent in their degree from any of the worlds, for wherever there is life there is action, and action is but the expression of one or other of these qualities.

===Chinese===
世界不止一个，它们在许多方面互不相同。可无论在哪个世界里，欢乐与悲哀——换句话说，善与恶——都从不缺席，只是各有各的分量；因为凡有生命处即有行动，而行动，无非是这两种性质之一的表露。

===Original===
After this Earth there is the world of the Shí. Beyond it again lies the Many-Coloured Land. Next comes the Land of Wonder, and after that the Land of Promise awaits us. You will cross clay to get into the Shí; you will cross water to attain the Many-Coloured Land; fire must be passed ere the Land of Wonder is attained, but we do not know what will be crossed for the fourth world.

===Chinese===
尘世之外，有仙丘（the Shí）的世界。再往外，横着斑斓之国（the Many-Coloured Land）。接下来是奇境（the Land of Wonder），再过去，应许之地（the Land of Promise）正等着我们。进仙丘，须跨越泥土；到斑斓之国，须跨越水；要抵达奇境，必先穿越火；至于第四个世界须跨越什么，我们就不得而知了。

===Original===
This adventure of Conn the Hundred Fighter and his son Art was by the way of water, and therefore he was more advanced in magic than Fionn was, all of whose adventures were by the path of clay and into Faery only, but Conn was the High King and so the arch-magician of Ireland.

===Chinese===
「百战」康恩（Conn the Hundred Fighter）和他儿子阿特（Art）的这场奇遇走的是水路；因此论法术，他比芬恩（Fionn）更为高深——芬恩的种种奇遇走的都是土路，而且仅止于仙境（Faery）；何况康恩身为至高王（the High King），也就顺理成章，是爱尔兰的首席大法师。

===Original===
A council had been called in the Many-Coloured Land to discuss the case of a lady named Becuma Cneisgel, that is, Becuma of the White Skin, the daughter of Eogan Inver. She had run away from her husband Labraid and had taken refuge with Gadiar, one of the sons of Manannán mac Lir, the god of the sea, and the ruler, therefore, of that sphere.

===Chinese===
斑斓之国里召开了一场议事会，审理一位女士的案子：她名叫贝库玛·克尼斯格尔（Becuma Cneisgel），意即白肤贝库玛，是埃奥甘·因弗（Eogan Inver）的女儿。她抛下丈夫拉布拉德（Labraid）私逃，投奔了加迪亚尔（Gadiar）——海神玛纳南·麦克·利尔（Manannán mac Lir）的儿子之一；海神既是海之神，自然也是那一界的统治者。

===Original===
It seems, then, that there is marriage in two other spheres. In the Shí matrimony is recorded as being parallel in every respect with earth-marriage, and the desire which urges to it seems to be as violent and inconstant as it is with us; but in the Many-Coloured Land marriage is but a contemplation of beauty, a brooding and meditation wherein all grosser desire is unknown and children are born to sinless parents.

===Chinese===
这样看来，此外两界也是有婚姻的。据记载，仙丘的婚配与尘世的婚姻事事相当，促人成婚的那股欲望，看来也同我们这里的一样，狂热而善变；可在斑斓之国，婚姻不过是对美的观照，是一场孵育般的沉思冥想，其间全然不知更粗鄙的欲望为何物，孩子生下来，父母皆无罪。

===Original===
In the Shí the crime of Becuma would have been lightly considered, and would have received none or but a nominal punishment, but in the second world a horrid gravity attaches to such a lapse, and the retribution meted is implacable and grim. It may be dissolution by fire, and that can note a destruction too final for the mind to contemplate; or it may be banishment from that sphere to a lower and worse one.

===Chinese===
贝库玛这桩罪若在仙丘，本会被看得很轻，或不加惩罚，或只施一个名义上的发落；可在这第二重世界里，这样的失足却系着一种可怕的严肃，量出的报应无情而阴冷。或是以火销形——那意味着一种心智无法凝想的、彻底到头的毁灭；或是从那一界放逐到更低、更坏的一界去。

===Original===
This was the fate of Becuma of the White Skin.

===Chinese===
这便是白肤贝库玛的命运。

===Original===
One may wonder how, having attained to that sphere, she could have carried with her so strong a memory of the earth. It is certain that she was not a fit person to exist in the Many-Coloured Land, and it is to be feared that she was organised too grossly even for life in the Shí.

===Chinese===
人们也许要纳罕：她既然升到了那一界，怎么还能随身带着对尘世如此强烈的记忆。可以肯定，她本就不是宜居于斑斓之国的人；只怕她的禀性粗浊得太厉害，连仙丘里的生活也容不下她。

===Original===
She was an earth-woman, and she was banished to the earth.

===Chinese===
她是个尘世的女人，于是被放逐到了尘世。

===Original===
Word was sent to the Shís of Ireland that this lady should not be permitted to enter any of them; from which it would seem that the ordinances of the Shí come from the higher world, and, it might follow, that the conduct of earth lies in the Shí.

===Chinese===
旨令传遍爱尔兰的诸仙丘：不许这位女士进入其中任何一座。由此看来，仙丘的法度似出自更高的世界；而依此推论，尘世的行止善恶，怕也就握在仙丘手里。

===Original===
In that way, the gates of her own world and the innumerable doors of Faery being closed against her, Becuma was forced to appear in the world of men.

===Chinese===
就这样，自己世界的大门与仙境的无数门扉一齐对她关死了，贝库玛只得现身于人类的世界。

===Original===
It is pleasant, however, notwithstanding her terrible crime and her woeful punishment, to think how courageous she was. When she was told her sentence, nay, her doom, she made no outcry, nor did she waste any time in sorrow. She went home and put on her nicest clothes.

===Chinese===
然而，尽管她罪孽可怕、惩罚堪哀，想到她何等勇敢，总还是件叫人愉快的事。判决宣下来的时候——不，是她的劫数（Doom）宣下来的时候——她没有喊一声冤，也没有把光阴枉费在悲伤里。她回家去，穿上了自己最漂亮的衣裳。

===Original===
She wore a red satin smock, and, over this, a cloak of green silk out of which long fringes of gold swung and sparkled, and she had light sandals of white bronze on her thin, shapely feet. She had long soft hair that was yellow as gold, and soft as the curling foam of the sea. Her eyes were wide and clear as water and were grey as a dove’s breast. Her teeth were white as snow and of an evenness to marvel at. Her lips were thin and beautifully curved: red lips in truth, red as winter berries and tempting as the fruits of summer. The people who superintended her departure said mournfully that when she was gone there would be no more beauty left in their world.

===Chinese===
她穿一袭红缎衬袍，外面罩一件绿丝斗篷，斗篷上长长的金穗摇曳生辉；一双纤秀的脚上，趿着白铜打的轻便凉鞋。她一头长发柔软，黄得像金子，软得像海上卷起的泡沫。她的眼睛又阔又清，像一泓水，灰得像鸽子的胸脯。她的牙齿白得像雪，齐整得令人称奇。她的嘴唇薄薄的，弧线优美：那是真正的红唇，红得像冬天的浆果，诱人如夏天的果实。监送她离去的人们哀哀地说，她一走，他们这个世界里，美也就一无所剩了。

===Original===
She stepped into a coracle, it was pushed on the enchanted waters, and it went forward, world within world, until land appeared, and her boat swung in low tide against a rock at the foot of Ben Edair.

===Chinese===
她跨进一只柳条小舟（coracle），小舟被推上有魔法的海面，一路前行，一重世界套着一重世界，直到陆地出现，她的船才顺着落潮，悠悠荡在本·埃代尔（Ben Edair）山脚的一块岩石旁。

===Original===
So far for her.

===Chinese===
她的事，至此说罢。

### II / II

===Original===
Conn the Hundred Fighter, Ard-Rí of Ireland, was in the lowest spirits that can be imagined, for his wife was dead. He had been Ard-Ri for nine years, and during his term the corn used to be reaped three times in each year, and there was full and plenty of everything. There are few kings who can boast of more kingly results than he can, but there was sore trouble in store for him.

===Chinese===
爱尔兰的至高王（Ard-Rí）「百战」康恩，正陷在人所能想象的最低落的情绪里，因为他的王后死了。他做至高王已有九年，任内谷物一年收割三茬，样样丰足充盈。能把王道业绩夸到他这个地步的国王没有几位，可等着他的，却是钻心的祸事。

===Original===
He had been married to Eithne, the daughter of Brisland Binn, King of Norway, and, next to his subjects, he loved his wife more than all that was lovable in the world. But the term of man and woman, of king or queen, is set in the stars, and there is no escaping Doom for anyone; so, when her time came, Eithne died.

===Chinese===
他娶的王后叫埃丝妮（Eithne），是挪威国王布里斯兰·宾（Brisland Binn）的女儿；除了子民之外，他爱王后胜过世上一切可爱之物。然而男人与女人、国王与王后的期限都写在星辰上，谁的劫数也逃不脱；于是她的时限一到，埃丝妮便死了。

===Original===
Now there were three great burying-places in Ireland﻿—the Brugh of the Boyne in Ulster, over which Angus Og is chief and god; the Shí mound of Cruachan Ahi, where Ethal Anbual presides over the underworld of Connacht, and Tailltin, in Royal Meath. It was in this last, the sacred place of his own lordship, that Conn laid his wife to rest.

===Chinese===
爱尔兰原有三大葬地：一是阿尔斯特博因河畔的布鲁格（the Brugh of the Boyne），由安格斯·奥格（Angus Og）在那里主宰为神；二是克鲁亨·阿伊（Cruachan Ahi）的仙丘土冢，埃塔尔·安布阿尔（Ethal Anbual）在那里统掌康诺特（Connacht）的冥府；三是王土米斯（Royal Meath）的塔尔廷（Tailltin）。康恩便把王后安葬在最后这一处——他自己领地内的圣地。

===Original===
Her funeral games were played during nine days. Her keen was sung by poets and harpers, and a cairn ten acres wide was heaved over her clay. Then the keening ceased and the games drew to an end; the princes of the Five Provinces returned by horse or by chariot to their own places; the concourse of mourners melted away, and there was nothing left by the great cairn but the sun that dozed upon it in the daytime, the heavy clouds that brooded on it in the night, and the desolate, memoried king.

===Chinese===
她的葬礼竞技进行了九天。诗人和竖琴手唱起她的挽歌，一座方圆十英亩的石冢堆上了她的遗骸。随后哭唱止息，竞技收场；五省的诸侯们或骑马或驾车，各回各的封地；吊唁的人潮渐渐散尽，大石冢旁什么都不剩了，只剩下白天在冢上打盹的太阳，夜里在冢上盘踞的重重乌云，还有那个凄凄惶惶、满怀追忆的王。

===Original===
For the dead queen had been so lovely that Conn could not forget her; she had been so kind at every moment that he could not but miss her at every moment; but it was in the Council Chamber and the Judgement Hall that he most pondered her memory. For she had also been wise, and lacking her guidance, all grave affairs seemed graver, shadowing each day and going with him to the pillow at night.

===Chinese===
因为死去的王后是那样可爱，康恩忘不了她；她是时时刻刻那样温存，他也时时刻刻不能不想念她；而最教他反复追想的地方，是在议事厅里，在审判厅里。因为她还很有智慧；失了她的指引，一切重大的政务显得愈发重大，白天给每一天蒙上阴影，夜里又随他一同上枕。

===Original===
The trouble of the king becomes the trouble of the subject, for how shall we live if judgement is withheld, or if faulty decisions are promulgated? Therefore, with the sorrow of the king, all Ireland was in grief, and it was the wish of every person that he should marry again.

===Chinese===
国王的烦恼便成了臣民的烦恼，因为裁决若迟迟不下，谬断若连连颁行，我们还怎么活命？于是，随着国王的哀伤，整个爱尔兰都悲恸起来，人人心里都盼着他再娶。

===Original===
Such an idea, however, did not occur to him, for he could not conceive how any woman should fill the place his queen had vacated. He grew more and more despondent, and less and less fitted to cope with affairs of state, and one day he instructed his son Art to take the rule during his absence, and he set out for Ben Edair.

===Chinese===
这样的念头他却从未起过，因为他想不出天下有哪个女人填得上王后空出的位子。他越来越消沉，越来越无力应付国务；有一天，他吩咐儿子阿特在他离开期间执掌朝政，自己动身往本·埃代尔去了。

===Original===
For a great wish had come upon him to walk beside the sea; to listen to the roll and boom of long, grey breakers; to gaze on an unfruitful, desolate wilderness of waters; and to forget in those sights all that he could forget, and if he could not forget then to remember all that he should remember.

===Chinese===
因为一股强烈的心愿攫住了他：要到海边去走一走；去听长长的灰色巨浪翻滚轰鸣；去凝望那不结果实的、荒凉的水的荒野；在那些景象里，能忘掉的都忘掉；若是忘不掉，那就把该记住的都记住。

===Original===
He was thus gazing and brooding when one day he observed a coracle drawing to the shore. A young girl stepped from it and walked to him among black boulders and patches of yellow sand.

===Chinese===
他正这样望着、想着，有一天，忽见一只柳条小舟向岸边驶来。一位年轻女子从舟中步出，踏着黑色的巨砾和一片片黄沙，朝他走来。

### III / III

===Original===
Being a king he had authority to ask questions. Conn asked her, therefore, all the questions that he could think of, for it is not every day that a lady drives from the sea, and she wearing a golden-fringed cloak of green silk through which a red satin smock peeped at the openings. She replied to his questions, but she did not tell him all the truth; for, indeed, she could not afford to.

===Chinese===
身为国王，他有权发问。于是康恩把自己想得出的问题问了个遍——毕竟不是天天都有女士从海上来，何况她披着金穗绿丝斗篷，开口处还露出里头红缎衬袍的一角。对他的问题她一一作答，却没有把实情和盘托出；因为，说实在的，她实话不起。

===Original===
She knew who he was, for she retained some of the powers proper to the worlds she had left, and as he looked on her soft yellow hair and on her thin red lips, Conn recognised, as all men do, that one who is lovely must also be good, and so he did not frame any inquiry on that count; for everything is forgotten in the presence of a pretty woman, and a magician can be bewitched also.

===Chinese===
她知道他是谁，因为她还保有几分离开的那些世界所固有的神通；而康恩望着她柔软的黄发、薄薄的红唇，也像天下所有男人一样认定：长得可爱的人必定心地良善，于是在这一条上便一句也没有追问；须知在漂亮女人面前，一切都会被忘掉，法师也是会被迷住的。

===Original===
She told Conn that the fame of his son Art had reached even the Many-Coloured Land, and that she had fallen in love with the boy. This did not seem unreasonable to one who had himself ventured much in Faery, and who had known so many of the people of that world leave their own land for the love of a mortal.

===Chinese===
她告诉康恩，他儿子阿特的名声连斑斓之国都传到了，她已经爱上了这个少年。这话在康恩听来并不离奇，因为他本人在仙境历过不少险，也见惯了那个世界的许多人为着爱一个凡人而离开自己的国土。

===Original===
“What is your name, my sweet lady?” said the king.

===Chinese===
“你叫什么名字，我可爱的女士？”国王问。

===Original===
“I am called Delvcaem (Fair Shape) and I am the daughter of Morgan,” she replied.

===Chinese===
“人们叫我德尔夫卡姆（Delvcaem），意即‘美好身形’，我是摩根（Morgan）的女儿。”她答道。

===Original===
“I have heard much of Morgan,” said the king. “He is a very great magician.”

===Chinese===
“摩根其人，我听到过不少，”国王说，“他是一位非常伟大的法师。”

===Original===
During this conversation Conn had been regarding her with the minute freedom which is right only in a king. At what precise instant he forgot his dead consort we do not know, but it is certain that at this moment his mind was no longer burdened with that dear and lovely memory. His voice was melancholy when he spoke again.

===Chinese===
这场谈话之间，康恩一直以那种唯有国王才使得的细致入微的自由打量着她。他究竟在哪一瞬忘掉了亡妻，我们无从知道；但可以肯定，此刻他心里已不再压着那段亲爱可爱的记忆。再开口时，他的声音忧郁了。

===Original===
“You love my son!”

===Chinese===
“你爱我的儿子！”

===Original===
“Who could avoid loving him?” she murmured.

===Chinese===
“谁能不爱他呢？”她喃喃道。

===Original===
“When a woman speaks to a man about the love she feels for another man she is not liked. And,” he continued, “when she speaks to a man who has no wife of his own about her love for another man then she is disliked.”

===Chinese===
“一个女人对一个男人谈起她对另一个男人的爱，她就不讨人喜欢了。而且，”他继续说道，“要是她谈的这个男人自己又没有妻子，那她就要招人嫌了。”

===Original===
“I would not be disliked by you,” Becuma murmured.

===Chinese===
“我可不愿招你嫌。”贝库玛喃喃道。

===Original===
“Nevertheless,” said he regally, “I will not come between a woman and her choice.”

===Chinese===
“话虽如此，”他以王者的气度说，“我总不至于横在一个女人和她的选择中间。”

===Original===
“I did not know you lacked a wife,” said Becuma, but indeed she did.

===Chinese===
“我原本不知道你缺一位妻子。”贝库玛说。其实她知道得清清楚楚。

===Original===
“You know it now,” the king replied sternly.

===Chinese===
“你如今知道了。”国王严厉地答道。

===Original===
“What shall I do?” she inquired, “am I to wed you or your son?”

===Chinese===
“那我该怎么办？”她问，“我是嫁你呢，还是嫁你儿子？”

===Original===
“You must choose,” Conn answered.

===Chinese===
“你必须自己选。”康恩答道。

===Original===
“If you allow me to choose it means that you do not want me very badly,” said she with a smile.

===Chinese===
“你若容我自己选，那说明你要我要得不那么急。”她微笑着说。

===Original===
“Then I will not allow you to choose,” cried the king, “and it is with myself you shall marry.”

===Chinese===
“那我就不容你选，”国王喊道，“你要嫁的，是我本人。”

===Original===
He took her hand in his and kissed it.

===Chinese===
他把她的手握在自己手里，吻了吻。

===Original===
“Lovely is this pale thin hand. Lovely is the slender foot that I see in a small bronze shoe,” said the king.

===Chinese===
“多么可爱，这只苍白纤细的手。多么可爱，这只小铜鞋里的纤秀的脚。”国王说。

===Original===
After a suitable time she continued:

===Chinese===
过了合宜的时分，她继续说道：

===Original===
“I should not like your son to be at Tara when I am there, or for a year afterwards, for I do not wish to meet him until I have forgotten him and have come to know you well.”

===Chinese===
“我在塔拉（Tara）的时候，不愿你的儿子也在；此后一年也不愿。因为我要先忘掉他，先同你相熟了，才愿意见他。”

===Original===
“I do not wish to banish my son,” the king protested.

===Chinese===
“我不想放逐我的儿子。”国王抗议道。

===Original===
“It would not really be a banishment,” she said. “A prince’s duty could be set him, and in such an absence he would improve his knowledge both of Ireland and of men. Further,” she continued with downcast eyes, “when you remember the reason that brought me here you will see that his presence would be an embarrassment to us both, and my presence would be unpleasant to him if he remembers his mother.”

===Chinese===
“那其实算不得放逐，”她说，“可以派给王子一桩职分；趁这样的外出，他既能增进对爱尔兰的见识，也能增进对人的见识。再说，”她垂下眼睛继续道，“等你想起我到这里来的缘由，你就会明白：他在场，你我两人都要难堪；他若还记得他母亲，我在场，他也要不痛快。”

===Original===
“Nevertheless,” said Conn stubbornly, “I do not wish to banish my son; it is awkward and unnecessary.”

===Chinese===
“话虽如此，”康恩执拗地说，“我还是不想放逐我的儿子；这既别扭，又没有必要。”

===Original===
“For a year only,” she pleaded.

===Chinese===
“只一年。”她恳求道。

===Original===
“It is yet,” he continued thoughtfully, “a reasonable reason that you give and I will do what you ask, but by my hand and word I don’t like doing it.”

===Chinese===
“不过，”他若有所思地接着说，“你给的理由倒也成立，我就依你所请；可是凭我的手、凭我的话，我不喜欢这么办。”

===Original===
They set out then briskly and joyfully on the homeward journey, and in due time they reached Tara of the Kings.

===Chinese===
于是两人轻快欢畅地踏上了归途，适时抵达了诸王的塔拉（Tara of the Kings）。

### IV / IV

===Original===
It is part of the education of a prince to be a good chess player, and to continually exercise his mind in view of the judgements that he will be called upon to give and the knotty, tortuous, and perplexing matters which will obscure the issues which he must judge. Art, the son of Conn, was sitting at chess with Cromdes, his father’s magician.

===Chinese===
善于下棋是王子教养的一部分，好让他不断砥砺心智，以备日后要作的种种裁决，以及那些纠缠曲折、令人困惑、会把判题遮蔽得晦暗不明的事务。康恩之子阿特，正同他父亲的法师克罗姆德斯（Cromdes）对弈。

===Original===
“Be very careful about the move you are going to make,” said Cromdes.

===Chinese===
“你将要走的那一步，千万小心。”克罗姆德斯说。

===Original===
“*Can* I be careful?” Art inquired. “Is the move that you are thinking of in my power?”

===Chinese===
“我小心得了吗？”阿特问道。“你心里想的那一步棋，是我走得动的吗？”

===Original===
“It is not,” the other admitted.

===Chinese===
“走不动。”对方承认。

===Original===
“Then I need not be more careful than usual,” Art replied, and he made his move.

===Chinese===
“那我也就不必比平日更小心了。”阿特答道，随即落了子。

===Original===
“It is a move of banishment,” said Cromdes.

===Chinese===
“这是一步放逐之着。”克罗姆德斯说。

===Original===
“As I will not banish myself, I suppose my father will do it, but I do not know why he should.”

===Chinese===
“我自己既然不会放逐自己，想必是要我父亲来放逐了；只是我不明白，他为什么非放不可。”

===Original===
“Your father will not banish you.”

===Chinese===
“你父亲不会放逐你。”

===Original===
“Who then?”

===Chinese===
“那么是谁？”

===Original===
“Your mother.”

===Chinese===
“你母亲。”

===Original===
“My mother is dead.”

===Chinese===
“我母亲死了。”

===Original===
“You have a new one,” said the magician.

===Chinese===
“你有了一位新的。”法师说。

===Original===
“Here is news,” said Art. “I think I shall not love my new mother.”

===Chinese===
“这可是新闻，”阿特说，“我看，我是不会爱我的新母亲的。”

===Original===
“You will yet love her better than she loves you,” said Cromdes, meaning thereby that they would hate each other.

===Chinese===
“将来你爱她，还要胜过她爱你呢。”克罗姆德斯说——这话的意思是，他们两个要彼此相恨。

===Original===
While they spoke the king and Becuma entered the palace.

===Chinese===
两人正说着，国王和贝库玛走进了王宫。

===Original===
“I had better go to greet my father,” said the young man.

===Chinese===
“我最好去迎一迎我父亲。”年轻人说。

===Original===
“You had better wait until he sends for you,” his companion advised, and they returned to their game.

===Chinese===
“你最好等他来传。”同伴劝道。两人便回到棋局上。

===Original===
In due time a messenger came from the king directing Art to leave Tara instantly, and to leave Ireland for one full year.

===Chinese===
到时候，国王差来一名信使，命阿特即刻离开塔拉，并且离开爱尔兰，为期整整一年。

===Original===
He left Tara that night, and for the space of a year he was not seen again in Ireland. But during that period things did not go well with the king nor with Ireland. Every year before that time three crops of corn used to be lifted off the land, but during Art’s absence there was no corn in Ireland and there was no milk. The whole land went hungry.

===Chinese===
当夜他便离开了塔拉，一年之间，爱尔兰再没有人见过他。可在那段日子里，国王不顺，爱尔兰也不顺。在那之前，这片土地年年收三茬谷物；阿特不在的期间，爱尔兰没有谷子，也没有奶。整个国土挨起饿来。

===Original===
Lean people were in every house, lean cattle in every field; the bushes did not swing out their timely berries or seasonable nuts; the bees went abroad as busily as ever, but each night they returned languidly, with empty pouches, and there was no honey in their hives when the honey season came. People began to look at each other questioningly, meaningly, and dark remarks passed between them, for they knew that a bad harvest means, somehow, a bad king, and, although this belief can be combated, it is too firmly rooted in wisdom to be dismissed.

===Chinese===
家家户户是瘦削的人，块块田野是瘦削的牲口；灌木不按时令捧出浆果，不应季节捧出坚果；蜜蜂出巢还同往常一样忙碌，每夜回来却有气无力，粉袋空空；到了产蜜的季节，蜂巢里没有蜜。人们开始面面相觑，目光里带着疑问、带着深意，阴沉沉的话在人与人之间传来传去——因为他们知道，收成不好，冥冥之中就意味着国王不好；这信念虽然可以驳一驳，可它扎根在智慧里，太深，驳不倒。

===Original===
The poets and magicians met to consider why this disaster should have befallen the country and by their arts they discovered the truth about the king’s wife, and that she was Becuma of the White Skin, and they discovered also the cause of her banishment from the Many-Coloured Land that is beyond the sea, which is beyond even the grave.

===Chinese===
诗人们和法师们聚议这场灾祸为何临头；凭着法术，他们查明了王后的真相——原来她就是白肤贝库玛——也查明了她被逐出斑斓之国的缘由。那斑斓之国在海的那一边，甚至在坟墓的那一边。

===Original===
They told the truth to the king, but he could not bear to be parted from that slender-handed, gold-haired, thin-lipped, blithe enchantress, and he required them to discover some means whereby he might retain his wife and his crown. There was a way and the magicians told him of it.

===Chinese===
他们把真相禀给了国王；可他不忍与那纤手、金发、薄唇、快快活活的女法师分离，便责令他们设法，好让他既留住王后，又留住王冠。法子是有的，法师们说了出来。

===Original===
“If the son of a sinless couple can be found and if his blood be mixed with the soil of Tara the blight and ruin will depart from Ireland,” said the magicians.

===Chinese===
“只要找得出一对无罪夫妻的儿子，把他的血掺进塔拉的泥土，灾荒与败坏就会离开爱尔兰。”法师们说。

===Original===
“If there is such a boy I will find him,” cried the Hundred Fighter.

===Chinese===
“真有这么一个孩子的话，我一定找到他。”「百战」康恩嚷道。

===Original===
At the end of a year Art returned to Tara. His father delivered to him the sceptre of Ireland, and he set out on a journey to find the son of a sinless couple such as he had been told of.

===Chinese===
一年期满，阿特回到塔拉。父亲把爱尔兰的王杖交到他手里，他便启程去寻访那样一位据说存在的无罪夫妻之子。

### V / V

===Original===
The High King did not know where exactly he should look for such a saviour, but he was well educated and knew how to look for whatever was lacking. This knowledge will be useful to those upon whom a similar duty should ever devolve.

===Chinese===
至高王不知道究竟该到哪里去找这样一位救主，但他受过良好的教育，知道怎样去寻找一切缺失的东西。这门学问，日后凡有类似职分落到头上的人，都用得着。

===Original===
He went to Ben Edair. He stepped into a coracle and pushed out to the deep, and he permitted the coracle to go as the winds and the waves directed it.

===Chinese===
他去了本·埃代尔，跨进一只柳条小舟，划向深处，然后任凭小舟随风波指引，自行漂去。

===Original===
In such a way he voyaged among the small islands of the sea until he lost all knowledge of his course and was adrift far out in ocean. He was under the guidance of the stars and the great luminaries.

===Chinese===
他就这样在海上诸小岛之间航行，直到航向全然不省，远远漂进了大洋。指引他的，是星辰，是那些巨大的天光。

===Original===
He saw black seals that stared and barked and dived dancingly, with the round turn of a bow and the forward onset of an arrow. Great whales came heaving from the green-hued void, blowing a wave of the sea high into the air from their noses and smacking their wide flat tails thunderously on the water. Porpoises went snorting past in bands and clans. Small fish came sliding and flickering, and all the outlandish creatures of the deep rose by his bobbing craft and swirled and sped away.

===Chinese===
他看见黑海豹瞪着眼、吠着、舞着潜入水中，转身圆如张弓，前冲疾似离弦之箭。巨鲸从绿色的深渊里涌出，从鼻孔把一股海水高高喷上半空，又把宽扁的尾巴雷鸣般拍在水面上。鼠海豚成帮结伙，呼哧呼哧地游过。小鱼滑着、闪着；深水里千奇百怪的生物在他颠簸的小舟旁浮起，打个旋，倏地远去。

===Original===
Wild storms howled by him so that the boat climbed painfully to the sky on a mile-high wave, balanced for a tense moment on its level top, and sped down the glassy side as a stone goes furiously from a sling.

===Chinese===
狂风暴雨从他身旁呼啸而过，小舟被一英里高的巨浪艰难地推上天去，在浪峰平顶上紧绷绷地悬停一瞬，又顺着玻璃般的浪壁疾滑而下，像一块石头从投石索里怒飞而出。

===Original===
Or, again, caught in the chop of a broken sea, it stayed shuddering and backing, while above his head there was only a low sad sky, and around him the lap and wash of grey waves that were never the same and were never different.

===Chinese===
再不然，小舟陷在碎海的乱涛里，抖着，退着，滞在原地；头顶上只有一方低沉悲哀的天空，四周是灰浪的轻拍与冲刷——那些浪从不相同，也从不两样。

===Original===
After long staring on the hungry nothingness of air and water he would stare on the skin-stretched fabric of his boat as on a strangeness, or he would examine his hands and the texture of his skin and the stiff black hairs that grew behind his knuckles and sprouted around his ring, and he found in these things newness and wonder.

===Chinese===
对着空中水上那一片饥饿的虚无凝望良久之后，他会转而盯住小舟上绷紧如兽皮的船体，像在看一件陌生之物；或是端详自己的双手、手上皮肤的纹理、指节后面生出的硬黑毛，和指环周围钻出来的须毛——他从这些东西里找到了新鲜与惊奇。

===Original===
Then, when days of storm had passed, the low grey clouds shivered and cracked in a thousand places, each grim islet went scudding to the horizon as though terrified by some great breadth, and when they had passed he stared into vast after vast of blue infinity, in the depths of which his eyes stayed and could not pierce, and wherefrom they could scarcely be withdrawn. A sun beamed thence that filled the air with sparkle and the sea with a thousand lights, and looking on these he was reminded of his home at Tara: of the columns of white and yellow bronze that blazed out sunnily on the sun, and the red and white and yellow painted roofs that beamed at and astonished the eye.

===Chinese===
后来，风暴的日子过去了，低低的灰云在千百处碎裂开来，一座座阴沉的云岛纷纷窜向天边，仿佛被什么浩大之物吓着了；云过之后，他凝望进一重又一重蔚蓝的无穷，眼睛落进那深处，穿不透，也几乎收不回来。一轮太阳从那里照临，把空中照得光影闪烁，把海上照出千点光亮；看着这些，他想起塔拉的家：想起白铜黄铜的柱子在太阳底下灿灿生光，红的白的黄的彩绘屋顶灼灼照人，看得人眼花。

===Original===
Sailing thus, lost in a succession of days and nights, of winds and calms, he came at last to an island.

===Chinese===
他就这样航行着，迷失在昼夜的接连、风与平静的接连里，终于来到一座岛前。

===Original===
His back was turned to it, and long before he saw it he smelled it and wondered; for he had been sitting as in a daze, musing on a change that had seemed to come in his changeless world; and for a long time he could not tell what that was which made a difference on the salt-whipped wind or why he should be excited. For suddenly he had become excited and his heart leaped in violent expectation.

===Chinese===
他背朝着岛，还没看见，早就闻到了，心里称奇；因为他一直坐在那里恍恍惚惚，寻思他那亘古不变的世界里仿佛降临的一场变化；有好一阵子，他说不清是什么使这带盐沫的风变了味道，也说不清自己为什么兴奋。因为他突然兴奋起来，心在猛烈的期待里直跳。

===Original===
“It is an October smell,” he said.

===Chinese===
“这是十月的气味。”他说。

===Original===
“It is apples that I smell.”

===Chinese===
“我闻到的是苹果。”

===Original===
He turned then and saw the island, fragrant with apple trees, sweet with wells of wine; and, hearkening towards the shore, his ears, dulled yet with the unending rhythms of the sea, distinguished and were filled with song; for the isle was, as it were, a nest of birds, and they sang joyously, sweetly, triumphantly.

===Chinese===
他这才转过身，看见了那座岛：苹果树芬芳馥郁，酒泉甘甜流溢；侧耳向岸上听去，他那只被大海无穷节拍磨钝了的耳朵，分辨出了歌声，随即被歌声灌满；因为那座岛俨然是一巢鸟，它们唱得欢畅，唱得甜润，唱得凯旋一般昂扬。

===Original===
He landed on that lovely island, and went forward under the darting birds, under the apple boughs, skirting fragrant lakes about which were woods of the sacred hazel and into which the nuts of knowledge fell and swam; and he blessed the gods of his people because of the ground that did not shiver and because of the deeply rooted trees that could not gad or budge.

===Chinese===
他登上那座可爱的岛，在穿梭的飞鸟底下、苹果树枝底下前行，绕着芬芳的湖走；湖畔环绕着神圣的榛树林，智慧的坚果落进湖里，随波浮沉；他为脚下不颤动的土地、为那些根深蒂固、晃不动挪不了的树木，称谢他族人的众神。

### VI / VI

===Original===
Having gone some distance by these pleasant ways he saw a shapely house dozing in the sunlight.

===Chinese===
沿着这些宜人的路径走了程路，他望见一座秀美的屋宇正在阳光里打盹。

===Original===
It was thatched with the wings of birds, blue wings and yellow and white wings, and in the centre of the house there was a door of crystal set in posts of bronze.

===Chinese===
屋顶苫着鸟的翅膀——蓝翅膀、黄翅膀、白翅膀；屋子正中有一扇水晶门，安在铜柱之间。

===Original===
The queen of this island lived there, Rigru (Large-eyed), the daughter of Lodan, and wife of Daire Degamra. She was seated on a crystal throne with her son Segda by her side, and they welcomed the High King courteously.

===Chinese===
岛上的王后就住在这里：里格鲁（Rigru），意即“大眼”，洛丹（Lodan）之女，达雷·德加姆拉（Daire Degamra）之妻。她坐在水晶宝座上，儿子塞格达（Segda）侍坐一旁；母子二人恭而有礼地迎迓至高王。

===Original===
There were no servants in this palace; nor was there need for them. The High King found that his hands had washed themselves, and when later on he noticed that food had been placed before him he noticed also that it had come without the assistance of servile hands. A cloak was laid gently about his shoulders, and he was glad of it, for his own was soiled by exposure to sun and wind and water, and was not worthy of a lady’s eye.

===Chinese===
这座宫殿里没有仆役；也用不着。至高王发现自己的双手已经自己洗净了；随后他注意到饭食已摆在面前，也注意到这饭食来时并不曾借奴仆之手。一件斗篷轻轻披上他的肩头，他为之欢喜，因为自己那件经了日晒、风吹、水浸，已经污损，配不上女士的眼睛。

===Original===
Then he was invited to eat.

===Chinese===
随后便请他用饭。

===Original===
He noticed, however, that food had been set for no one but himself, and this did not please him, for to eat alone was contrary to the hospitable usage of a king, and was contrary also to his contract with the gods.

===Chinese===
然而他注意到，饭食只为他一人而设，这叫他心中不悦，因为独自用饭，既违背国王好客的惯例，也违背他与众神的契约。

===Original===
“Good, my hosts,” he remonstrated, “it is geasa (taboo) for me to eat alone.”

===Chinese===
“好啊，我的主人们，”他进言道，“独自用饭，于我是犯盖萨（geasa，禁忌）的。”

===Original===
“But we never eat together,” the queen replied.

===Chinese===
“可我们从来不在一起用饭。”王后答道。

===Original===
“I cannot violate my geasa,” said the High King.

===Chinese===
“我不能违背我的盖萨。”至高王说。

===Original===
“I will eat with you,” said Segda (Sweet Speech), “and thus, while you are our guest you will not do violence to your vows.”

===Chinese===
“我陪您一起用饭，”塞格达（Segda，意即“甜言”）说，“这样，您做我们客人的时候，就不至于强违誓约了。”

===Original===
“Indeed,” said Conn, “that will be a great satisfaction, for I have already all the trouble that I can cope with and have no wish to add to it by offending the gods.”

===Chinese===
“说真的，”康恩说，“那真是莫大的欣慰，因为我眼下能应付的祸事已经满了，实在不想再得罪众神、添上一桩。”

===Original===
“What is your trouble?” the gentle queen asked. “During a year,” Conn replied, “there has been neither corn nor milk in Ireland. The land is parched, the trees are withered, the birds do not sing in Ireland, and the bees do not make honey.”

===Chinese===
“您有什么祸事？”温柔的王后问。“整整一年了，”康恩答道，“爱尔兰没有谷子，也没有奶。土地焦干，树木凋枯，鸟不在爱尔兰歌唱，蜜蜂不酿蜜。”

===Original===
“You are certainly in trouble,” the queen assented.

===Chinese===
“您确实在祸事里。”王后应道。

===Original===
“But,” she continued, “for what purpose have you come to our island?”

===Chinese===
“可是，”她接着说，“您到我们岛上来，为的是什么呢？”

===Original===
“I have come to ask for the loan of your son.”

===Chinese===
“我来，是想向你们借你们的儿子。”

===Original===
“A loan of my son!”

===Chinese===
“借我的儿子！”

===Original===
“I have been informed,” Conn explained, “that if the son of a sinless couple is brought to Tara and is bathed in the waters of Ireland the land will be delivered from those ills.”

===Chinese===
“有人告诉我，”康恩解释道，“只要把无罪夫妻的儿子带到塔拉，叫他在爱尔兰的水里洗一个浴，这片土地就能从那些灾殃中得救。”

===Original===
The king of this island, Daire, had not hitherto spoken, but he now did so with astonishment and emphasis.

===Chinese===
岛上的国王达雷此前一直没有开口，这时开了口，语气里又是惊愕，又是斩截。

===Original===
“We would not lend our son to anyone, not even to gain the kingship of the world,” said he.

===Chinese===
“我们的儿子，谁也不借，”他说，“哪怕借他换来全世界的王位，也不借。”

===Original===
But Segda, observing that the guest’s countenance was discomposed, broke in:

===Chinese===
塞格达见客人的面色乱了，插嘴道：

===Original===
“It is not kind to refuse a thing that the Ard-Rí of Ireland asks for, and I will go with him.”

===Chinese===
“爱尔兰的至高王（Ard-Rí）所求的事，回绝了可不厚道。我跟他去。”

===Original===
“Do not go, my pulse,” his father advised.

===Chinese===
“别去，我的心跳。”他父亲劝道。

===Original===
“Do not go, my one treasure,” his mother pleaded.

===Chinese===
“别去，我的独一珍宝。”他母亲恳求道。

===Original===
“I must go indeed,” the boy replied, “for it is to do good I am required, and no person may shirk such a requirement.”

===Chinese===
“我实在非去不可，”少年答道，“因为人家要我去做的是一件善事，这样的差事，谁也不得推辞。”

===Original===
“Go then,” said his father, “but I will place you under the protection of the High King and of the Four Provincial Kings of Ireland, and under the protection of Art, the son of Conn, and of Fionn, the son of Uail, and under the protection of the magicians and poets and the men of art in Ireland.” And he thereupon bound these protections and safeguards on the Ard-Rí with an oath.

===Chinese===
“那就去吧，”他父亲说，“但我要把你置于至高王的保护之下，置于爱尔兰四省国王的保护之下，置于康恩之子阿特、尤尔（Uail）之子芬恩（Fionn）的保护之下，还要置于爱尔兰的法师、诗人和艺人的保护之下。”说罢，他便凭一项誓言，把这些保护与保障加在了至高王身上。

===Original===
“I will answer for these protections,” said Conn.

===Chinese===
“这些保护，由我一力担保。”康恩说。

===Original===
He departed then from the island with Segda and in three days they reached Ireland, and in due time they arrived at Tara.

===Chinese===
随后他带着塞格达离岛而去，三天到了爱尔兰，适时抵达塔拉。

### VII / VII

===Original===
On reaching the palace Conn called his magicians and poets to a council and informed them that he had found the boy they sought﻿—the son of a virgin. These learned people consulted together, and they stated that the young man must be killed, and that his blood should be mixed with the earth of Tara and sprinkled under the withered trees.

===Chinese===
一到王宫，康恩便召集他的法师和诗人议事，告诉他们：他们要找的孩子，他找到了——一个处女之子。这些有学问的人合议了一番，然后声称：这少年必须杀死，他的血须掺进塔拉的泥土，洒在枯树的根下。

===Original===
When Segda heard this he was astonished and defiant; then, seeing that he was alone and without prospect of succour, he grew downcast and was in great fear for his life. But remembering the safeguards under which he had been placed, he enumerated these to the assembly, and called on the High King to grant him the protections that were his due.

===Chinese===
塞格达听了，先是惊愕，继而强硬；随后眼看自己孤身一人、无援可待，便垂下头去，为自己的性命大起恐惧。但他记起自己被置于其下的那些保障，便向大会一一陈明，吁请至高王把理应属于他的保护赐给他。

===Original===
Conn was greatly perturbed, but, as in duty bound, he placed the boy under the various protections that were in his oath, and, with the courage of one who has no more to gain or lose, he placed Segda, furthermore, under the protection of all the men of Ireland.

===Chinese===
康恩大为不安，但职责所系，他把少年置于自己誓约里的各项保护之下；随后，又以一副再无可得、再无可失者的勇气，把塞格达进一步置于全体爱尔兰人的保护之下。

===Original===
But the men of Ireland refused to accept that bond, saying that although the Ard-Rí was acting justly towards the boy he was not acting justly towards Ireland.

===Chinese===
但爱尔兰人拒绝接受这项约束。他们说：至高王待这少年固然公道，待爱尔兰却不公道。

===Original===
“We do not wish to slay this prince for our pleasure,” they argued, “but for the safety of Ireland he must be killed.”

===Chinese===
“我们并不是为取乐才要杀这位王子，”他们争辩道，“但为了爱尔兰的安危，他必须被杀。”

===Original===
Angry parties were formed. Art, and Fionn the son of Uail, and the princes of the land were outraged at the idea that one who had been placed under their protection should be hurt by any hand. But the men of Ireland and the magicians stated that the king had gone to Faery for a special purpose, and that his acts outside or contrary to that purpose were illegal, and committed no person to obedience.

===Chinese===
怒气冲冲的派别立起来了。阿特、尤尔之子芬恩和全国的诸侯义愤填膺：置于他们保护之下的人，岂容任何一只手来加害！但爱尔兰民众与法师们声称：国王赴仙境原为一件特定的差事，凡在此目的之外、或与此目的相悖的行为皆属非法，约束不了任何人。

===Original===
There were debates in the Council Hall, in the marketplace, in the streets of Tara, some holding that national honour dissolved and absolved all personal honour, and others protesting that no man had aught but his personal honour, and that above it not the gods, not even Ireland, could be placed﻿—for it is to be known that Ireland is a god.

===Chinese===
议事厅里、市场上、塔拉的大街上，处处是辩论：一派主张，国家荣誉可以消解并赦免一切个人荣誉；另一派抗议说，一个人除个人荣誉外一无所有，在它之上，众神放不得——连爱尔兰也放不得——因为须知，爱尔兰本身就是一位神。

===Original===
Such a debate was in course, and Segda, to whom both sides addressed gentle and courteous arguments, grew more and more disconsolate.

===Chinese===
这样的辩论正进行着，两派都对塞格达说着温柔客气的道理，塞格达却越来越愁苦。

===Original===
“You shall die for Ireland, dear heart,” said one of them, and he gave Segda three kisses on each cheek.

===Chinese===
“你要为爱尔兰而死，亲爱的。”其中一个说，在塞格达两颊各亲了三下。

===Original===
“Indeed,” said Segda, returning those kisses, “indeed I had not bargained to die for Ireland, but only to bathe in her waters and to remove her pestilence.”

===Chinese===
“说实话，”塞格达回着吻说，“说实话，我讲定的可不是为爱尔兰死，只是在她水里洗个浴，去掉她的疫病。”

===Original===
“But dear child and prince,” said another, kissing him likewise, “if any one of us could save Ireland by dying for her how cheerfully we would die.”

===Chinese===
“可是亲爱的孩子、亲爱的王子，”另一个也同样吻着他说，“我们中间无论哪一个，若能以死救爱尔兰，我们会死得多么欢欢喜喜。”

===Original===
And Segda, returning his three kisses, agreed that the death was noble, but that it was not in his undertaking.

===Chinese===
塞格达回了他三吻，承认这死是高贵的，只是不在他应承的差事之内。

===Original===
Then, observing the stricken countenances about him, and the faces of men and women hewn thin by hunger, his resolution melted away, and he said:

===Chinese===
随后，望着四周哀恸的面容，望着那些被饥饿削瘦的男男女女的脸，他的决心化掉了。他说：

===Original===
“I think I must die for you,” and then he said:

===Chinese===
“我想，我必须为你们去死。”随后他说：

===Original===
“I will die for you.”

===Chinese===
“我愿为你们去死。”

===Original===
And when he had said that, all the people present touched his cheek with their lips, and the love and peace of Ireland entered into his soul, so that he was tranquil and proud and happy.

===Chinese===
这话一出，在场的人都用嘴唇碰了碰他的脸颊，爱尔兰的爱与安宁进入了他的灵魂，他于是安详、骄傲而快乐。

===Original===
The executioner drew his wide, thin blade and all those present covered their eyes with their cloaks, when a wailing voice called on the executioner to delay yet a moment. The High King uncovered his eyes and saw that a woman had approached driving a cow before her.

===Chinese===
行刑人抽出那宽而薄的刀刃，在场的人纷纷用斗篷掩住眼睛；这时，一个哀号的嗓音叫行刑人再缓一缓。至高王掀开斗篷露出眼睛，见一个妇人赶着一头母牛，走上前来。

===Original===
“Why are you killing the boy?” she demanded.

===Chinese===
“你们为什么杀这孩子？”她质问。

===Original===
The reason for this slaying was explained to her.

===Chinese===
杀人的缘由向她解说了一遍。

===Original===
“Are you sure,” she asked, “that the poets and magicians really know everything?”

===Chinese===
“你们拿得准么？”她问，“诗人和法师们当真无所不知？”

===Original===
“Do they not?” the king inquired.

===Chinese===
“难道不是么？”国王反问。

===Original===
“Do they?” she insisted.

===Chinese===
“是么？”她逼问。

===Original===
And then turning to the magicians:

===Chinese===
随后她转向法师们：

===Original===
“Let one magician of the magicians tell me what is hidden in the bags that are lying across the back of my cow.”

===Chinese===
“请法师们中间的一位告诉我：横在我牛背上的那些袋子里，藏的是什么。”

===Original===
But no magician could tell it, nor did they try to.

===Chinese===
可没有一个法师说得出来，他们也不试着说。

===Original===
“Questions are not answered thus,” they said. “There is formulae, and the calling up of spirits, and lengthy complicated preparations in our art.”

===Chinese===
“问题不是这样回答的，”他们说，“我们的法术里有咒式，有召灵，还有冗长繁复的预备功夫。”

===Original===
“I am not badly learned in these arts,” said the woman, “and I say that if you slay this cow the effect will be the same as if you had killed the boy.”

===Chinese===
“这些法术，我也并非不精通，”妇人说，“我告诉你们：你们若杀了这头母牛，功效同杀了那孩子一模一样。”

===Original===
“We would prefer to kill a cow or a thousand cows rather than harm this young prince,” said Conn, “but if we spare the boy will these evils return?”

===Chinese===
“与其伤这位少年王子，我们宁愿杀一头牛、一千头牛，”康恩说，“可若饶了这孩子，这些灾祸还会回来么？”

===Original===
“They will not be banished until you have banished their cause.”

===Chinese===
“不把它们的祸根放逐出去，它们就不会被放逐。”

===Original===
“And what is their cause?”

===Chinese===
“那祸根是什么？”

===Original===
“Becuma is the cause, and she must be banished.”

===Chinese===
“贝库玛就是祸根，她必须被放逐。”

===Original===
“If you must tell me what to do,” said Conn, “tell me at least to do something that I can do.”

===Chinese===
“你们既然非告诉我怎么做不可，”康恩说，“那就至少告诉我一件我做得到的事。”

===Original===
“I will tell you certainly. You can keep Becuma and your ills as long as you want to. It does not matter to me. Come, my son,” she said to Segda, for it was Segda’s mother who had come to save him; and then that sinless queen and her son went back to their home of enchantment, leaving the king and Fionn and the magicians and nobles of Ireland astonished and ashamed.

===Chinese===
“我自然要告诉你。贝库玛和你的灾祸，你愿意留多久，就留多久；与我毫不相干。走吧，我的儿子。”她对塞格达说——来救他的，正是塞格达的母亲。随后，那位无罪的王后带着儿子回他们那座有魔法的家园去了，留下国王、芬恩和爱尔兰的法师贵人们在原地，又是惊愕，又是羞惭。

### VIII / VIII

===Original===
There are good and evil people in this and in every other world, and the person who goes hence will go to the good or the evil that is native to him, while those who return come as surely to their due. The trouble which had fallen on Becuma did not leave her repentant, and the sweet lady began to do wrong as instantly and innocently as a flower begins to grow. It was she who was responsible for the ills which had come on Ireland, and we may wonder why she brought these plagues and droughts to what was now her own country.

===Chinese===
这个世界和其余每一个世界里，都有善人与恶人；离开此世的人，会去到他天性所系的那善或那恶里；而回来的人，也必定回到自己应得的份上。落在贝库玛身上的祸事没有使她悔改，这位甜美的女士做起恶来，就像花开起来一样，即刻而起，浑然无辜。降临爱尔兰的灾殃，正是她一手造成的；我们可以纳罕，她为什么要把这一场场瘟疫和旱灾，带给她如今自己的国土。

===Original===
Under all wrongdoing lies personal vanity or the feeling that we are endowed and privileged beyond our fellows. It is probable that, however courageously she had accepted fate, Becuma had been sharply stricken in her pride; in the sense of personal strength, aloofness, and identity, in which the mind likens itself to god and will resist every domination but its own. She had been punished, that is, she had submitted to control, and her sense of freedom, of privilege, of very being, was outraged. The mind flinches even from the control of natural law, and how much more from the despotism of its own separated likenesses, for if another can control me that other has usurped me, has become me, and how terribly I seem diminished by the seeming addition!

===Chinese===
一切恶行底下，都伏着个人的虚荣，或者说，那种自觉禀赋特异、特权凌于同侪之上的感觉。贝库玛无论接受命运时多么勇敢，她的自尊多半已被狠狠刺伤——那是一种关乎个人力量、超然与自体的感觉，心智在其中自比于神，除了自己的统辖，对什么支配都要抗拒。她受了惩罚，也就是说，她屈服于了支配；于是她的自由感、特权感、乃至存在感，都遭到了粗暴的凌辱。心智连自然法则的支配都要畏缩，何况屈服于与自己同模同样者的专制——因为旁人若能支配我，那旁人便已篡夺了我，已变成了我；这表面上的添附，把我削减得何等可怕！

===Original===
This sense of separateness is vanity, and is the bed of all wrongdoing. For we are not freedom, we are control, and we must submit to our own function ere we can exercise it. Even unconsciously we accept the rights of others to all that we have, and if we will not share our good with them, it is because we cannot, having none; but we will yet give what we have, although that be evil. To insist on other people sharing in our personal torment is the first step towards insisting that they shall share in our joy, as we shall insist when we get it.

===Chinese===
这种自外于人的感觉便是虚荣，是一切恶行的温床。因为我们并不是自由，我们是统辖；必须先服从自身的职能，然后才能行使它。哪怕是在无意之中，我们也承认旁人对我们一切所有的权利；我们若不肯把好处分给他们，那是因为我们自己没有好处，分不得；可我们仍要把手里有的分出去，哪怕那是恶。非要旁人来分担我们个人的苦楚，这是第一步；等我们得了欢乐，我们也会同样坚持要他们分担——到那一天，我们必定这样坚持。

===Original===
Becuma considered that if she must suffer all else she met should suffer also. She raged, therefore, against Ireland, and in particular she raged against young Art, her husband’s son, and she left undone nothing that could afflict Ireland or the prince. She may have felt that she could not make them suffer, and that is a maddening thought to any woman. Or perhaps she had really desired the son instead of the father, and her thwarted desire had perpetuated itself as hate. But it is true that Art regarded his mother’s successor with intense dislike, and it is true that she actively returned it.

===Chinese===
贝库玛认定：自己既然必须受苦，那她遇见的一切也必须一同受苦。于是她迁怒于爱尔兰，尤其迁怒于她丈夫的儿子——年轻的阿特；凡是能给爱尔兰或这位王子添堵的事，她一件也没有留下不做。她也许自觉叫不动他们吃苦，这个念头，对任何女人都是逼人发疯的念头。又或许，她当初真心想要的原是儿子而不是父亲，那受挫的欲望便化作了恨，绵绵不休。但有一点是真的：阿特以极度的憎恶看待他母亲的继任者；还有一点也是真的：她积极地以憎恶回报。

===Original===
One day Becuma came on the lawn before the palace, and seeing that Art was at chess with Cromdes she walked to the table on which the match was being played and for some time regarded the game. But the young prince did not take any notice of her while she stood by the board, for he knew that this girl was the enemy of Ireland, and he could not bring himself even to look at her.

===Chinese===
一天，贝库玛来到宫前的草坪上，见阿特正与克罗姆德斯下棋，便走到对局的桌旁，观了一阵棋。可年轻的王子在她立于棋盘旁的整段时间里，一点也没有理会她，因为他知道这女子是爱尔兰的仇敌，他连朝她看一眼都不愿意。

===Original===
Becuma, looking down on his beautiful head, smiled as much in rage as in disdain.

===Chinese===
贝库玛俯视着他俊美的头，笑了；那笑里，怒与轻蔑各占一半。

===Original===
“O son of a king,” said she, “I demand a game with you for stakes.”

===Chinese===
“王的儿子啊，”她说，“我要求同你赌一局。”

===Original===
Art then raised his head and stood up courteously, but he did not look at her.

===Chinese===
阿特这才抬起头，客气地站起身来，却不朝她看。

===Original===
“Whatever the queen demands I will do,” said he.

===Chinese===
“王后要求的，我都照办。”他说。

===Original===
“Am I not your mother also?” she replied mockingly, as she took the seat which the chief magician leaped from.

===Chinese===
“我不也是你的母亲么？”她讥诮地答道，一面在首席法师跳起来让出的座位上坐了。

===Original===
The game was set then, and her play was so skilful that Art was hard put to counter her moves. But at a point of the game Becuma grew thoughtful, and, as by a lapse of memory, she made a move which gave the victory to her opponent. But she had intended that. She sat then, biting on her lip with her white small teeth and staring angrily at Art.

===Chinese===
棋局摆开，她棋路精妙，阿特穷于招架。但下到一处，贝库玛忽然若有所思，像是记忆失灵了一瞬，走了一步把胜利让给对手的棋。可她要的正是这个。随后她坐在那里，用细小洁白的牙齿咬着嘴唇，怒冲冲地瞪着阿特。

===Original===
“What do you demand from me?” she asked.

===Chinese===
“你向我要求什么？”她问。

===Original===
“I bind you to eat no food in Ireland until you find the wand of Curoi, son of Darè.”

===Chinese===
“我约束你：在找到达雷之子库罗伊（Curoi）的魔杖之前，不得在爱尔兰进食。”

===Original===
Becuma then put a cloak about her and she went from Tara northward and eastward until she came to the dewy, sparkling Brugh of Angus mac an Og in Ulster, but she was not admitted there. She went thence to the Shí ruled over by Eogabal, and although this lord would not admit her, his daughter Ainè, who was her foster-sister, let her into Faery. She made inquiries and was informed where the dun of Curoi mac Darè was, and when she had received this intelligence she set out for Sliev Mis. By what arts she coaxed Curoi to give up his wand it matters not, enough that she was able to return in triumph to Tara. When she handed the wand to Art, she said:

===Chinese===
于是贝库玛披上斗篷，离开塔拉，向北又向东而去，来到阿尔斯特那座露珠晶莹、光彩闪烁的安格斯·麦克·安·奥格（Angus mac an Og）的布鲁格，却没有被放进去。她又去埃戈瓦尔（Eogabal）统治的仙丘；这位君主虽不肯放她进去，他的女儿艾妮（Ainè）——她的义姊妹——却引她进了仙境。她四处打探，得到了库罗伊·麦克·达雷（Curoi mac Darè）堡寨所在的音信；得了音信，她便动身前往斯利夫·米斯（Sliev Mis）。她用什么法术哄得库罗伊交出魔杖，无关紧要；要紧的是，她终于能够凯旋塔拉。她把魔杖递给阿特时，说：

===Original===
“I claim my game of revenge.”

===Chinese===
“我讨我那一局复仇的棋。”

===Original===
“It is due to you,” said Art, and they sat on the lawn before the palace and played.

===Chinese===
“这是你应得的。”阿特说。两人在宫前的草坪上坐下，对弈起来。

===Original===
A hard game that was, and at times each of the combatants sat for an hour staring on the board before the next move was made, and at times they looked from the board and for hours stared on the sky seeking as though in heaven for advice. But Becuma’s foster-sister, Ainè, came from the Shí, and, unseen by any, she interfered with Art’s play, so that, suddenly, when he looked again on the board, his face went pale, for he saw that the game was lost.

===Chinese===
那是一场苦战。有时，两个对局者在走出下一步之前，枯坐一个钟头盯着棋盘；有时他们又从棋盘上抬起眼来，一连几个钟头望着天空，仿佛要到上天那里讨个主意。可贝库玛的义姊妹艾妮从仙丘来了；谁也不曾看见，她暗中拨乱了阿特的棋——于是，猛然间，阿特再看棋盘时，脸就白了：棋局已经输了。

===Original===
“I didn’t move that piece,” said he sternly.

===Chinese===
“那枚子不是我走的。”他严厉地说。

===Original===
“Nor did I,” Becuma replied, and she called on the onlookers to confirm that statement.

===Chinese===
“也不是我。”贝库玛答道，还唤围观的人来证明这话。

===Original===
She was smiling to herself secretly, for she had seen what the mortal eyes around could not see.

===Chinese===
她独自暗暗地微笑，因为四周凡人的眼睛看不见的东西，她看见了。

===Original===
“I think the game is mine,” she insisted softly.

===Chinese===
“我想，这局棋是我的了。”她轻轻地咬定。

===Original===
“I think that your friends in Faery have cheated,” he replied, “but the game is yours if you are content to win it that way.”

===Chinese===
“我看，是你在仙境里的朋友们作了弊，”他答道，“不过，你若心安于这样赢，棋便归你。”

===Original===
“I bind you,” said Becuma, “to eat no food in Ireland until you have found Delvcaem, the daughter of Morgan.”

===Chinese===
“我约束你，”贝库玛说，“在找到摩根之女德尔夫卡姆之前，不得在爱尔兰进食。”

===Original===
“Where do I look for her?” said Art in despair.

===Chinese===
“我到哪里去找她？”阿特绝望地说。

===Original===
“She is in one of the islands of the sea,” Becuma replied, “that is all I will tell you,” and she looked at him maliciously, joyously, contentedly, for she thought he would never return from that journey, and that Morgan would see to it.

===Chinese===
“她在海上的一座岛上，”贝库玛答道，“我只会告诉你这么多。”她望着他，恶毒地、欢畅地、心满意足地望着他，因为她想，他这一去必回不来——摩根自会料理停当。

### IX / IX

===Original===
Art, as his father had done before him, set out for the Many-Coloured Land, but it was from Inver Colpa he embarked and not from Ben Edair.

===Chinese===
阿特像他父亲当年一样，动身前往斑斓之国；只是他登船的地点是因弗·科尔帕（Inver Colpa），不是本·埃代尔。

===Original===
At a certain time he passed from the rough green ridges of the sea to enchanted waters, and he roamed from island to island asking all people how he might come to Delvcaem, the daughter of Morgan. But he got no news from anyone, until he reached an island that was fragrant with wild apples, gay with flowers, and joyous with the song of birds and the deep mellow drumming of the bees. In this island he was met by a lady, Credè, the Truly Beautiful, and when they had exchanged kisses, he told her who he was and on what errand he was bent.

===Chinese===
行到某个时刻，他越过了海上起伏粗砺的绿色长脊，进入有魔法的海域，从此岛游到彼岛，逢人便打听怎样才能到摩根之女德尔夫卡姆那里去。可是谁也没有消息给他，直到他抵达一座岛——岛上野苹果芬芳，繁花明媚，鸟儿的歌声和蜜蜂低沉醇厚的鼓鸣一片欢腾。在这座岛上，一位女士迎住了他：克蕾德（Credè），那位“真美”。互吻见礼之后，他告诉她自己是谁，怀着怎样的差事。

===Original===
“We have been expecting you,” said Credè, “but alas, poor soul, it is a hard, and a long, bad way that you must go; for there is sea and land, danger and difficulty between you and the daughter of Morgan.”

===Chinese===
“我们一直在盼你来，”克蕾德说，“可是，唉，可怜的人，你要走的，是一条又苦、又长、又坏的路；因为你和摩根的女儿之间，隔着海，隔着陆地，隔着危险与艰难。”

===Original===
“Yet I must go there,” he answered.

===Chinese===
“可我非去不可。”他答道。

===Original===
“There is a wild dark ocean to be crossed. There is a dense wood where every thorn on every tree is sharp as a spear-point and is curved and clutching. There is a deep gulf to be gone through,” she said, “a place of silence and terror, full of dumb, venomous monsters. There is an immense oak forest﻿—dark, dense, thorny, a place to be strayed in, a place to be utterly bewildered and lost in. There is a vast dark wilderness, and therein is a dark house, lonely and full of echoes, and in it there are seven gloomy hags, who are warned already of your coming and are waiting to plunge you in a bath of molten lead.”

===Chinese===
“要先渡一片狂野的黑海洋。要穿一片密林，那里每棵树上的每根刺都尖利如矛头，弯着，勾着。还要通过一道深渊，”她说，“那是一片沉寂与恐怖之地，满是哑口的、剧毒的怪物。还有一片浩大的橡树林——黑、密、多刺，是叫人走岔的路，是叫人彻底昏头、彻底迷失的地方。还有一片广袤的黑暗荒野；荒野里有一座黑屋子，孤零零的，满是回声；屋里坐着七个阴郁的妖婆，她们已经得了你要来的风声，正等着把你按进一浴盆熔化的铅里。”

===Original===
“It is not a choice journey,” said Art, “but I have no choice and must go.”

===Chinese===
“这可不是一条可挑拣的旅程，”阿特说，“可我无可挑拣，必须去。”

===Original===
“Should you pass those hags,” she continued, “and no one has yet passed them, you must meet Ailill of the Black Teeth, the son of Mongan Tender Blossom, and who could pass that gigantic and terrible fighter?”

===Chinese===
“你若过得那七个妖婆——至今还没有人过去过——你还得会一会黑牙阿伊利尔（Ailill of the Black Teeth），蒙根·嫩花（Mongan Tender Blossom）之子；那样一位魁伟可怕的斗士，谁过得去？”

===Original===
“It is not easy to find the daughter of Morgan,” said Art in a melancholy voice.

===Chinese===
“要找到摩根的女儿，不容易啊。”阿特声音忧郁地说。

===Original===
“It is not easy,” Credè replied eagerly, “and if you will take my advice﻿—”

===Chinese===
“是不容易，”克蕾德急切地应道，“你要是肯听我的劝——”

===Original===
“Advise me,” he broke in, “for in truth there is no man standing in such need of counsel as I do.”

===Chinese===
“你劝吧，”他插嘴道，“说实在的，天下再没有比我更需要指教的人了。”

===Original===
“I would advise you,” said Credè in a low voice, “to seek no more for the sweet daughter of Morgan, but to stay in this place where all that is lovely is at your service.”

===Chinese===
“我劝你，”克蕾德低声说，“别再去寻摩根那可爱的女儿了，就留在这个地方——这里一切可爱之物，都听你差遣。”

===Original===
“But, but﻿—” cried Art in astonishment.

===Chinese===
“可是，可是——”阿特惊呼道。

===Original===
“Am I not as sweet as the daughter of Morgan?” she demanded, and she stood before him queenly and pleadingly, and her eyes took his with imperious tenderness.

===Chinese===
“难道我不和摩根的女儿一样可爱么？”她质问。她站在他面前，仪态是王后，语气是恳求；她的眼睛摄住了他的眼睛，温柔里透着专横。

===Original===
“By my hand,” he answered, “you are sweeter and lovelier than any being under the sun, but﻿—”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”他答道，“你比普天之下的任何一个生命都更可爱、更美丽，可是——”

===Original===
“And with me,” she said, “you will forget Ireland.”

===Chinese===
“跟我在一起，”她说，“你会忘掉爱尔兰。”

===Original===
“I am under bonds,” cried Art, “I have passed my word, and I would not forget Ireland or cut myself from it for all the kingdoms of the Many-Coloured Land.”

===Chinese===
“我负着誓约，”阿特喊道，“我已经许下了话；就是把斑斓之国所有的王国都给我，我也不肯忘掉爱尔兰，不肯同它一刀两断。”

===Original===
Credè urged no more at that time, but as they were parting she whispered, “There are two girls, sisters of my own, in Morgan’s palace. They will come to you with a cup in either hand; one cup will be filled with wine and one with poison. Drink from the right-hand cup, O my dear.”

===Chinese===
这一次，克蕾德没有再劝；可两人分手之际，她低声道：“摩根的宫里有两个女孩，是我自己的姊妹。她们会来见你，两手各端一杯：一杯斟的是酒，一杯盛的是毒。要喝右手那一杯，哦，我亲爱的。”

===Original===
Art stepped into his coracle, and then, wringing her hands, she made yet an attempt to dissuade him from that drear journey.

===Chinese===
阿特跨进他的柳条小舟；她绞着双手，又做了一次努力，劝他别去走那凄凉的旅程。

===Original===
“Do not leave me,” she urged. “Do not affront these dangers. Around the palace of Morgan there is a palisade of copper spikes, and on the top of each spike the head of a man grins and shrivels. There is one spike only which bears no head, and it is for your head that spike is waiting. Do not go there, my love.”

===Chinese===
“别丢下我，”她恳求道，“别去招惹这些凶险。摩根的宫殿四周，围着一圈铜尖桩；每根桩顶上都有一颗人头，龇着牙，干瘪着。只有一根桩上没有头——那根桩等的就是你的头。别去那儿，我的爱人。”

===Original===
“I must go indeed,” said Art earnestly.

===Chinese===
“我实在非去不可。”阿特恳切地说。

===Original===
“There is yet a danger,” she called. “Beware of Delvcaem’s mother, Dog Head, daughter of the King of the Dog Heads. Beware of her.”

===Chinese===
“还有一重凶险呢，”她喊道，“当心德尔夫卡姆的母亲，狗首（Dog Head），狗首人之王的女儿。当心她。”

===Original===
“Indeed,” said Art to himself, “there is so much to beware of that I will beware of nothing. I will go about my business,” he said to the waves, “and I will let those beings and monsters and the people of the Dog Heads go about their business.”

===Chinese===
“说真的，”阿特自言自语道，“要当心的东西这么多，我索性什么都不当心了。我走我的路，”他对海浪说，“让那些怪物啊、妖孽啊、狗首人啊，也走它们的路去。”

### X / X

===Original===
He went forward in his light bark, and at some moment found that he had parted from those seas and was adrift on vaster and more turbulent billows. From those dark-green surges there gaped at him monstrous and cavernous jaws; and round, wicked, red-rimmed, bulging eyes stared fixedly at the boat. A ridge of inky water rushed foaming mountainously on his board, and behind that ridge came a vast warty head that gurgled and groaned. But at these vile creatures he thrust with his lengthy spear or stabbed at closer reach with a dagger.

===Chinese===
他驾着轻快的小船前进；不知哪一刻，发觉自己已别了那片海域，漂在更浩大、更狂乱的长浪上。那些墨绿的巨涌里，一张张怪嘴如窟穴般向他洞开；一双双滚圆、凶恶、红眶暴凸的眼睛死死盯着小船。一道墨黑的水脊挟着山一样的白沫，朝他的船板冲来；水脊后面，浮出一颗巨大的瘤头，咕噜着，呻吟着。对这一个个恶物，他挺起长矛搠去，或欺近身去，用匕首刺去。

===Original===
He was not spared one of the terrors which had been foretold. Thus, in the dark thick oak forest he slew the seven hags and buried them in the molten lead which they had heated for him. He climbed an icy mountain, the cold breath of which seemed to slip into his body and chip off inside of his bones, and there, until he mastered the sort of climbing on ice, for each step that he took upwards he slipped back ten steps. Almost his heart gave way before he learned to climb that venomous hill. In a forked glen into which he slipped at nightfall he was surrounded by giant toads, who spat poison, and were icy as the land they lived in, and were cold and foul and savage. At Sliav Saev he encountered the long-maned lions who lie in wait for the beasts of the world, growling woefully as they squat above their prey and crunch those terrified bones. He came on Ailill of the Black Teeth sitting on the bridge that spanned a torrent, and the grim giant was grinding his teeth on a pillar stone. Art drew nigh unobserved and brought him low.

===Chinese===
预言过的凶险，一样也没有饶过他。就这样，在黑压压的密橡树林里，他杀掉了那七个妖婆，把她们埋进她们为他烧热的熔铅里。他攀上一座冰山，山的寒气仿佛钻进他的身体，在他的骨头里一片片剥落；在那里，直到他练会了那种攀冰的功夫之前，他每向上迈一步，就要向后滑十步。不等学会攀那座毒山，他的心几乎先垮了。黄昏时分，他滑进一道岔谷，被巨蟾围住——那些蟾蜍喷着毒，同它们栖身的土地一样冰冷，又冷，又脏，又凶。在斯利夫·塞夫（Sliav Saev），他遇上了那些长鬃的狮子——它们专候世间的走兽，蹲在猎物上方呜呜哀吼，嚼着那些吓坏了的骨头。他又撞见黑牙阿伊利尔坐在横跨激流的桥上，那阴沉的巨人正拿一块石柱磨牙。阿特悄没声儿地掩近，把他放倒了。

===Original===
It was not for nothing that these difficulties and dangers were in his path. These things and creatures were the invention of Dog Head, the wife of Morgan, for it had become known to her that she would die on the day her daughter was wooed. Therefore none of the dangers encountered by Art were real, but were magical chimeras conjured against him by the great witch.

===Chinese===
这些艰难凶险横在他路上，并非无缘无故。这些事物和妖物，都是摩根之妻狗首的造物；因为她已经得知：她女儿被人求娶之日，便是她死之日。所以，阿特一路遭遇的凶险没有一样是真的，全是大女巫对他施法变出的幻象。

===Original===
Affronting all, conquering all, he came in time to Morgan’s dun, a place so lovely that after the miseries through which he had struggled he almost wept to see beauty again.

===Chinese===
他一一迎上，一一征服，适时来到了摩根的堡寨——那地方美得叫他在挣扎过那一重重苦难之后，再见美丽，几乎落下泪来。

===Original===
Delvcaem knew that he was coming. She was waiting for him, yearning for him. To her mind Art was not only love, he was freedom, for the poor girl was a captive in her father’s home. A great pillar an hundred feet high had been built on the roof of Morgan’s palace, and on the top of this pillar a tiny room had been constructed, and in this room Delvcaem was a prisoner.

===Chinese===
德尔夫卡姆知道他要来。她等着他，渴念着他。在她心里，阿特不只是爱情，更是自由，因为这可怜的姑娘是父亲家中的囚徒。摩根宫殿的屋顶上，立着一根一百英尺高的大柱，柱顶造了一间小小的屋子，德尔夫卡姆就被囚在那间屋里。

===Original===
She was lovelier in shape than any other princess of the Many-Coloured Land. She was wiser than all the other women of that land, and she was skilful in music, embroidery, and chastity, and in all else that pertained to the knowledge of a queen.

===Chinese===
论身姿，她比斑斓之国的任何一位公主都可爱。她比那片国土上所有的女子都聪慧；她精于音乐、刺绣与贞德，也精于凡属王后学识的其他一切。

===Original===
Although Delvcaem’s mother wished nothing but ill to Art, she yet treated him with the courtesy proper in a queen on the one hand and fitting towards the son of the King of Ireland on the other. Therefore, when Art entered the palace he was met and kissed, and he was bathed and clothed and fed. Two young girls came to him then, having a cup in each of their hands, and presented him with the kingly drink, but, remembering the warning which Credè had given him, he drank only from the right-hand cup and escaped the poison.

===Chinese===
德尔夫卡姆的母亲虽对阿特别无异心、只有恶意，待他却仍合着两重礼数：一重是王后当有的礼数，一重是对爱尔兰国王之子相宜的礼数。于是，阿特一进宫，便有人迎迓亲吻；有人为他沐浴、更衣、进餐。随后两个少女来到他面前，手中各端一杯，向他奉上君王的饮品；阿特记着克蕾德的警告，只饮了右手那一杯，避开了毒。

===Original===
Next he was visited by Delvcaem’s mother, Dog Head, daughter of the King of the Dog Heads, and Morgan’s queen. She was dressed in full armour, and she challenged Art to fight with her.

===Chinese===
接着，德尔夫卡姆的母亲来见他——狗首，狗首人之王的女儿，摩根的王后。她全身披挂，向阿特索战。

===Original===
It was a woeful combat, for there was no craft or sagacity unknown to her, and Art would infallibly have perished by her hand but that her days were numbered, her star was out, and her time had come. It was her head that rolled on the ground when the combat was over, and it was her head that grinned and shrivelled on the vacant spike which she had reserved for Art’s.

===Chinese===
那是一场惨烈的搏斗：天下没有一种武艺或机变是她所不晓的；若不是她的寿数已定、她的星已熄、她的时辰已到，阿特必定丧命于她之手。搏斗结束时，滚落在地的是她的头；在那根她专为阿特留着的空桩上龇牙干瘪的，也是她的头。

===Original===
Then Art liberated Delvcaem from her prison at the top of the pillar and they were affianced together. But the ceremony had scarcely been completed when the tread of a single man caused the palace to quake and seemed to jar the world.

===Chinese===
随后，阿特把德尔夫卡姆从柱顶的囚牢里放了出来，两人订了婚约。可典礼才完，一个人的脚步便震得宫殿摇撼，仿佛连整个世界都为之震颤。

===Original===
It was Morgan returning to the palace.

===Chinese===
是摩根回宫来了。

===Original===
The gloomy king challenged him to combat also, and in his honour Art put on the battle harness which he had brought from Ireland. He wore a breastplate and helmet of gold, a mantle of blue satin swung from his shoulders, his left hand was thrust into the grips of a purple shield, deeply bossed with silver, and in the other hand he held the wide-grooved, blue hilted sword which had rung so often into fights and combats, and joyous feats and exercises.

===Chinese===
阴沉的国王也向他索战。出于对他的敬意，阿特穿上从爱尔兰带来的战装。他头戴金盔，胸披金甲，一领蓝缎披风从肩头飘摆；左手扣进一面紫色盾牌的握手——盾上镶着深深的银凸饰；另一只手握着那把宽槽蓝柄的剑——这剑曾多少次铿然鸣响，投入厮杀与格斗、欢腾的比武与演武。

===Original===
Up to this time the trials through which he had passed had seemed so great that they could not easily be added to. But if all those trials had been gathered into one vast calamity they would not equal one half of the rage and catastrophe of his war with Morgan.

===Chinese===
到这时为止，他历过的种种考验似乎已大得难以再加。可即便把那些考验统统归拢成一场浩劫，也抵不上他与摩根之战的狂怒与灾变的一半。

===Original===
For what he could not effect by arms Morgan would endeavour by guile, so that while Art drove at him or parried a crafty blow, the shape of Morgan changed before his eyes, and the monstrous king was having at him in another form, and from a new direction.

===Chinese===
因为摩根凭武力取不到的，便施诡计来取；于是，当阿特向他突刺、或格开他刁钻一击的当口，摩根的身形就在他眼前变了，那巨怪之王又换一副模样，从新的方向朝他扑来。

===Original===
It was well for the son of the Ard-Rí that he had been beloved by the poets and magicians of his land, and that they had taught him all that was known of shape-changing and words of power.

===Chinese===
至高王之子幸好一向为本国的诗人与法师们所钟爱，他们把已知的变形之术和言灵之力，尽数授给了他。

===Original===
He had need of all these.

===Chinese===
他用得着这一切。

===Original===
At times, for the weapon must change with the enemy, they fought with their foreheads as two giant stags, and the crash of their monstrous onslaught rolled and lingered on the air long after their skulls had parted. Then as two lions, long-clawed, deep-mouthed, snarling, with rigid mane, with red-eyed glare, with flashing, sharp-white fangs, they prowled lithely about each other seeking for an opening. And then as two green-ridged, white-topped, broad-swung, overwhelming, vehement billows of the deep, they met and crashed and sunk into and rolled away from each other; and the noise of these two waves was as the roar of all ocean when the howl of the tempest is drowned in the league-long fury of the surge.

===Chinese===
时而——因为兵器须随敌手而变——他们顶着额头相斗，像两头巨鹿，那可怖冲撞的轰鸣，在他们头骨分开之后许久，还在空中滚动、萦回。时而又像两头狮子——长爪深喉，龇牙低吼，鬃毛倒竖，红眼灼灼，白森森的利齿寒光闪烁——彼此轻捷地绕行，寻着破绽。时而又像深海里两道绿脊白顶、横扫而来、势不可当、烈烈轰轰的巨浪，相迎，相撞，相吞，相离；这两道浪的声响，犹如全部大洋的怒号——暴风的嚎叫，淹没在绵延一里格的怒涛狂澜之中。

===Original===
But when the wife’s time has come the husband is doomed. He is required elsewhere by his beloved, and Morgan went to rejoin his queen in the world that comes after the Many-Coloured Land, and his victor shore that knowledgeable head away from its giant shoulders.

===Chinese===
可妻子的时辰一到，丈夫的劫数便注定了。他的爱人在别处唤他；摩根便往斑斓之国之后的那重世界里，去与他的王后重聚；得胜者一刀削下那颗无所不晓的头，让它离了那副巨人的肩膀。

===Original===
He did not tarry in the Many-Coloured Land, for he had nothing further to seek there. He gathered the things which pleased him best from among the treasures of its grisly king, and with Delvcaem by his side they stepped into the coracle.

===Chinese===
他没有在斑斓之国逗留，因为那里再没有他可寻的东西了。他从那位狰狞国王的财宝里，拣了些最合心意之物；德尔夫卡姆在他身旁，两人跨进了柳条小舟。

===Original===
Then, setting their minds on Ireland, they went there as it were in a flash.

===Chinese===
随后，两人把心念定在爱尔兰上，一闪之间，便到了那里。

===Original===
The waves of all the world seemed to whirl past them in one huge, green cataract. The sound of all these oceans boomed in their ears for one eternal instant. Nothing was for that moment but a vast roar and pour of waters. Thence they swung into a silence equally vast, and so sudden that it was as thunderous in the comparison as was the elemental rage they quitted. For a time they sat panting, staring at each other, holding each other, lest not only their lives but their very souls should be swirled away in the gusty passage of world within world; and then, looking abroad, they saw the small bright waves creaming by the rocks of Ben Edair, and they blessed the power that had guided and protected them, and they blessed the comely land of Ir.

===Chinese===
普天之下的波浪，仿佛汇成一道浩大的绿色飞瀑，从他们身边呼啸卷过。所有大洋的轰响，在他们耳中鸣响了一个永恒的瞬间。那一刻，除了一片汪洋的咆哮与倾泻，再无别物。随后，他们荡进一片同样浩大的寂静；那寂静来得如此突兀，两相对照之下，竟同他们刚刚离开的天地之怒一样震耳欲聋。有一阵子，他们坐在那里喘息，彼此凝望，彼此相拥，生怕不止性命、连灵魂也要被这世界套世界的狂风通道卷走；随后放眼望去，只见细小明亮的浪花在本·埃代尔的礁石边泛着白沫，他们便称谢那指引他们、庇佑他们的大能，也称谢“爱尔”（Ir）这片秀美的国土。

===Original===
On reaching Tara, Delvcaem, who was more powerful in art and magic than Becuma, ordered the latter to go away, and she did so.

===Chinese===
一到塔拉，德尔夫卡姆——她的法术神通比贝库玛更为高强——便命贝库玛离开，贝库玛便走了。

===Original===
She left the king’s side. She came from the midst of the counsellors and magicians. She did not bid farewell to anyone. She did not say goodbye to the king as she set out for Ben Edair.

===Chinese===
她离开了国王身边。她从谋臣和法师们的中间走出来。她没有向任何人道别。她动身往本·埃代尔去的时候，也没有对国王说一声再见。

===Original===
Where she could go to no man knew, for she had been banished from the Many-Coloured Land and could not return there. She was forbidden entry to the Shí by Angus Og, and she could not remain in Ireland. She went to Sasana and she became a queen in that country, and it was she who fostered the rage against the Holy Land which has not ceased to this day.

===Chinese===
她还能到哪里去，没有人知道：她已被逐出斑斓之国，回不去了；安格斯·奥格禁止她进入仙丘；爱尔兰，她又留不得。她去了萨萨纳（Sasana），在那个国家做起了王后；那场针对圣地的仇恨，至今不曾止息——当初滋养它的，正是她。

## Dedication / 献词

===Original===
To 

 **Seumas and Iris** 

 and to 

 **Helen Fraser**

===Chinese===
献给

**Seumas and Iris**

并献给

**海伦·弗雷泽**（Helen Fraser）

## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===
1. Pronounce Fewn to rhyme with “tune.” ↩︎
2. Pronounced Usheen. ↩︎
3. This version of the death of Uail is not correct. Also Cnocha is not in Lochlann but in Ireland. ↩︎
4. The Hill of Howth. ↩︎

===Chinese===
1. 芬恩（Fionn）读作 Fewn，与 tune 押韵。↩︎
2. 奥辛（Oisín）读作 Usheen。↩︎
3. 这一版本所载尤尔（Uail）之死并不确实。另外，克诺卡（Cnocha）并不在洛克兰（Lochlann），而是在爱尔兰。↩︎
4. 即豪斯山（the Hill of Howth）。↩︎

## Mongan’s Frenzy / 蒙根的狂怒

### I / I

===Original===
The abbot of the Monastery of Moville sent word to the storytellers of Ireland that when they were in his neighbourhood they should call at the monastery, for he wished to collect and write down the stories which were in danger of being forgotten.

===Chinese===
莫维尔修道院（Monastery of Moville）的院长传话给爱尔兰各地的说书人：凡行经修道院左近的，务必进院一叙，因为他想把那些濒临失传的故事搜集起来，笔录成书。

===Original===
“These things also must be told,” said he.

===Chinese===
“这些事也必须有人讲述。”他说。

===Original===
In particular he wished to gather tales which told of the deeds that had been done before the Gospel came to Ireland.

===Chinese===
他尤其想搜集那类故事，讲的是福音传到爱尔兰之前所行的种种事迹。

===Original===
“For,” said he, “there are very good tales among those ones, and it would be a pity if the people who come after us should be ignorant of what happened long ago, and of the deeds of their fathers.”

===Chinese===
“因为，”他说，“那些故事里有极好的篇章；倘若后人竟对远古发生过什么一无所知，对先辈的功业一无所知，那岂不可惜。”

===Original===
So, whenever a storyteller chanced in that neighbourhood he was directed to the monastery, and there he received a welcome and his fill of all that is good for man.

===Chinese===
于是，只要有说书人碰巧行经那一带，人们便给他指点修道院的路。到了那里，他必受款待，凡于人有益的美善之物，尽他受用。

===Original===
The abbot’s manuscript boxes began to fill up, and he used to regard that growing store with pride and joy. In the evenings, when the days grew short and the light went early, he would call for some one of these manuscripts and have it read to him by candlelight, in order that he might satisfy himself that it was as good as he had judged it to be on the previous hearing.

===Chinese===
院长的手稿箱渐渐满了起来，他望着那与日俱增的珍藏，又得意，又欢喜。每到日短天黑的傍晚，他便命人取来其中一卷，就着烛光读给他听，好让自己确信：这故事确如他上一回听到时判定的那样好。

===Original===
One day a storyteller came to the monastery, and, like all the others, he was heartily welcomed and given a great deal more than his need.

===Chinese===
一天，有个说书人来到修道院。他与所有来客一样受到热诚欢迎，得到的款待远超所需。

===Original===
He said that his name was Cairidè, and that he had a story to tell which could not be bettered among the stories of Ireland.

===Chinese===
他说自己名叫凯里德（Cairidè），还说随身带着一个故事，在爱尔兰所有的故事里，再没有比它更好的。

===Original===
The abbot’s eyes glistened when he heard that. He rubbed his hands together and smiled on his guest.

===Chinese===
院长一听，眼睛顿时亮了。他搓着双手，朝客人堆起满脸笑。

===Original===
“What is the name of your story?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“你的故事叫什么名字？”他问。

===Original===
“It is called ‘Mongan’s Frenzy.’ ”

===Chinese===
“它叫作《蒙根的狂怒》。”

===Original===
“I never heard of it before,” cried the abbot joyfully.

===Chinese===
“我从来没有听说过它。”院长欢喜得喊了起来。

===Original===
“I am the only man that knows it,” Cairidè replied.

===Chinese===
“如今世上只有我一个人知道它。”凯里德答道。

===Original===
“But how does that come about?” the abbot inquired.

===Chinese===
“这又是怎么一回事？”院长问。

===Original===
“Because it belongs to my family,” the storyteller answered. “There was a Cairidè of my nation with Mongan when he went into Faery. This Cairidè listened to the story when it was first told. Then he told it to his son, and his son told it to his son, and that son’s great-great-grandson’s son told it to his son’s son, and he told it to my father, and my father told it to me.”

===Chinese===
“因为这故事是我家传的。”说书人答道，“蒙根（Mongan）当年走进仙境（Faery）时，我族中有一位凯里德随行在侧。故事头一回被讲出来的时候，就是这位凯里德听的。后来他讲给他的儿子，他的儿子又讲给儿子，那儿子的玄孙之子又讲给了他儿子的儿子，那人再讲给我的父亲，我父亲讲给了我。”

===Original===
“And you shall tell it to me,” cried the abbot triumphantly.

===Chinese===
“那你就讲给我听吧。”院长得胜似的喊道。

===Original===
“I will indeed,” said Cairidè.

===Chinese===
“我正要讲给您听。”凯里德说。

===Original===
Vellum was then brought and quills. The copyists sat at their tables. Ale was placed beside the storyteller, and he told this tale to the abbot.

===Chinese===
随即取来犊皮纸和羽笔，誊写生各就各位。麦芽酒摆在说书人手边，他便向院长讲起这个故事。

### II / II

===Original===
Said Cairidè:

===Chinese===
凯里德讲道：

===Original===
Mongan’s wife at that time was Brótiarna, the Flame Lady. She was passionate and fierce, and because the blood would flood suddenly to her cheek, so that she who had seemed a lily became, while you looked upon her, a rose, she was called Flame Lady. She loved Mongan with ecstasy and abandon, and for that also he called her Flame Lady.

===Chinese===
那时蒙根的妻子是布罗蒂亚娜（Brótiarna），人称“火焰夫人”（the Flame Lady）。她性子火热，为人泼烈；热血会突然涌上她的脸颊——方才望她还像一朵百合，就在你注视的这一会儿，她已变成一朵玫瑰——所以人们叫她火焰夫人。她爱蒙根爱得如痴如狂、不管不顾；为这个，他也叫她火焰夫人。

===Original===
But there may have been something of calculation even in her wildest moment, for if she was delighted in her affection she was tormented in it also, as are all those who love the great ones of life and strive to equal themselves where equality is not possible.

===Chinese===
可即便在她最狂放的时刻，兴许也藏着几分盘算：她在爱恋中欢喜，也在爱恋中受煎熬，一如世上所有爱上人中龙凤的人——偏要在无法比肩的地方，硬争一个比肩。

===Original===
For her husband was at once more than himself and less than himself. He was less than himself because he was now Mongan. He was more than himself because he was one who had long disappeared from the world of men. His lament had been sung and his funeral games played many, many years before, and Brótiarna sensed in him secrets, experiences, knowledges in which she could have no part, and for which she was greedily envious.

===Chinese===
因为她的丈夫，既多于他自己，又少于他自己。说他少于他自己，是因为他如今是蒙根；说他多于他自己，是因为他是一位早已从人世间消逝的人。为他唱的哀歌早已唱过，为他办的葬礼竞技也早已办过许多许多年；布罗蒂亚娜感觉到他身上藏着秘密、经历和学问，那是她无从有份的东西，为此她嫉妒得心痒难熬。

===Original===
So she was continually asking him little, simple questions apropos of every kind of thing.

===Chinese===
于是她没完没了地就各式各样的事，问他一些小小的、听着天真的问题。

===Original===
She weighed all that he said on whatever subject, and when he talked in his sleep she listened to his dream.

===Chinese===
无论他谈什么题目，她都要掂量他说的每一句话；他睡梦里说话时，她便凑近去听他的梦。

===Original===
The knowledge that she gleaned from those listenings tormented her far more than it satisfied her, for the names of other women were continually on his lips, sometimes in terms of dear affection, sometimes in accents of anger or despair, and in his sleep he spoke familiarly of people whom the storytellers told of, but who had been dead for centuries. Therefore she was perplexed, and became filled with a very rage of curiosity.

===Chinese===
从那些偷听里拾掇来的零碎，与其说满足了她，不如说更煎熬了她：别的女人的名字不断挂在他嘴边，有时是亲昵怜惜的口吻，有时是愤怒或绝望的声调；他睡梦里还亲亲热热地念叨一些人——那些人只活在说书人的故事里，人已死了好几百年了。因此她满心惶惑，渐渐被一种狂怒般的好奇填满了。

===Original===
Among the names which her husband mentioned there was one which, because of the frequency with which it appeared, and because of the tone of anguish and love and longing in which it was uttered, she thought of oftener than the others: this name was Duv Laca. Although she questioned and cross-questioned Cairidè, her storyteller, she could discover nothing about a lady who had been known as the Black Duck. But one night when Mongan seemed to speak with Duv Laca he mentioned her father as Fiachna Duv mac Demain, and the storyteller said that king had been dead for a vast number of years.

===Chinese===
丈夫念叨过的名字里头，有一个她比谁都想得多：它出现得最勤，吐出来时又总带着痛楚、爱恋与渴慕的声气——这个名字就是杜夫·拉卡（Duv Laca）。她一遍又一遍盘问她家的说书人凯里德，却怎么也打听不出那位人称“黑鸭”（Black Duck）的夫人的来历。可有一夜，蒙根像是正同杜夫·拉卡说话，提到了她的父亲菲亚赫纳·杜夫·麦克·德曼（Fiachna Duv mac Demain），说书人却说，那位王已经死去不知多少年了。

===Original===
She asked her husband then, boldly, to tell her the story of Duv Laca, and under the influence of their mutual love he promised to tell it to her some time, but each time she reminded him of his promise he became confused, and said that he would tell it some other time.

===Chinese===
于是她大起胆子，求丈夫给她讲杜夫·拉卡的故事；趁着两人情浓，他答应改日讲给她听。可她每回一提这个约，他就变得讪讪的，说还是另找时候再讲吧。

===Original===
As time went on the poor Flame Lady grew more and more jealous of Duv Laca, and more and more certain that, if only she could know what had happened, she would get some ease to her tormented heart and some assuagement of her perfectly natural curiosity. Therefore she lost no opportunity of reminding Mongan of his promise, and on each occasion he renewed the promise and put it back to another time.

===Chinese===
日子一久，可怜的火焰夫人对杜夫·拉卡的醋意越来越浓，也越来越认定：只要能知道当年到底出了什么事，她那颗受煎熬的心就能得些安宁，她那份再自然不过的好奇也能得些抚慰。于是她逮着一切机会提醒蒙根践约，而他每一回都重申前约，再把它推到下一回。

### III / III

===Original===
In the year when Ciaran the son of the Carpenter died, the same year when Tuathal Maelgariv was killed and the year when Diarmait the son of Cerrbel became king of all Ireland, the year 538 of our era in short, it happened that there was a great gathering of the men of Ireland at the Hill of Uisneach in Royal Meath.

===Chinese===
且说木匠之子基兰（Ciaran）去世的那一年，图阿尔·梅尔加里夫（Tuathal Maelgariv）被杀的同一年，瑟尔贝尔之子迪尔梅德（Diarmait）登基为全爱尔兰之王的那一年——总而言之，公元538年——爱尔兰人在王土米斯（Royal Meath）的乌斯尼奇山（Hill of Uisneach）举行了一场盛大的集会。

===Original===
In addition to the Council which was being held, there were games and tournaments and brilliant deployments of troops, and universal feastings and enjoyments. The gathering lasted for a week, and on the last day of the week Mongan was moving through the crowd with seven guards, his storyteller Cairidè, and his wife.

===Chinese===
会议之外，还有竞技比武，有焕然耀眼的军阵操演，还有普天同庆的宴饮欢愉。集会一连开了七天；到了最后一天，蒙根正带着七名卫士、他的说书人凯里德和他的妻子在人群中走动。

===Original===
It had been a beautiful day, with brilliant sunshine and great sport, but suddenly clouds began to gather in the sky to the west, and others came rushing blackly from the east. When these clouds met the world went dark for a space, and there fell from the sky a shower of hailstones, so large that each man wondered at their size, and so swift and heavy that the women and young people of the host screamed from the pain of the blows they received.

===Chinese===
那本是个艳阳高照、赛事正酣的好天。可忽然间，西边天上聚起了云，又有乌云从东边黑压压地涌来。两路云一相会，天地霎时昏暗了一阵，天上落下好一阵冰雹——雹子大得人人称奇，又急又沉，打得人群里的妇人和后生一个个疼得尖叫起来。

===Original===
Mongan’s men made a roof of their shields, and the hailstones battered on the shields so terribly that even under them they were afraid. They began to move away from the host looking for shelter, and when they had gone apart a little way they turned the edge of a small hill and a knoll of trees, and in the twinkling of an eye they were in fair weather.

===Chinese===
蒙根的随从用盾牌搭起一片顶。冰雹砸在盾上砰砰作响，凶猛骇人，躲在底下的人也心惊胆战。他们开始离开人群寻处躲避，才走出去不多远，刚绕过一座小山的山肩和一丛树冈，眨眼之间，已是朗朗晴日。

===Original===
One minute they heard the clashing and bashing of the hailstones, the howling of the venomous wind, the screams of women and the uproar of the crowd on the Hill of Uisneach, and the next minute they heard nothing more of those sounds and saw nothing more of these sights, for they had been permitted to go at one step out of the world of men and into the world of Faery.

===Chinese===
前一刻，他们还听得见冰雹的劈啪轰击、毒风的呼啸、妇人的尖叫，以及乌斯尼奇山上人群的鼎沸；后一刻，那些声响统统听不见了，那些景象也统统看不见了——因为他们蒙了恩准，一步之间，跨出世人的世界，跨进了仙界的疆土。

### IV / IV

===Original===
There is a difference between this world and the world of Faery, but it is not immediately perceptible. Everything that is here is there, but the things that are there are better than those that are here. All things that are bright are there brighter. There is more gold in the sun and more silver in the moon of that land. There is more scent in the flowers, more savour in the fruit. There is more comeliness in the men and more tenderness in the women. Everything in Faery is better by this one wonderful degree, and it is by this betterness you will know that you are there if you should ever happen to get there.

===Chinese===
这个世界与仙境的世界是有分别的，只是那分别不能一眼看破。这边有的东西，那边样样都有；只是那边的东西，样样都比这边的更好。这边明亮的，那边更加明亮。那片土地上的太阳含金更多，月亮含银更多。花更香，果更甜。男人更俊雅，女人更温存。仙境里的万事万物，都恰好奇妙地胜出一筹；你若有朝一日当真到了那里，凭这一筹之胜，你便知道自己到了何处。

===Original===
Mongan and his companions stepped from the world of storm into sunshine and a scented world. The instant they stepped they stood, bewildered, looking at each other silently, questioningly, and then with one accord they turned to look back whence they had come.

===Chinese===
蒙根一行从风雨的世界一步踏进阳光里，踏进一个芬芳的世界。脚跟才落地，他们便怔怔站住，默默相顾，满眼疑问；随即不约而同地回过头去，望向来路。

===Original===
There was no storm behind them. The sunlight drowsed there as it did in front, a peaceful flooding of living gold. They saw the shapes of the country to which their eyes were accustomed, and recognised the well-known landmarks, but it seemed that the distant hills were a trifle higher, and the grass which clothed them and stretched between was greener, was more velvety: that the trees were better clothed and had more of peace as they hung over the quiet ground.

===Chinese===
身后并没有风雨。那边的阳光同前方一样，慵懒地铺展着，是一片安详的、活金般的流光。他们望见自己看惯了的山川形胜，认出了熟稔的地标；只是远山仿佛略高了些，覆在山上、铺在山间的青草更绿了，更如天鹅绒了；树木也披挂得更丰美，静静垂在安宁的土地上，透出更多的静谧。

===Original===
But Mongan knew what had happened, and he smiled with glee as he watched his astonished companions, and he sniffed that balmy air as one whose nostrils remembered it.

===Chinese===
蒙根却知道出了什么事。他看着一脸惊愕的同伴们，咧嘴直乐，又深深嗅着那温软的空气，像一个鼻孔还记得它的人。

===Original===
“You had better come with me,” he said.

===Chinese===
“你们最好跟我来。”他说。

===Original===
“Where are we?” his wife asked.

===Chinese===
“我们这是在哪儿？”妻子问。

===Original===
“Why, we are here,” cried Mongan; “where else should we be?”

===Chinese===
“嗨，我们在这儿呀，”蒙根喊道，“还能在哪儿？”

===Original===
He set off then, and the others followed, staring about them cautiously, and each man keeping a hand on the hilt of his sword.

===Chinese===
于是他动身前行，众人跟着走，小心翼翼地四下张望，每人都有一只手扣在剑柄上。

===Original===
“Are we in Faery?” the Flame Lady asked.

===Chinese===
“我们是在仙境吗？”火焰夫人问。

===Original===
“We are,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“正是。”蒙根说。

===Original===
When they had gone a little distance they came to a grove of ancient trees. Mightily tall and well grown these trees were, and the trunk of each could not have been spanned by ten broad men. As they went among these quiet giants into the dappled obscurity and silence, their thoughts became grave, and all the motions of their minds elevated as though they must equal in greatness and dignity those ancient and glorious trees. When they passed through the grove they saw a lovely house before them, built of mellow wood and with a roof of bronze﻿—it was like the dwelling of a king, and over the windows of the Sunny Room there was a balcony. There were ladies on this balcony, and when they saw the travellers approaching they sent messengers to welcome them.

===Chinese===
走了一程，他们来到一片古树的林子。这些树生得高大雄伟，每一株的树干，十个彪形大汉手拉手也围不拢。穿行在这些沉静的巨人之间，走进斑驳的幽暗与寂静里，众人的心思庄重起来，胸中意气也随之高远，仿佛非要与这些古老光荣的树木比一比伟岸与尊严。穿过树林，眼前现出一座可爱的宅子，以温润的木料建成，覆着青铜的屋顶——那像是一位王的居所；向阳厅（the Sunny Room）的窗子上方有一道露台。露台上立着几位女子，望见来客渐近，便遣了使者前去相迎。

===Original===
Mongan and his companions were then brought into the house, and all was done for them that could be done for honoured guests. Everything within the house was as excellent as all without, and it was inhabited by seven men and seven women, and it was evident that Mongan and these people were well acquainted.

===Chinese===
蒙根一行随即被请进屋中，贵客该受的礼遇，一样不缺地都给了他们。屋里屋外一般精美；宅中住着七男七女，而蒙根与这些人分明是旧相识。

===Original===
