In the evening a feast was prepared, and when they had eaten well there was a banquet. There were seven vats of wine, and as Mongan loved wine he was very happy, and he drank more on that occasion than anyone had ever noticed him to drink before.

===Chinese===
入夜备下了盛宴，酒饭用罢，又开华筵。席上有七大桶酒。蒙根素来嗜酒，快活非常，那一晚喝下肚的酒，比任何人以往见过的都多。

===Original===
It was while he was in this condition of glee and expansion that the Flame Lady put her arms about his neck and begged he would tell her the story of Duv Laca, and, being boisterous then and full of good spirits, he agreed to her request, and he prepared to tell the tale.

===Chinese===
正当他酒酣兴发、心花怒放的当口，火焰夫人搂住他的脖颈，恳求他讲一讲杜夫·拉卡的故事。他那时正豪兴滔滔，满心畅快，一口应允，便准备开讲。

===Original===
The seven men and seven women of the Fairy Palace then took their places about him in a half-circle; his own seven guards sat behind them; his wife, the Flame Lady, sat by his side; and at the back of all Cairidè his storyteller sat, listening with all his ears, and remembering every word that was uttered.

===Chinese===
仙宫的七男七女便围着他坐成半圈；自家的七名卫士坐在他们身后；妻子火焰夫人坐在他身旁；最后头坐着他的说书人凯里德——他竖起两只耳朵听着，说出的每一个字都记在心里。

### V / V

===Original===
Said Mongan:

===Chinese===
蒙根讲道：

===Original===
In the days of long ago and the times that have disappeared forever, there was one Fiachna Finn the son of Baltan, the son of Murchertach, the son of Muredach, the son of Eogan, the son of Neill. He went from his own country when he was young, for he wished to see the land of Lochlann, and he knew that he would be welcomed by the king of that country, for Fiachna’s father and Eolgarg’s father had done deeds in common and were obliged to each other.

===Chinese===
在那久远的岁月、早已永远消逝的年代里，有一位菲亚赫纳·芬（Fiachna Finn），他是巴尔坦（Baltan）之子，穆尔切塔赫（Murchertach）之孙，穆雷达克（Muredach）、埃奥甘（Eogan）、尼尔（Neill）一脉相承的后人。他年轻时离开故土，想去看看洛克兰（Lochlann）之地；他知道那国的王必定欢迎他，因为菲亚赫纳的父亲与埃奥尔加格的父亲曾共过事，彼此欠着情分。

===Original===
He was welcomed, and he stayed at the Court of Lochlann in great ease and in the midst of pleasures.

===Chinese===
他果然大受欢迎，在洛克兰的王廷里逍遥自在，日日欢愉。

===Original===
It then happened that Eolgarg Mor fell sick and the doctors could not cure him. They sent for other doctors, but they could not cure him, nor could anyone say what he was suffering from, beyond that he was wasting visibly before their eyes, and would certainly become a shadow and disappear in air unless he was healed and fattened and made visible.

===Chinese===
不巧埃奥尔加格·莫尔（Eolgarg Mor）病倒了，医生们治不好他。又请来别的医生，还是治不好；也没人说得出他害的是什么病，只看得见他当着众人的眼一日日消瘦下去，若不把他医好、养壮、让他重新显出形来，他必定要瘦成一道影子，散进空气里。

===Original===
They sent for more distant doctors, and then for others more distant still, and at last they found a man who claimed that he could make a cure if the king were supplied with the medicine which he would order.

===Chinese===
他们又派人去请更远处的医生，然后再请更更远的，最后总算寻到一人，声称只要照他开的方子把药备齐，他便能把王治好。

===Original===
“What medicine is that?” said they all.

===Chinese===
“那是什么药？”众人齐声问。

===Original===
“This is the medicine,” said the doctor. “Find a perfectly white cow with red ears, and boil it down in the lump, and if the king drinks that rendering he will recover.”

===Chinese===
“药是这样的，”医生说，“寻一头通体纯白、双耳赤红的母牛，整头下锅熬成膏；王喝下那熬出的膏汁，病就好了。”

===Original===
Before he had well said it messengers were going from the palace in all directions looking for such a cow. They found lots of cows which were nearly like what they wanted, but it was only by chance they came on the cow which would do the work, and that beast belonged to the most notorious and malicious and cantankerous female in Lochlann, the Black Hag. Now the Black Hag was not only those things that have been said; she was also whiskered and warty and one-eyed and obstreperous, and she was notorious and ill-favoured in many other ways also.

===Chinese===
他话音未落，王宫的信使已四下里出发，去寻这样一头牛。近似模样的牛寻到不少，可真能顶事的那一头，却是碰巧撞见的；而那头牛的主人，是洛克兰最名声在外、最歹毒、最难缠的一个女人——黑老妪（the Black Hag）。这黑老妪还不止上文说的那些毛病：她还长着颊须，浑身疣子，是独眼，吵闹起来没个完；至于声名狼藉、相貌丑陋，旁的方面也还多得是。

===Original===
They offered her a cow in the place of her own cow, but she refused to give it. Then they offered a cow for each leg of her cow, but she would not accept that offer unless Fiachna went bail for the payment. He agreed to do so, and they drove the beast away.

===Chinese===
他们提出以一牛换她的牛，她不肯给。又提出她的牛每条腿折一头牛、四条腿赔四头，她还是不依，除非菲亚赫纳出面担保这笔赔付。他应承了下来，众人便把牛赶走了。

===Original===
On the return journey he was met by messengers who brought news from Ireland. They said that the King of Ulster was dead, and that he, Fiachna Finn, had been elected king in the dead king’s place. He at once took ship for Ireland, and found that all he had been told was true, and he took up the government of Ulster.

===Chinese===
归途中，信使迎面而来，带来了爱尔兰的消息：阿尔斯特（Ulster）王驾崩，众人已推举他菲亚赫纳·芬补上死者的王位。他当即乘船回爱尔兰，发现所报句句属实，便执掌起阿尔斯特的政事。

### VI / VI

===Original===
A year passed, and one day as he was sitting at judgement there came a great noise from without, and this noise was so persistent that the people and suitors were scandalised, and Fiachna at last ordered that the noisy person should be brought before him to be judged.

===Chinese===
一年过去。一天，他正坐堂断案，外头传来好大一阵喧哗。那吵闹没完没了，堂上百姓和告状的都皱起眉来，菲亚赫纳终于下令，把那吵闹之人带上堂来听审。

===Original===
It was done, and to his surprise the person turned out to be the Black Hag.

===Chinese===
人带来了，他一看，竟是黑老妪，不由吃了一惊。

===Original===
She blamed him in the court before his people, and complained that he had taken away her cow, and that she had not been paid the four cows he had gone bail for, and she demanded judgement from him and justice.

===Chinese===
她当着满堂百姓数落他，抱怨他牵走了她的牛，抱怨他担保的四头牛至今没有赔付；她要他裁断，还她公道。

===Original===
“If you will consider it to be justice, I will give you twenty cows myself,” said Fiachna.

===Chinese===
“只要你觉得这算公道，我自己赔你二十头牛也愿意。”菲亚赫纳说。

===Original===
“I would not take all the cows in Ulster,” she screamed.

===Chinese===
“把阿尔斯特的牛全给我我也不依！”她尖声叫道。

===Original===
“Pronounce judgement yourself,” said the king, “and if I can do what you demand I will do it.” For he did not like to be in the wrong, and he did not wish that any person should have an unsatisfied claim upon him.

===Chinese===
“那你就自己来裁断吧，”王说，“凡你要求的，只要我办得到，我一定办。”因为他不愿亏理，也不愿任何人手里攥着一件对他未偿的索求。

===Original===
The Black Hag then pronounced judgement, and the king had to fulfil it.

===Chinese===
黑老妪便裁断了，王只好照办。

===Original===
“I have come,” said she, “from the east to the west; you must come from the west to the east and make war for me, and revenge me on the King of Lochlann.”

===Chinese===
“我从东边来到西边，”她说，“你就必须从西边打到东边，替我兴兵，向洛克兰王报仇。”

===Original===
Fiachna had to do as she demanded, and, although it was with a heavy heart, he set out in three days’ time for Lochlann, and he brought with him ten battalions.

===Chinese===
菲亚赫纳只得照她所求去办。他心头沉重，三天后还是发兵洛克兰，带去了十个营的人马。

===Original===
He sent messengers before him to Big Eolgarg warning him of his coming, of his intention, and of the number of troops he was bringing; and when he landed Eolgarg met him with an equal force, and they fought together.

===Chinese===
他先遣信使知会大埃奥尔加格（Big Eolgarg），说明自己将至、来意如何、带兵几许。他一登陆，埃奥尔加格便以相当的兵力迎战，两军厮杀起来。

===Original===
In the first battle three hundred of the men of Lochlann were killed, but in the next battle Eolgarg Mor did not fight fair, for he let some venomous sheep out of a tent, and these attacked the men of Ulster and killed nine hundred of them.

===Chinese===
头一阵，洛克兰人折了三百；可到了下一阵，埃奥尔加格·莫尔打得不地道了——他从一顶帐篷里放出一群毒羊。毒羊直扑阿尔斯特人，咬死了九百个。

===Original===
So vast was the slaughter made by these sheep and so great the terror they caused, that no one could stand before them, but by great good luck there was a wood at hand, and the men of Ulster, warriors and princes and charioteers, were forced to climb up the trees, and they roosted among the branches like great birds, while the venomous sheep ranged below bleating terribly and tearing up the ground.

===Chinese===
这群羊杀伤之惨、震怖之烈，竟至无人能在它们面前站住脚。亏得运气好，旁边就有片树林，阿尔斯特人——勇士也好，王孙也好，御者也好——只好爬上树去，像一群大鸟栖在枝桠间；树下毒羊奔突，咩咩惨叫，把地皮都刨翻了。

===Original===
Fiachna Fim was also sitting in a tree, very high up, and he was disconsolate.

===Chinese===
菲亚赫纳·芬也坐在一棵树上，坐得极高，垂头丧气。

===Original===
“We are disgraced,” said he.

===Chinese===
“我们丢尽脸面了。”他说。

===Original===
“It is very lucky,” said the man in the branch below, “that a sheep cannot climb a tree.”

===Chinese===
“真是万幸，”下方枝上那位说，“羊不会爬树。”

===Original===
“We are disgraced forever,” said the King of Ulster.

===Chinese===
“我们把脸丢到永远了。”阿尔斯特王说。

===Original===
“If those sheep learn how to climb, we are undone surely,” said the man below.

===Chinese===
“这群羊要是学会了爬树，我们可就真完了。”下面那人说。

===Original===
“I will go down and fight the sheep,” said Fiachna.

===Chinese===
“我这就下去同羊打一场。”菲亚赫纳说。

===Original===
But the others would not let the king go.

===Chinese===
众人却不放王下去。

===Original===
“It is not right,” they said, “that you should fight sheep.”

===Chinese===
“让大王去斗羊，”众人说，“这不成体统。”

===Original===
“Someone must fight them,” said Fiachna Finn, “but no more of my men shall die until I fight myself; for if I am fated to die, I will die and I cannot escape it, and if it is the sheep’s fate to die, then die they will; for there is no man can avoid destiny, and there is no sheep can dodge it either.”

===Chinese===
“总得有人去斗它们，”菲亚赫纳·芬说，“但在我亲自出战之前，我的人不能再死了；因为我若命中该死，我便死，逃也逃不掉；若轮到羊死，它们也躲不过。世上没有哪个男人躲得开命数，也没有哪只羊躲得开。”

===Original===
“Praise be to god!” said the warrior that was higher up.

===Chinese===
“赞美神明！”上面那位勇士说。

===Original===
“Amen!” said the man who was higher than he, and the rest of the warriors wished good luck to the king.

===Chinese===
“阿门！”比他还高的人应道。其余的勇士纷纷祝王好运。

===Original===
He started then to climb down the tree with a heavy heart, but while he hung from the last branch and was about to let go, he noticed a tall warrior walking towards him. The king pulled himself up on the branch again and sat dangle-legged on it to see what the warrior would do.

===Chinese===
于是他怀着沉重的心往树下爬。可当他吊在最后一根枝上、正要撒手时，忽见一名高大的战士朝自己走来。王重又攀上枝头，两条腿悬空坐着，看那战士要做什么。

===Original===
The stranger was a very tall man, dressed in a green cloak with a silver brooch at the shoulder. He had a golden band about his hair and golden sandals on his feet, and he was laughing heartily at the plight of the men of Ireland.

===Chinese===
那生客是个极高的人，披一件绿斗篷，肩上别着银胸针；发间箍一道金带，脚上蹬金色凉鞋。他正冲着爱尔兰人这副狼狈相放声大笑。

### VII / VII

===Original===
“It is not nice of you to laugh at us,” said Fiachna Finn.

===Chinese===
“你这样嘲笑我们，可不厚道。”菲亚赫纳·芬说。

===Original===
“Who could help laughing at a king hunkering on a branch and his army roosting around him like hens?” said the stranger.

===Chinese===
“一位王蹲在树杈上，大军像一群母鸡栖满他四周——这样的光景，谁忍得住笑？”生客说。

===Original===
“Nevertheless,” the king replied, “it would be courteous of you not to laugh at misfortune.”

===Chinese===
“话虽如此，”王答道，“不笑人遭难，才算知礼。”

===Original===
“We laugh when we can,” commented the stranger, “and are thankful for the chance.”

===Chinese===
“能笑的时候我们只管笑，”生客评道，“有这样的机会，还该感谢呢。”

===Original===
“You may come up into the tree,” said Fiachna, “for I perceive that you are a mannerly person, and I see that some of the venomous sheep are charging in this direction. I would rather protect you,” he continued, “than see you killed; for,” said he lamentably, “I am getting down now to fight the sheep.”

===Chinese===
“你可以上树来，”菲亚赫纳说，“我看得出你是个懂礼数的人；而且我瞧见有几只毒羊正朝这边冲来。与其看着你丧命，我宁可护你一护，”他接着说，“因为——”他悲声补了一句，“我这就下树去斗羊了。”

===Original===
“They will not hurt me,” said the stranger.

===Chinese===
“它们伤不着我。”生客说。

===Original===
“Who are you?” the king asked.

===Chinese===
“你是谁？”王问。

===Original===
“I am Manannán, the son of Lir.”

===Chinese===
“我是利尔（Lir）之子玛纳南。”

===Original===
Fiachna knew then that the stranger could not be hurt.

===Chinese===
菲亚赫纳随即明白，这生客是伤不着的。

===Original===
“What will you give me if I deliver you from the sheep?” asked Manannán.

===Chinese===
“若我救你脱离这群羊，你拿什么谢我？”玛纳南问。

===Original===
“I will give you anything you ask, if I have that thing.”

===Chinese===
“只要我有的，你要什么我便给什么。”

===Original===
“I ask the rights of your crown and of your household for one day.”

===Chinese===
“我讨你王冠的权利和你家室的权利，为期一日。”

===Original===
Fiachna’s breath was taken away by that request, and he took a little time to compose himself, then he said mildly:

===Chinese===
这个请求惊得菲亚赫纳透不过气来。他定了好一会儿神，才温和地说：

===Original===
“I will not have one man of Ireland killed if I can save him. All that I have they give me, all that I have I give to them, and if I must give this also, then I will give this, although it would be easier for me to give my life.”

===Chinese===
“只要救得了一个爱尔兰人，我断不肯让他死。我所有的一切是他们给我的，我所有的一切也给他们；倘若连这个也必须给，那我便给这个吧——虽然要我交出性命，倒还容易些。”

===Original===
“That is agreed,” said Manannán.

===Chinese===
“一言为定。”玛纳南说。

===Original===
He had something wrapped in a fold of his cloak, and he unwrapped and produced this thing.

===Chinese===
他斗篷的褶里裹着一样东西；他解开褶子，把那东西取了出来。

===Original===
It was a dog.

===Chinese===
那是一条狗。

===Original===
Now if the sheep were venomous, this dog was more venomous still, for it was fearful to look at. In body it was not large, but its head was of a great size, and the mouth that was shaped in that head was able to open like the lid of a pot. It was not teeth which were in that head, but hooks and fangs and prongs. Dreadful was that mouth to look at, terrible to look into, woeful to think about; and from it, or from the broad, loose nose that waggled above it, there came a sound which no word of man could describe, for it was not a snarl, nor was it a howl, although it was both of these. It was neither a growl nor a grunt, although it was both of these; it was not a yowl nor a groan, although it was both of these: for it was one sound made up of these sounds, and there was in it, too, a whine and a yelp, and a long-drawn snoring noise, and a deep purring noise, and a noise that was like the squeal of a rusty hinge, and there were other noises in it also.

===Chinese===
羊若算得毒，这条狗比羊还毒三分，因为它那模样，看一眼都叫人心惊。身子不算大，脑袋却大得出奇，那脑袋上开出来的那张嘴，张开来能像锅盖一样翻开。嘴里长的不是牙齿，是钩子、獠牙和尖刺。那张嘴看着可怕，往里看吓人，想一想难过；从那张嘴里——或者从它上面那只晃晃悠悠的大塌鼻子里——发出一种声响，人间的言语没有一个字形容得出：那不是狺叫，也不是嗥叫，虽然两样都是；那不是低吼，也不是呼噜，虽然两样都是；那不是哀嗥，也不是呻吟，虽然两样也都是——它是所有这些声音合成的一声，里头还夹着呜咽，夹着狺吠，一阵拖得老长的鼾声，一阵深深的喉鸣，还有一声像锈门轴的尖叫，此外还有别的许多响动。

===Original===
“The gods be praised!” said the man who was in the branch above the king.

===Chinese===
“赞美诸神！”王头顶枝上的人说。

===Original===
“What for this time?” said the king.

===Chinese===
“这回又是为什么？”王说。

===Original===
“Because that dog cannot climb a tree,” said the man.

===Chinese===
“因为那条狗不会爬树。”那人说。

===Original===
And the man on a branch yet above him groaned out “Amen!”

===Chinese===
比他更高一根枝上的人哼出了一声“阿门！”

===Original===
“There is nothing to frighten sheep like a dog,” said Manannán, “and there is nothing to frighten these sheep like this dog.”

===Chinese===
“天下吓羊的东西，莫过于狗，”玛纳南说，“而吓这几只羊的东西，莫过于这条狗。”

===Original===
He put the dog on the ground then.

===Chinese===
说罢他把狗放到了地上。

===Original===
“Little dogeen, little treasure,” said he, “go and kill the sheep.”

===Chinese===
“小狗丁点儿，小心肝儿，”他说，“去，把羊都宰了。”

===Original===
And when he said that the dog put an addition and an addendum on to the noise he had been making before, so that the men of Ireland stuck their fingers into their ears and turned the whites of their eyes upwards, and nearly fell off their branches with the fear and the fright which that sound put into them.

===Chinese===
他这话一出口，那狗先前的声响上头又添了一笔，外加补了一条附录，吓得爱尔兰人个个把手指塞进耳朵，两眼翻出眼白，险些从各自的枝子上栽下去——那声音灌进他们心里的，就只剩害怕和惊惶。

===Original===
It did not take the dog long to do what he had been ordered. He went forward, at first, with a slow waddle, and as the venomous sheep came to meet him in bounces, he then went to meet them in wriggles; so that in a while he went so fast that you could see nothing of him but a head and a wriggle. He dealt with the sheep in this way, a jump and a chop for each, and he never missed his jump and he never missed his chop. When he got his grip he swung round on it as if it was a hinge. The swing began with the chop, and it ended with the bit loose and the sheep giving its last kick. At the end of ten minutes all the sheep were lying on the ground, and the same bit was out of every sheep, and every sheep was dead.

===Chinese===
领了命，狗没费多少工夫。起先它摇摇摆摆、慢慢腾腾地往前走；毒羊一蹦一蹦迎上来，它便扭扭摆摆迎上去；不一刻它快到这步田地——你只能看见一颗脑袋和一道扭动的影子。它料理羊的法子是：一扑一咬，一只一结，扑从不落空，咬也从不落空。咬住了，它便就势抡一圈，仿佛那咬住的地方是一道门轴；这一抡从下口起头，到那块肉扯脱、羊蹬出最后一腿收尾。十分钟后，羊全躺在了地上；每只羊身上咬脱的都是同一块肉，每只羊都死了。

===Original===
“You can come down now,” said Manannán.

===Chinese===
“你们现在可以下来了。”玛纳南说。

===Original===
“That dog can’t climb a tree,” said the man in the branch above the king warningly.

===Chinese===
“那条狗不会爬树。”王头顶枝上的人提醒道。

===Original===
“Praise be to the gods!” said the man who was above him.

===Chinese===
“赞美诸神！”他上面那人说。

===Original===
“Amen!” said the warrior who was higher up than that.

===Chinese===
“阿门！”更高处的勇士说。

===Original===
And the man in the next tree said:

===Chinese===
隔壁树上的人开口了：

===Original===
“Don’t move a hand or a foot until the dog chokes himself to death on the dead meat.”

===Chinese===
“手也别动，脚也别动，等那狗吃死羊肉噎死自己再说。”

===Original===
The dog, however, did not eat a bit of the meat. He trotted to his master, and Manannán took him up and wrapped him in his cloak.

===Chinese===
然而那狗一口肉也没吃。它一路小跑回到主人身边，玛纳南把它抱起来，裹进斗篷里。

===Original===
“Now you can come down,” said he.

===Chinese===
“现在你们可以下来了。”他说。

===Original===
“I wish that dog was dead!” said the king.

===Chinese===
“我恨不得那条狗死了才好！”王说。

===Original===
But he swung himself out of the tree all the same, for he did not wish to seem frightened before Manannán.

===Chinese===
可他还是翻身下了树，因为他不愿在玛纳南面前显得胆怯。

===Original===
“You can go now and beat the men of Lochlann,” said Manannán. “You will be King of Lochlann before nightfall.”

===Chinese===
“你现在可以去收拾洛克兰人了，”玛纳南说，“天黑之前，你就是洛克兰王。”

===Original===
“I wouldn’t mind that,” said the king.

===Chinese===
“这我倒不介意。”王说。

===Original===
“It’s no threat,” said Manannán.

===Chinese===
“这不是恫吓。”玛纳南说。

===Original===
The son of Lir turned then and went away in the direction of Ireland to take up his one-day rights, and Fiachna continued his battle with the Lochlannachs.

===Chinese===
利尔之子随即转身，朝爱尔兰方向去了，去享用他那一天的权利；菲亚赫纳则继续同洛克兰人（Lochlannachs）交战。

===Original===
He beat them before nightfall, and by that victory he became King of Lochlann and King of the Saxons and the Britons.

===Chinese===
天黑之前他大破敌军；凭着这一胜，他做了洛克兰王，又做了撒克逊人与不列颠人的王。

===Original===
He gave the Black Hag seven castles with their territories, and he gave her one hundred of every sort of cattle that he had captured. She was satisfied.

===Chinese===
他给了黑老妪七座城堡连同属地，又把缴获的每一种牲畜各一百头给了她。她这才满意了。

===Original===
Then he went back to Ireland, and after he had been there for some time his wife gave birth to a son.

===Chinese===
随后他回到爱尔兰。过了一段时日，他的妻子生下一个儿子。

### VIII / VIII

===Original===
“You have not told me one word about Duv Laca,” said the Flame Lady reproachfully.

===Chinese===
“杜夫·拉卡的事，你还一个字都没讲呢。”火焰夫人嗔怪道。

===Original===
“I am coming to that,” replied Mongan.

===Chinese===
“这就讲到了。”蒙根答道。

===Original===
He motioned towards one of the great vats, and wine was brought to him, of which he drank so joyously and so deeply that all people wondered at his thirst, his capacity, and his jovial spirits.

===Chinese===
他朝其中一只大酒桶摆了摆手，酒便给他端了来。他喝得那样欢畅、那样深长，满座的人都惊叹他的渴、他的量，和他那股快活劲儿。

===Original===
“Now, I will begin again.”

===Chinese===
“好，我重新讲起。”

---

===Original===
Said Mongan:

===Chinese===
蒙根讲道：

===Original===
There was an attendant in Fiachna Finn’s palace who was called An Dáv, and the same night that Fiachna’s wife bore a son, the wife of An Dáv gave birth to a son also. This latter child was called mac an Dáv, but the son of Fiachna’s wife was named Mongan.

===Chinese===
菲亚赫纳·芬宫中有一名侍从，名叫安·达夫（An Dáv）。菲亚赫纳的妻子产下男婴的那一夜，安·达夫的妻子也生了一个儿子。后生的这个孩子叫作麦克·安·达夫（mac an Dáv），而菲亚赫纳之妻的儿子，取名叫蒙根。

===Original===
“Ah!” murmured the Flame Lady.

===Chinese===
“啊！”火焰夫人低声道。

===Original===
The queen was angry. She said it was unjust and presumptuous that the servant should get a child at the same time that she got one herself, but there was no help for it, because the child was there and could not be obliterated.

===Chinese===
王后动了气。她说奴仆竟同她同夜得子，既不公道，又僭越无礼。可这也无可奈何——孩子已经在那儿了，总不能把他抹掉。

===Original===
Now this also must be told.

===Chinese===
如今这事也须交代。

===Original===
There was a neighbouring prince called Fiachna Duv, and he was the ruler of the Dal Fiatach. For a long time he had been at enmity and spiteful warfare with Fiachna Finn; and to this Fiachna Duv there was born in the same night a daughter, and this girl was named Duv Laca of the White Hand.

===Chinese===
邻邦有一位君主，名叫菲亚赫纳·杜夫（Fiachna Duv），统辖着达尔·菲亚塔赫（Dal Fiatach）。他同菲亚赫纳·芬长年相互仇视，明争暗斗。就在同一夜，这位菲亚赫纳·杜夫家里添了一个女儿，女孩取名叫白手杜夫·拉卡（Duv Laca of the White Hand）。

===Original===
“Ah!” cried the Flame Lady.

===Chinese===
“啊！”火焰夫人叫出声来。

===Original===
“You see!” said Mongan, and he drank anew and joyously of the fairy wine.

===Chinese===
“你瞧见了吧！”蒙根说着，又欢畅地饮了一通仙酒。

===Original===
In order to end the trouble between Fiachna Finn and Fiachna Duv the babies were affianced to each other in the cradle on the day after they were born, and the men of Ireland rejoiced at that deed and at that news. But soon there came dismay and sorrow in the land, for when the little Mongan was three days old his real father, Manannán the son of Lir, appeared in the middle of the palace. He wrapped Mongan in his green cloak and took him away to rear and train in the Land of Promise, which is beyond the sea that is at the other side of the grave.

===Chinese===
为平息菲亚赫纳·芬与菲亚赫纳·杜夫之间的纷争，两个婴儿出生第二天便在摇篮里订了亲。爱尔兰人闻讯，人人欢喜，个个称庆。可不久，愁云惨雾便笼罩了大地——小蒙根出生才三天，他的生父利尔之子玛纳南赫然现身王宫正中。他用绿斗篷裹起蒙根，带到应许之地（the Land of Promise）去抚养调教；那地方在大海彼岸，而那片海，在坟茔的另一边。

===Original===
When Fiachna Duv heard that Mongan, who was affianced to his daughter Duv Laca, had disappeared, he considered that his compact of peace was at an end, and one day he came by surprise and attacked the palace. He killed Fiachna Finn in that battle, and be crowned himself King of Ulster.

===Chinese===
菲亚赫纳·杜夫听说与女儿杜夫·拉卡订了亲的蒙根失踪了，认定那份和平之约就此作废，便趁一日不备，突袭王宫。那一战里他杀死了菲亚赫纳·芬，自立为阿尔斯特王。

===Original===
The men of Ulster disliked him, and they petitioned Manannán to bring Mongan back, but Manannán would not do this until the boy was sixteen years of age and well reared in the wisdom of the Land of Promise. Then he did bring Mongan back, and by his means peace was made between Mongan and Fiachna Duv, and Mongan was married to his cradle-bride, the young Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
阿尔斯特人不喜欢他，便恳求玛纳南把蒙根送回来。可玛纳南不肯，直到少年满了十六岁、把应许之地的智慧学了个饱，才送他回来。回来之后，经他从中斡旋，蒙根与菲亚赫纳·杜夫讲和了；蒙根也娶了他摇篮里的未婚妻——年轻的杜夫·拉卡。

### IX / IX

===Original===
One day Mongan and Duv Laca were playing chess in their palace. Mongan had just made a move of skill, and he looked up from the board to see if Duv Laca seemed as discontented as she had a right to be. He saw then over Duv Laca’s shoulder a little black-faced, tufty-headed cleric leaning against the doorpost inside the room.

===Chinese===
一天，蒙根和杜夫·拉卡在宫里下棋。蒙根刚走了一步妙着，便从棋盘上抬起眼来，看杜夫·拉卡是不是露出了她理当露出的那副懊恼相。这一抬眼，他望见杜夫·拉卡肩后、屋里的门柱边，倚着一个黑脸膛、头发乱蓬蓬的小教士。

===Original===
“What are you doing there?” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“你在那儿做什么？”蒙根说。

===Original===
“What are you doing there yourself?” said the little black-faced cleric.

===Chinese===
“你又在这儿做什么？”黑脸小教士说。

===Original===
“Indeed, I have a right to be in my own house,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“这可是我自家的屋子，我自然有权在这里。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Indeed I do not agree with you,” said the cleric.

===Chinese===
“这话我可不赞成。”教士说。

===Original===
“Where ought I be, then?” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“那我该在哪儿？”蒙根说。

===Original===
“You ought to be at Dun Fiathac avenging the murder of your father,” replied the cleric, “and you ought to be ashamed of yourself for not having done it long ago. You can play chess with your wife when you have won the right to leisure.”

===Chinese===
“你该在邓·菲亚萨克（Dun Fiathac），为你父亲的被弑报仇，”教士答道，“迟迟不去，你自己也该害臊。等你挣得那份清闲，再同妻子下棋不迟。”

===Original===
“But how can I kill my wife’s father?” Mongan exclaimed.

===Chinese===
“可我怎么能杀我妻子的父亲呢？”蒙根叫道。

===Original===
“By starting about it at once,” said the cleric.

===Chinese===
“这就动手，便杀得成了。”教士说。

===Original===
“Here is a way of talking!” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“好一个说话的路数！”蒙根说。

===Original===
“I know,” the cleric continued, “that Duv Laca will not agree with a word I say on this subject, and that she will try to prevent you from doing what you have a right to do, for that is a wife’s business, but a man’s business is to do what I have just told you; so come with me now and do not wait to think about it, and do not wait to play any more chess. Fiachna Duv has only a small force with him at this moment, and we can burn his palace as he burned your father’s palace, and kill himself as he killed your father, and crown you King of Ulster rightfully the way he crowned himself wrongfully as a king.”

===Chinese===
“我知道，”教士接着说，“在这件事上，杜夫·拉卡连一个字也不会赞成我，她还要拦着你，不让你去做你有权去做的事，因为那是做妻子的本分；可做男人的本分，就是去做我方才告诉你的事。所以现在就跟我走，别停下来想，也别再下棋。菲亚赫纳·杜夫此刻身边只有一小队人马，他烧了你父亲的王宫，我们就烧他的王宫；他杀了你父亲，我们就杀他本人；他立自己为王立得不正，我们立你为阿尔斯特王，立得堂堂正正。”

===Original===
“I begin to think that you own a lucky tongue, my black-faced friend,” said Mongan, “and I will go with you.”

===Chinese===
“我开始觉得，你这舌头是条吉利的舌头，我黑脸的朋友，”蒙根说，“我跟你走。”

===Original===
He collected his forces then, and he burned Fiachna Duv’s fortress, and he killed Fiachna Duv, and he was crowned King of Ulster.

===Chinese===
他随即聚起兵马，焚了菲亚赫纳·杜夫的堡寨，杀了菲亚赫纳·杜夫，加冕做了阿尔斯特王。

===Original===
Then for the first time he felt secure and at liberty to play chess. But he did not know until afterwards that the black-faced, tufty-headed person was his father Manannán, although that was the fact.

===Chinese===
直到那时，他才头一回觉得安稳自在，可以放心下棋了。只是他当时并不知道——后来才晓得——那个黑脸膛、乱头发的人，就是他的父亲玛纳南；虽然事实正是如此。

===Original===
There are some who say, however, that Fiachna the Black was killed in the year 624 by the lord of the Scot’s Dal Riada, Condad Cerr, at the battle of Ard Carainn; but the people who say this do not know what they are talking about, and they do not care greatly what it is they say.

===Chinese===
不过也有人说什么黑菲亚赫纳死于624年，在阿德·卡林之战（battle of Ard Carainn）里，死在苏格兰人的达尔·里亚达（Dal Riada）之主康达德·切尔（Condad Cerr）手上；可是说这话的人，既不知道自己在说什么，也不大在意自己说的究竟是什么。

### X / X

===Original===
“There is nothing to marvel about in this Duv Laca,” said the Flame Lady scornfully. “She has got married, and she has been beaten at chess. It has happened before.”

===Chinese===
“这个杜夫·拉卡有什么可惊叹的。”火焰夫人嗤之以鼻，“嫁了人，又输了一盘棋。这种事从前也有过。”

===Original===
“Let us keep to the story,” said Mongan, and, having taken some few dozen deep draughts of the wine, he became even more jovial than before. Then he recommenced his tale:

===Chinese===
“我们还是照着故事讲。”蒙根说。他又灌下几十大口酒，比先前愈发兴高采烈，随后重新开讲：

---

===Original===
It happened on a day that Mongan had need of treasure. He had many presents to make, and he had not as much gold and silver and cattle as was proper for a king. He called his nobles together and discussed what was the best thing to be done, and it was arranged that he should visit the provincial kings and ask boons from them.

===Chinese===
一天，蒙根手头需要财货。他有许多礼要送，可他的金子、银子和牲畜，却不及一位王该有的数目。他把贵族们召来商议怎么办最好，议定由他遍访各省之王，向他们讨取恩赏。

===Original===
He set out at once on his round of visits, and the first province he went to was Leinster.

===Chinese===
他当即启程巡访，头一个去的省份是伦斯特（Leinster）。

===Original===
The King of Leinster at that time was Branduv, the son of Echach. He welcomed Mongan and treated him well, and that night Mongan slept in his palace.

===Chinese===
那时的伦斯特王，是埃卡赫（Echach）之子布兰杜夫（Branduv）。他热诚欢迎蒙根，殷勤款待；当夜蒙根便宿在他的王宫里。

===Original===
When he awoke in the morning he looked out of a lofty window, and he saw on the sunny lawn before the palace a herd of cows. There were fifty cows in all, for he counted them, and each cow had a calf beside her, and each cow and calf was pure white in colour, and each of them had red ears.

===Chinese===
早晨醒来，他从一扇高窗望出去，看见宫前向阳的草地上有一群牛。一共五十头——他数了——每头母牛身旁都跟着一头牛犊；牛和犊通体纯白，耳朵都是红的。

===Original===
When Mongan saw these cows, he fell in love with them as he had never fallen in love with anything before.

===Chinese===
蒙根一见这群牛，登时爱上了它们；他此生还从未这样爱过任何东西。

===Original===
He came down from the window and walked on the sunny lawn among the cows, looking at each of them and speaking words of affection and endearment to them all; and while he was thus walking and talking and looking and loving, he noticed that someone was moving beside him. He looked from the cows then, and saw that the King of Leinster was at his side.

===Chinese===
他下了高楼，走进向阳的草地，来到牛群中间，一头一头地端详，对每一头都说着怜爱亲昵的话。他正这么走着、说着、看着、爱着，忽觉身旁有人走动。他把目光从牛身上挪开，见伦斯特王已立在自己身边。

===Original===
“Are you in love with the cows?” Branduv asked him.

===Chinese===
“大王是爱上这些牛了？”布兰杜夫问他。

===Original===
“I am,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“爱上了。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Everybody is,” said the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
“谁见了都爱。”伦斯特王说。

===Original===
“I never saw anything like them,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“我从未见过这样的牛。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Nobody has,” said the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
“谁也没见过。”伦斯特王说。

===Original===
“I never saw anything I would rather have than these cows,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“天底下的东西，我最想得到的就是这群牛了。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“These,” said the King of Leinster, “are the most beautiful cows in Ireland, and,” he continued thoughtfully, “Duv Laca is the most beautiful woman in Ireland.”

===Chinese===
“这些牛，”伦斯特王说，“是爱尔兰最美的牛；而——”他若有所思地接着说，“杜夫·拉卡是爱尔兰最美的女人。”

===Original===
“There is no lie in what you say,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“你这话没有一个字是假的。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Is it not a queer thing,” said the King of Leinster, “that I should have what you want with all your soul, and you should have what I want with all my heart?”

===Chinese===
“你说奇不奇，”伦斯特王说，“你一心一意想要的，偏偏在我手里；我一心一意想要的，偏偏在你手里？”

===Original===
“Queer indeed,” said Mongan, “but what is it that you do want?”

===Chinese===
“确实奇，”蒙根说，“不过，你想要的究竟是什么？”

===Original===
“Duv Laca, of course,” said the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
“自然是杜夫·拉卡。”伦斯特王说。

===Original===
“Do you mean,” said Mongan, “that you would exchange this herd of fifty pure white cows having red ears﻿—”

===Chinese===
“你的意思是说，”蒙根道，“你肯拿这五十头红耳纯白牛来换——”

===Original===
“And their fifty calves,” said the King of Leinster﻿—

===Chinese===
“连它们的五十头牛犊，”伦斯特王道——

===Original===
“For Duv Laca, or for any woman in the world?”

===Chinese===
“换杜夫·拉卡，或者换世上任何一个女人？”

===Original===
“I would,” cried the King of Leinster, and he thumped his knee as he said it.

===Chinese===
“我肯！”伦斯特王喊道，说着一拳捶在自己膝盖上。

===Original===
“Done,” roared Mongan, and the two kings shook hands on the bargain.

===Chinese===
“成交！”蒙根大吼一声。两位王为这桩交易握了握手。

===Original===
Mongan then called some of his own people, and before any more words could be said and before any alteration could be made, he set his men behind the cows and marched home with them to Ulster.

===Chinese===
蒙根随即唤来几名自己人，趁着再多的话来不及说、再多的变故来不及生，便叫人赶起牛群在前，浩浩荡荡回阿尔斯特去了。

### XI / XI

===Original===
Duv Laca wanted to know where the cows came from, and Mongan told her that the King of Leinster had given them to him. She fell in love with them as Mongan had done, but there was nobody in the world could have avoided loving those cows: such cows they were! such wonders! Mongan and Duv Laca used to play chess together, and then they would go out together to look at the cows, and then they would go in together and would talk to each other about the cows. Everything they did they did together, for they loved to be with each other.

===Chinese===
杜夫·拉卡想知道这些牛是哪里来的，蒙根告诉她，是伦斯特王送的。她同蒙根一样爱上了这些牛——其实换了世上谁也免不了爱上它们：那样的牛啊！那样的奇珍啊！蒙根和杜夫·拉卡常在一处下棋，下罢棋便一同出去看牛，看罢牛又一同进屋，彼此谈的还是牛。他们做什么都在一起，因为他们喜欢彼此相守。

===Original===
However, a change came.

===Chinese===
然而，变故来了。

===Original===
One morning a great noise of voices and trampling of horses and rattle of armour came about the palace. Mongan looked from the window.

===Chinese===
一天早晨，宫外响起一片喧哗，马蹄杂沓，甲胄铿锵。蒙根从窗口望去。

===Original===
“Who is coming?” asked Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
“谁来了？”杜夫·拉卡问。

===Original===
But he did not answer her.

===Chinese===
可他没有答话。

===Original===
“The noise must announce the visit of a king,” Duv Laca continued.

===Chinese===
“这样大的排场，来的必是一位王。”杜夫·拉卡又说。

===Original===
But Mongan did not say a word.

===Chinese===
可蒙根一言不发。

===Original===
Duv Laca then went to the window.

===Chinese===
杜夫·拉卡便走到窗前。

===Original===
“Who is that king?” she asked.

===Chinese===
“那是哪位王？”她问。

===Original===
And her husband replied to her then.

===Chinese===
丈夫这才答她。

===Original===
“That is the King of Leinster,” said he mournfully.

===Chinese===
“那是伦斯特王。”他惨然道。

===Original===
“Well,” said Duv Laca surprised, “is he not welcome?”

===Chinese===
“哦，”杜夫·拉卡吃了一惊，“难道不欢迎他么？”

===Original===
“He is welcome indeed,” said Mongan lamentably.

===Chinese===
“欢迎自然欢迎。”蒙根凄然道。

===Original===
“Let us go out and welcome him properly,” Duv Laca suggested.

===Chinese===
“那我们出去好好迎一迎他吧。”杜夫·拉卡提议。

===Original===
“Let us not go near him at all,” said Mongan, “for he is coming to complete his bargain.”

===Chinese===
“我们还是压根儿别靠近他，”蒙根说，“因为他是来完成他的交易来了。”

===Original===
“What bargain are you talking about?” Duv Laca asked.

===Chinese===
“你说的是什么交易？”杜夫·拉卡问。

===Original===
But Mongan would not answer that.

===Chinese===
可蒙根不肯回答这个。

===Original===
“Let us go out,” said he, “for we must go out.”

===Chinese===
“出去吧，”他说，“不能不出去。”

===Original===
Mongan and Duv Laca went out then and welcomed the King of Leinster. They brought him and his chief men into the palace, and water was brought for their baths, and rooms were appointed for them, and everything was done that should be done for guests.

===Chinese===
蒙根和杜夫·拉卡随即出迎伦斯特王。他们请他和他的随员入宫，备水沐浴，拨屋安置；宾客该享的礼数，一样不缺。

===Original===
That night there was a feast, and after the feast there was a banquet, and all through the feast and the banquet the King of Leinster stared at Duv Laca with joy, and sometimes his breast was delivered of great sighs, and at times he moved as though in perturbation of spirit and mental agony.

===Chinese===
当夜设宴，宴罢又开华筵。宴席也罢，华筵也罢，伦斯特王自始至终喜滋滋地盯住杜夫·拉卡，胸中不时娩出几声长叹，有时又坐立不安，仿佛魂灵震荡、五内俱焚。

===Original===
“There is something wrong with the King of Leinster,” Duv Laca whispered.

===Chinese===
“伦斯特王有点不对劲儿。”杜夫·拉卡悄声道。

===Original===
“I don’t care if there is,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“不对劲就不对劲吧，我不在乎。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“You must ask what he wants.”

===Chinese===
“你必须问问他想要什么。”

===Original===
“But I don’t want to know it,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“可我并不想知道。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Nevertheless, you musk ask him,” she insisted.

===Chinese===
“不管怎样，你非问不可。”她执意道。

===Original===
So Mongan did ask him, and it was in a melancholy voice that he asked it.

===Chinese===
蒙根只好问了，问的时候声音里透着凄凉。

===Original===
“Do you want anything?” said he to the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
“您可有什么想要的？”他向伦斯特王道。

===Original===
“I do indeed,” said Branduv.

===Chinese===
“确实有。”布兰杜夫说。

===Original===
“If it is in Ulster I will get it for you,” said Mongan mournfully.

===Chinese===
“只要阿尔斯特有的，我都给您办来。”蒙根惨然道。

===Original===
“It is in Ulster,” said Branduv.

===Chinese===
“就在阿尔斯特。”布兰杜夫说。

===Original===
Mongan did not want to say anything more then, but the King of Leinster was so intent and everybody else was listening and Duv Laca was nudging his arm, so he said:

===Chinese===
蒙根本不想再多说一个字，可伦斯特王那般急切，满座的人都在侧耳听，杜夫·拉卡又一劲捅他的手臂，他便开口道：

===Original===
“What is it that you do want?”

===Chinese===
“您想要的究竟是什么？”

===Original===
“I want Duv Laca.”

===Chinese===
“我要杜夫·拉卡。”

===Original===
“I want her too,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“我也想要她。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“You made your bargain,” said the King of Leinster, “my cows and their calves for your Duv Laca, and the man that makes a bargain keeps a bargain.”

===Chinese===
“是您亲手立的契，”伦斯特王说，“我的牛连同牛犊，换您的杜夫·拉卡；立约的人，就要守约。”

===Original===
“I never before heard,” said Mongan, “of a man giving away his own wife.”

===Chinese===
“男人亲手送掉自己妻子的事，”蒙根说，“我还从来没有听说过。”

===Original===
“Even if you never heard of it before, you must do it now,” said Duv Laca, “for honour is longer than life.”

===Chinese===
“就算你从前没听说过，如今也非做不可，”杜夫·拉卡说，“因为荣誉比生命更长。”

===Original===
Mongan became angry when Duv Laca said that. His face went red as a sunset, and the veins swelled in his neck and his forehead.

===Chinese===
杜夫·拉卡这话一出，蒙根动了怒。他的脸红得像落日，脖颈上、额角上的青筋根根绽起。

===Original===
“Do you say that?” he cried to Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
“你当真这样说？”他冲着杜夫·拉卡喝道。

===Original===
“I do,” said Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
“当真。”杜夫·拉卡说。

===Original===
“Let the King of Leinster take her,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“那就让伦斯特王带她走。”蒙根说。

### XII / XII

===Original===
Duv Laca and the King of Leinster went apart then to speak together, and the eye of the king seemed to be as big as a plate, so fevered was it and so enlarged and inflamed by the look of Duv Laca. He was so confounded with joy also that his words got mixed up with his teeth, and Duv Laca did not know exactly what it was he was trying to say, and he did not seem to know himself. But at last he did say something intelligible, and this is what he said.

===Chinese===
杜夫·拉卡与伦斯特王走到一旁说话。王的一只眼睛瞪得有盘子大——让杜夫·拉卡这一看，看得又热又胀，满是血丝。他还欢喜得魂飞魄散，话都同牙齿缠在了一处，杜夫·拉卡听不真他到底想说什么，看样子他自己也不知道。但最后他总算说出了一句囫囵话，是这么说的。

===Original===
“I am a very happy man,” said he.

===Chinese===
“我真是个天底下最幸福的男人。”他说。

===Original===
“And I,” said Duv Laca, “am the happiest woman in the world.”

===Chinese===
“而我呢，”杜夫·拉卡说，“是天底下最幸福的女人。”

===Original===
“Why should you be happy?” the astonished king demanded.

===Chinese===
“你凭什么幸福？”王惊得直问。

===Original===
“Listen to me,” she said. “If you tried to take me away from this place against my own wish, one half of the men of Ulster would be dead before you got me and the other half would be badly wounded in my defence.”

===Chinese===
“你听我说，”她道，“你若违背我的心愿硬把我从这里带走，不等得手，阿尔斯特人先得死掉一半；剩下那一半，为护我也会个个带重伤。”

===Original===
“A bargain is a bargain,” the King of Leinster began.

===Chinese===
“立了约就要守约。”伦斯特王开口道。

===Original===
“But,” she continued, “they will not prevent my going away, for they all know that I have been in love with you for ages.”

===Chinese===
“可是，”她接着说，“他们不会拦我走，因为他们全知道，我爱上你已经好多好多年了。”

===Original===
“What have you been in with me for ages?” said the amazed king.

===Chinese===
“你跟我什么了好多好多年？”王愕然道。

===Original===
“In love with you,” replied Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
“爱上了你。”杜夫·拉卡答道。

===Original===
“This is news,” said the king, “and it is good news.”

===Chinese===
“这可是新闻，”王说，“而且是好消息。”

===Original===
“But, by my word,” said Duv Laca, “I will not go with you unless you grant me a boon.”

===Chinese===
“不过，凭我的名誉起誓，”杜夫·拉卡说，“你不答应我一件事，我就不跟你走。”

===Original===
“All that I have,” cried Branduv, “and all that every-body has.”

===Chinese===
“我有的全给你，”布兰杜夫喊道，“人人有的都给你。”

===Original===
“And you must pass your word and pledge your word that you will do what I ask.”

===Chinese===
“你还必须许下诺言、立下信誓，保证照我的要求去做。”

===Original===
“I pass it and pledge it,” cried the joyful king.

===Chinese===
“我许，我立！”欢喜的王喊道。

===Original===
“Then,” said Duv Laca, “this is what I bind on you.”

===Chinese===
“那么，”杜夫·拉卡说，“我要给你缚上的就是这个。”

===Original===
“Light the yolk!” he cried.

===Chinese===
“点亮蛋黄！”他叫道。

===Original===
“Until one year is up and out you are not to pass the night in any house that I am in.”

===Chinese===
“从今往后，一年期满之前，凡我宿在哪座屋子，你就不得在那屋里过夜。”

===Original===
“By my head and hand!” Branduv stammered.

===Chinese===
“凭我的头和手起誓！”布兰杜夫结结巴巴地说。

===Original===
“And if you come into a house where I am during the time and term of that year, you are not to sit down in the chair that I am sitting in.”

===Chinese===
“而且在这一年期限之内，你若走进我所在的屋子，不得坐进我正坐着的那把椅子。”

===Original===
“Heavy is my doom!” he groaned.

===Chinese===
“我的劫数好沉重啊！”他呻吟道。

===Original===
“But,” said Duv Laca, “if I am sitting in a chair or a seat you are to sit in a chair that is over against me and opposite to me and at a distance from me.”

===Chinese===
“不过，”杜夫·拉卡说，“我坐椅子也好，坐凳子也好，你要坐的那把椅子，必须同我面对着面、正对着，还得隔开一段距离。”

===Original===
“Alas!” said the king, and he smote his hands together, and then he beat them on his head, and then he looked at them and at everything about, and he could not tell what anything was or where anything was, for his mind was clouded and his wits had gone astray.

===Chinese===
“唉！”王叫了一声，双掌互击，又抬手拍在自己头上，然后呆呆望着自己的手，望着四下的一切——什么都不认得了，什么都不知在哪儿了，因为他的心已蒙上乌云，神志也迷了路。

===Original===
“Why do you bind these woes on me?” he pleaded.

===Chinese===
“你为何把这些苦楚一桩桩缚在我身上？”他哀告道。

===Original===
“I wish to find out if you truly love me.”

===Chinese===
“我要看看你是不是真爱我。”

===Original===
“But I do,” said the king. “I love you madly and dearly, and with all my faculties and members.”

===Chinese===
“我自然是爱的，”王说，“我爱你爱得发狂，爱得深切，浑身上下、里里外外，每一分官能每一块骨头都爱。”

===Original===
“That is the way I love you,” said Duv Laca. “We shall have a notable year of courtship and joy. And let us go now,” she continued, “for I am impatient to be with you.”

===Chinese===
“我也正是这样爱你的。”杜夫·拉卡说，“我们将度过一个大可载入史册的求爱之年、欢乐之年。这就走吧，”她接着说，“我已等不及要同你在一起了。”

===Original===
“Alas!” said Branduv, as he followed her. “Alas, alas!” said the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
“唉！”布兰杜夫跟着她走，叹道。“唉，唉！”伦斯特王说。

### XIII / XIII

===Original===
“I think,” said the Flame Lady, “that whoever lost that woman had no reason to be sad.”

===Chinese===
“照我想，”火焰夫人说，“失去那个女人的男人，犯不上伤心。”

===Original===
Mongan took her chin in his hand and kissed her lips.

===Chinese===
蒙根托起她的下巴，吻了吻她的唇。

===Original===
“All that you say is lovely, for you are lovely,” said he, “and you are my delight and the joy of the world.”

===Chinese===
“你说的一切都可爱，因为你可爱，”他说，“你是我的欢喜，是人间的至乐。”

===Original===
Then the attendants brought him wine, and he drank so joyously of that and so deeply, that those who observed him thought he would surely burst and drown them. But he laughed loudly and with enormous delight, until the vessels of gold and silver and bronze chimed mellowly to his peal and the rafters of the house went creaking.

===Chinese===
侍者随即奉上美酒。他喝得那样欢畅、那样深沉，旁观的人都担心他马上要炸开，把他们统统淹了。可他放声大笑，笑得酣畅淋漓，笑得金银铜的器皿应着笑声铮铮和鸣，笑得屋梁咯吱作响。

---

===Original===
For (said he), Mongan loved Duv Laca of the White Hand better than he loved his life, better than he loved his honour. The kingdoms of the world did not weigh with him beside the string of her shoe. He would not look at a sunset if he could see her. He would not listen to a harp if he could hear her speak, for she was the delight of ages, the gem of time, and the wonder of the world till Doom.

===Chinese===
因为（他说），蒙根爱白手杜夫·拉卡，胜过爱自己的性命，胜过爱自己的荣誉。同她的一根鞋带相比，普天下的王国都算不得分量。若能看她，他便不看落日；若能听她说话，他便不听竖琴——因为她是历世历代的欢喜，是时光的宝石，直到劫数临头，都是世间的奇迹。

===Original===
She went to Leinster with the king of that country, and when she had gone Mongan fell grievously sick, so that it did not seem he could ever recover again; and he began to waste and wither, and he began to look like a skeleton, and a bony structure, and a misery.

===Chinese===
她随那一方的王去了伦斯特。她一走，蒙根便害起重病来，病得仿佛再也好不了了；他日渐消瘦，日渐枯槁，渐渐瞧着像一副骷髅、一把骨头架子、一个苦人儿了。

===Original===
Now this also must be known.

===Chinese===
如今这事也须知道。

===Original===
Duv Laca had a young attendant, who was her foster-sister as well as her servant, and on the day that she got married to Mongan, her attendant was married to mac an Dáv, who was servant and foster-brother to Mongan. When Duv Laca went away with the King of Leinster, her servant, mac an Dáv’s wife, went with her, so there were two wifeless men in Ulster at that time, namely, Mongan the king and mac an Dáv his servant.

===Chinese===
杜夫·拉卡有个年轻侍女，既是她的奴仆，又是她的义妹；杜夫·拉卡嫁给蒙根的那一天，这侍女嫁给了麦克·安·达夫——他是蒙根的仆人，也是蒙根的义弟。杜夫·拉卡随伦斯特王走时，她的侍女、麦克·安·达夫的妻子，也跟着走了。于是那时的阿尔斯特便有两个没了妻子的男人：一个是王蒙根，一个是他的仆人麦克·安·达夫。

===Original===
One day as Mongan sat in the sun, brooding lamentably on his fate, mac an Dáv came to him.

===Chinese===
一天，蒙根坐在太阳底下，凄凄惶惶地想着自己的命，麦克·安·达夫来了。

===Original===
“How are things with you, master?” asked Mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“主人，您近来怎么样？”麦克·安·达夫问。

===Original===
“Bad,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“糟透了。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“It was a poor day brought you off with Manannán to the Land of Promise,” said his servant.

===Chinese===
“当年玛纳南把您带去应许之地，那一天真是选坏了。”他的仆人说。

===Original===
“Why should you think that?” inquired Mongan.

===Chinese===
“这怎么讲？”蒙根问。

===Original===
“Because,” said mac an Dáv, “you learned nothing in the Land of Promise except how to eat a lot of food and how to do nothing in a deal of time.”

===Chinese===
“因为，”麦克·安·达夫说，“您在应许之地什么也没学会，只学会了怎么吞下许多吃食，怎么在老长的时间里一件事也不干。”

===Original===
“What business is it of yours?” said Mongan angrily.

===Chinese===
“关你什么事？”蒙根怒道。

===Original===
“It is my business surely,” said mac an Dáv, “for my wife has gone off to Leinster with your wife, and she wouldn’t have gone if you hadn’t made a bet and a bargain with that accursed king.”

===Chinese===
“怎么不关我的事，”麦克·安·达夫说，“我的老婆跟着您的老婆跑伦斯特去了；您要不同那个遭瘟的王打赌立契，她才不会走呢。”

===Original===
Mac an Dáv began to weep then.

===Chinese===
麦克·安·达夫哭了起来。

===Original===
“I didn’t make a bargain with any king,” said he, “and yet my wife has gone away with one, and it’s all because of you.”

===Chinese===
“我可是没同哪个王立过契，”他说，“我的老婆却跟了一个王跑了，全都怪您。”

===Original===
“There is no one sorrier for you than I am,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“天下再没有比我更替你难过的人了。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“There is indeed,” said mac an Dáv, “for I am sorrier myself.”

===Chinese===
“怎么没有，”麦克·安·达夫说，“我自己就比您更替我难过。”

===Original===
Mongan roused himself then.

===Chinese===
蒙根这才振作起来。

===Original===
“You have a claim on me truly,” said he, “and I will not have anyone with a claim on me that is not satisfied. Go,” he said to mac an Dáv, “to that fairy place we both know of. You remember the baskets I left there with the sod from Ireland in one and the sod from Scotland in the other; bring me the baskets and sods.”

===Chinese===
“你在我身上确实有一份索求，”他说，“凡对我有所索求的人，我不叫他不得满足。去，”他对麦克·安·达夫说，“去咱们都晓得的那处仙地。你还记得我留在那儿的两只篮子吧——一只装着爱尔兰的草皮，一只装着苏格兰（Scotland）的草皮；把篮子和草皮给我取来。”

===Original===
“Tell me the why of this?” said his servant.

===Chinese===
“这是图什么？”他的仆人问。

===Original===
“The King of Leinster will ask his wizards what I am doing, and this is what I will be doing. I will get on your back with a foot in each of the baskets, and when Branduv asks the wizards where I am they will tell him that I have one leg in Ireland and one leg in Scotland, and as long as they tell him that he will think he need not bother himself about me, and we will go into Leinster that way.”

===Chinese===
“伦斯特王必会叫他的巫师占算我在做什么，而我做的将是这么一件事：我骑上你的背，两只脚各踩一只篮子。布兰杜夫问巫师我在哪儿，巫师便告诉他——我一条腿站在爱尔兰，一条腿站在苏格兰。只要他们这么回他，他就以为犯不着再为我费心，咱们便趁这条路进伦斯特去。”

===Original===
“No bad way either,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“路子倒也不坏。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
They set out then.

===Chinese===
两人随即动身。

### XIV / XIV

===Original===
It was a long, uneasy journey, for although mac an Dáv was of stout heart and goodwill, yet no man can carry another on his back from Ulster to Leinster and go quick. Still, if you keep on driving a pig or a story they will get at last to where you wish them to go, and the man who continues putting one foot in front of the other will leave his home behind, and will come at last to the edge of the sea and the end of the world.

===Chinese===
那是一趟漫长难熬的旅程。麦克·安·达夫虽说心雄胆壮、满心愿意，可谁背着另一个人从阿尔斯特走到伦斯特，也快不起来。话虽如此，猪也好，故事也好，只要一路赶下去，终究能赶到你要它去的地方；一个人只要一步接一步不停脚，家便落在身后，末了总能走到大海边上、天地尽头。

===Original===
When they reached Leinster the feast of Moy Lifé was being held, and they pushed on by forced marches and long stages so as to be in time, and thus they came to the Moy of Cell Camain, and they mixed with the crowd that were going to the feast.

===Chinese===
到伦斯特时，正赶上莫伊·利菲（Moy Lifé）大节开办。两人兼程赶路，一站长似一站，总算赶上了时辰，来到凯尔·卡曼（Cell Camain）草原，混进赴会的人流里。

===Original===
A great and joyous concourse of people streamed about them. There were young men and young girls, and when these were not holding each other’s hands it was because their arms were round each other’s necks. There were old, lusty women going by, and when these were not talking together it was because their mouths were mutually filled with apples and meat-pies. There were young warriors with mantles of green and purple and red flying behind them on the breeze, and when these were not looking disdainfully on older soldiers it was because the older soldiers happened at the moment to be looking at them. There were old warriors with yard-long beards flying behind their shoulders llke wisps of hay, and when these were not nursing a broken arm or a cracked skull, it was because they were nursing wounds in their stomachs or their legs. There were troops of young women who giggled as long as their breaths lasted and beamed when it gave out. Bands of boys who whispered mysteriously together and pointed with their fingers in every direction at once, and would suddenly begin to run like a herd of stampeded horses. There were men with carts full of roasted meats. Women with little vats full of mead, and others carrying milk and beer. Folk of both sorts with towers swaying on their heads, and they dripping with honey. Children having baskets piled with red apples, and old women who peddled shellfish and boiled lobsters. There were people who sold twenty kinds of bread, with butter thrown in. Sellers of onions and cheese, and others who supplied spare bits of armour, odd scabbards, spear handles, breastplate-laces. People who cut your hair or told your fortune or gave you a hot bath in a pot. Others who put a shoe on your horse or a piece of embroidery on your mantle; and others, again, who took stains off your sword or dyed your fingernails or sold you a hound.

===Chinese===
一股欢天喜地的人潮在他们身边涌流。有青年小伙，有妙龄姑娘——两人不手拉手的时候，必是胳膊互相搂着脖子。有精气神十足的老妇人走过——她们不交谈的时候，必是嘴里彼此塞满了苹果和肉饼。有年轻的武士，绿、紫、红的斗篷在风里朝身后翻飞——他们不睥睨老战士的时候，必是老战士们恰好在看他们。有老的武士，尺来长的大胡子像干草束似的在肩后飘——他们不抚着断臂、不捂着裂开的头骨时，必是在捂肚子上的或腿上的伤。有一队一队的少妇，气力不断便吃吃笑个不停，气力接不上，便改作满脸春风。有一帮一帮的男孩，神神秘秘地交头接耳，手指头同时往四面八方乱指，冷不丁撒腿就跑，像一群体惊的马。有推着车卖烤肉的汉子；有拎着小桶卖蜂蜜酒的妇人，还有提着牛奶和啤酒的；有男有女，头上顶着摇摇晃晃的高塔，那塔淌着蜜，直往下滴；有挎着一篮篮红苹果的孩童；有叫卖贝肉煮龙虾的老妪。有卖二十种面包的，黄油还白送；有卖葱头奶酪的；还有兜售零碎甲胄、单只刀鞘、枪杆、胸甲绳带的。有替你理发、替你算命、支起大锅给你洗热水澡的；有给马钉掌、往斗篷上绣花的；还有替宝剑除锈斑、给你染指甲、卖你一条猎犬的。

===Original===
It was a great and joyous gathering that was going to the feast.

===Chinese===
赴会的就是这样一支浩浩荡荡、欢欢喜喜的人马。

===Original===
Mongan and his servant sat against a grassy hedge by the roadside and watched the multitude streaming past.

===Chinese===
蒙根和仆人坐在路旁一道青草篱下，看着人流涌过。

===Original===
Just then Mongan glanced to the right whence the people were coming. Then he pulled the hood of his cloak over his ears and over his brow.

===Chinese===
正这时，蒙根朝右边人流来处瞥了一眼，随即把斗篷的风帽拉下来，罩住耳朵，罩住额头。

===Original===
“Alas!” said he in a deep and anguished voice.

===Chinese===
“唉！”他声音沉痛地说。

===Original===
Mac an Dáv turned to him.

===Chinese===
麦克·安·达夫转过脸来。

===Original===
“Is it a pain in your stomach, master?”

===Chinese===
“主人，是肚子疼吗？”

===Original===
“It is not,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“不是。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Well, what made you make that brutal and belching noise?”

===Chinese===
“那您弄出那声又凶又像打嗝的动静，是做什么？”

===Original===
“It was a sigh I gave,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“我那是叹了口气。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Whatever it was,” said mac an Dáv, “what was it?”

===Chinese===
“不管它是什么，”麦克·安·达夫说，“它到底是什么呀？”

===Original===
“Look down the road on this side and tell me who is coming,” said his master.

===Chinese===
“顺着这边的路望过去，告诉我谁来啦。”他的主人说。

===Original===
“It is a lord with his troop.”

===Chinese===
“是一位大人，带着他的人马。”

===Original===
“It is the King of Leinster,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“那是伦斯特王。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“The man,” said mac an Dáv in a tone of great pity, “the man that took away your wife! And,” he roared in a voice of extraordinary savagery, “the man that took away my wife into the bargain, and she not in the bargain.”

===Chinese===
“那人啊，”麦克·安·达夫用满怀怜悯的声调说，“把您的老婆带走的那人！还有——”他陡然吼起来，声音凶得吓人，“把我的老婆也捎带带走的那人——我的老婆可不在契约里！”

===Original===
“Hush,” said Mongan, for a man who heard his shout stopped to tie a sandie, or to listen.

===Chinese===
“嘘，”蒙根说，因为有一个听见了吼声的人停下来系鞋带——要不就是停下来听动静。

===Original===
“Master,” said mac an Dáv as the troop drew abreast and moved past.

===Chinese===
“主人，”人马齐肩而过时，麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“What is it, my good friend?”

===Chinese===
“什么事，我的好朋友？”

===Original===
“Let me throw a little small piece of a rock at the King of Leinster.”

===Chinese===
“容我朝伦斯特王扔一小块碎石头吧。”

===Original===
“I will not.”

===Chinese===
“不行。”

===Original===
“A little bit only, a small bit about twice the size of my head.”

===Chinese===
“就一小块，一小块大约有我脑袋两倍大的。”

===Original===
“I will not let you,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“不许扔。”蒙根说。

===Original===
When the king had gone by mac an Dáv groaned a deep and dejected groan.

===Chinese===
王走远之后，麦克·安·达夫发出一声深沉沮丧的呻吟。

===Original===
“*Ocón!*” said he. “*Ocón-ío-go-deó!*” said he.

===Chinese===
“呜呼！”他说。“呜呼哀哉，直到永远！”他说。

===Original===
The man who had tied his sandal said then:

===Chinese===
那个系鞋带的人说话了：

===Original===
“Are you in pain, honest man?”

===Chinese===
“老哥，你身上疼吗？”

===Original===
“I am not in pain,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“不疼。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“Well, what was it that knocked a howl out of you like the yelp of a sick dog, honest man?”

===Chinese===
“那你怪叫那一声，叫得像条病狗似的，是怎么回事，老哥？”

===Original===
“Go away,” said mac an Dáv, “go away, you flat-faced, nosey person.”

===Chinese===
“走开，”麦克·安·达夫说，“走开，你这个扁脸的多事鬼。”

===Original===
“There is no politeness left in this country,” said the stranger, and he went away to a certain distance, and from thence he threw a stone at mac an Dáv’s nose, and hit it.

===Chinese===
“这个国家是一点礼数都不剩了。”那人说。他走开几步，远远地朝麦克·安·达夫的鼻子扔了一块石头，正中鼻梁。

### XV / XV

===Original===
The road was now not so crowded as it had been. Minutes would pass and only a few travellers would come, and minutes more would go when nobody was in sight at all.

===Chinese===
这时路上不似方才拥挤了。几分钟过去，才来三两个行客；再过几分钟，连一个人影也望不见。

===Original===
Then two men came down the road: they were clerics.

===Chinese===
后来路上下来两个人：是两个教士。

===Original===
“I never saw that kind of uniform before,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“这种号衣我从来没见过。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“Even if you didn’t,” said Mongan, “there are plenty of them about. They are men that don’t believe in our gods,” said he.

===Chinese===
“你就是没见过，”蒙根说，“如今这种人也遍地都是了。他们是不信咱们那些神的人。”他说。

===Original===
“Do they not, indeed?” said mac an Dáv. “The rascals!” said he. “What, what would Manannán say to that?”

===Chinese===
“竟然连咱们的神也不信？”麦克·安·达夫说，“好一群泼皮！”他说，“你说，你说，玛纳南听了会怎么想？”

===Original===
“The one in front carrying the big book is Tibraidè. He is the priest of Cell Camain, and he is the chief of those two.”

===Chinese===
“前头抱大书的那个叫蒂布拉德（Tibraidè）。他是凯尔·卡曼的司铎，两人里他是个头儿。”

===Original===
“Indeed, and indeed!” said mac an Dáv. “The one behind must be his servant, for he has a load on his back.”

===Chinese===
“真的，真的！”麦克·安·达夫说，“后头那个准是他的仆人，他背上驮着包袱呢。”

===Original===
The priests were reading their offices, and mac an Dáv marvelled at that.

===Chinese===
两位神父正念着日课，麦克·安·达夫看着啧啧称奇。

===Original===
“What is it they are doing?” said he.

===Chinese===
“他们这是干什么呢？”他说。

===Original===
“They are reading.”

===Chinese===
“他们在读书。”

===Original===
“Indeed, and indeed they are,” said mac an Dáv. “I can’t make out a word of the language except that the man behind says amen, amen, every time the man in front puts a grunt out of him. And they don’t like our gods at all!” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“真的，真的，是在读书，”麦克·安·达夫说，“可那话我一个字也听不懂，只听懂一件：前头那人每哼出一声，后头那人就应一声阿门、阿门。再说，他们压根儿不喜欢咱们的神！”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“They do not,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“是不喜欢。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Play a trick on them, master,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“主人，捉弄捉弄他们吧。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
Mongan agreed to play a trick on the priests.

===Chinese===
蒙根依了，准备捉弄两位神父。

===Original===
He looked at them hard for a minute, and then he waved his hand at them.

===Chinese===
他盯住他们看了足足一分钟，然后朝他们一挥手。

===Original===
The two priests stopped, and they stared straight in front of them, and then they looked at each other, and then they looked at the sky. The clerk began to bless himself, and then Tibraidè began to bless himself, and after that they didn’t know what to do. For where there had been a road with hedges on each side and fields stretching beyond them, there was now no road, no hedge, no field; but there was a great broad river sweeping across their path; a mighty tumble of yellowy-brown waters, very swift, very savage; churning and billowing and jockeying among rough boulders and islands of stone. It was a water of villainous depth and of detestable wetness; of ugly hurrying and of desolate cavernous sound. At a little to their right there was a thin uncomely bridge that waggled across the torrent.

===Chinese===
两位神父停住了。他们直勾勾望着前方，又面面相觑，又仰头望天。随行那位教士先在胸前画起十字，随后蒂布拉德也画起十字，画完便没了主意。因为方才还是一条两边夹着树篱、树篱外田野连绵的大路，此刻路没有了，篱没有了，田也没有了——横在他们面前的，是一条浩浩荡荡的大河：黄褐色的水倾泻翻滚，湍急，凶暴，在乱石与石岛之间翻腾、涌动、挤挤撞撞。那水歹毒地深，可憎地湿，浪头丑陋地你追我赶，声响空旷凄凉。右边不远，有一座细弱难看的桥，晃晃悠悠地跨在激流上。

===Original===
Tibraidè rubbed his eyes, and then he looked again.

===Chinese===
蒂布拉德揉了揉眼睛，再看。

===Original===
“Do you see what I see?” said he to the clerk.

===Chinese===
“你看见我看见的东西了吗？”他问那位教士。

===Original===
“I don’t know what you see,” said the clerk, “but what I see I never did see before, and I wish I did not see it now.”

===Chinese===
“我不知道你看见的是什么，”教士说，“可我看见的东西，我从前从没看见过，而且我巴不得现在也没看见。”

===Original===
“I was born in this place,” said Tibraidè, “my father was born here before me, and my grandfather was born here before him, but until this day and this minute I never saw a river here before, and I never heard of one.”

===Chinese===
“我就生在这个地方，”蒂布拉德说，“我父亲比我先生在这儿，我祖父又比我父亲先生在这儿；可直到今天这一分钟，我从没在这儿见过一条河，连听都没听说过。”

===Original===
“What will we do at all?” said the clerk. “What will we do at all?”

===Chinese===
“这可怎么是好？”教士说。“这可怎么是好啊？”

===Original===
“We will be sensible,” said Tibraidè sternly, “and we will go about our business,” said he. “If rivers fall out of the sky what has that to do with you, and if there is a river here, which there is, why, thank God, there is a bridge over it too.”

===Chinese===
“咱们要沉得住气，”蒂布拉德正色道，“该干什么干什么，”他说，“天上就是掉下河来，与你我何干？这儿既然有河——河确实有——那不结了，感谢天主，河上还架着桥呢。”

===Original===
“Would you put a toe on that bridge?” said the clerk.

===Chinese===
“那样的桥，你敢伸一个脚趾上去？”教士说。

===Original===
“What is the bridge for?” said Tibraidè.

===Chinese===
“桥不拿来走，拿来做什么？”蒂布拉德说。

===Original===
Mongan and mac an Dáv followed them.

===Chinese===
蒙根和麦克·安·达夫跟在他们后面。

===Original===
When they got to the middle of the bridge it broke under them, and they were precipitated into that boiling yellow flood.

===Chinese===
走到桥心，桥在脚下一断，两人直栽进那滚滚的黄汤里。

===Original===
Mongan snatched at the book as it fell from Tibraidès hand.

===Chinese===
书从蒂布拉德手里跌落，蒙根一把抄住。

===Original===
“Won’t you let them drown, master?” asked mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“主人，就不能让他们淹死吗？”麦克·安·达夫问。

===Original===
“No,” said Mongan, “I’ll send them a mile down the stream, and then they can come to land.”

===Chinese===
“不能，”蒙根说，“我送他们往下游漂一里，再让他们上岸。”

===Original===
Mongan then took on himself the form of Tibraidè and he turned mac an Dáv into the shape of the clerk.

===Chinese===
随后蒙根变作蒂布拉德的形貌，又把麦克·安·达夫变成那位教士的模样。

===Original===
“My head has gone bald,” said the servant in a whisper.

===Chinese===
“我的头怎么秃了。”仆人悄声说。

===Original===
“That is part of it,” replied Mongan.

===Chinese===
“变的就是这个。”蒙根答道。

===Original===
“So long as we know,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“咱们自己心里有数就行。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
They went on then to meet the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
两人便继续赶路，去见伦斯特王。

### XVI / XVI

===Original===
They met him near the place where the games were played.

===Chinese===
在赛场左近，他们迎到了他。

===Original===
“Good my soul, Tibraidè!” cried the King of Leinster, and he gave Mongan a kiss. Mongan kissed him back again.

===Chinese===
“哎呀我的天，蒂布拉德！”伦斯特王喊道，亲了蒙根一记。蒙根也回亲了他一记。

===Original===
“Amen, amen,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“阿门，阿门。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“What for?” said the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
“为何要阿门？”伦斯特王说。

===Original===
And then mac an Dáv began to sneeze, for he didn’t know what for.

===Chinese===
麦克·安·达夫随即打起喷嚏来，因为他也不知道为何要阿门。

===Original===
“It is a long time since I saw you, Tibraidè,” said the king, “but at this minute I am in great haste and hurry. Go you on before me to the fortress, and you can talk to the queen that you’ll find there, she that used to be the King of Ulster’s wife. Kevin Cochlach, my charioteer, will go with you, and I will follow you myself in a while.”

===Chinese===
“自打上一回见你，好些日子了，蒂布拉德，”王说，“可这会儿我正忙得脚不沾地。你们先替我上堡寨去，到了那儿可以同王后说说话——就是从前阿尔斯特王的那一位。我的御者凯文·科克拉克（Kevin Cochlach）陪你们同去，我过一阵子亲自赶来。”

===Original===
The King of Leinster went off then, and Mongan and his servant went with the charioteer and the people.

===Chinese===
伦斯特王自去了；蒙根主仆随着御者和众人上路。

===Original===
Mongan read away out of the book, for he found it interesting, and he did not want to talk to the charioteer, and mac an Dáv cried amen, amen, every time that Mongan took his breath. The people who were going with them said to one another that mac an Dáv was a queer kind of clerk, and that they had never seen anyone who had such a mouthful of amens.

===Chinese===
蒙根自顾自读那本书——他读出了兴致，懒得同御者搭话；蒙根每换一口气，麦克·安·达夫便应一声阿门、阿门。同行的人彼此议论，说这么个教士真是古怪，满嘴阿门的人，他们还从来没见过。

===Original===
But in a while they came to the fortress, and they got into it without any trouble, for Kevin Cochlach, the king’s charioteer, brought them in. Then they were led to the room where Duv Laca was, and as he went into that room Mongan shut his eyes, for he did not want to look at Duv Laca while other people might be looking at him.

===Chinese===
不多时到了堡寨，进寨毫无阻滞——有王的御者凯文·科克拉克领着呢。随后有人引他们去杜夫·拉卡所在的屋子。进那道门的时候蒙根闭上了眼睛，因为他不愿在旁人可能盯着他看的时候，去看杜夫·拉卡。

===Original===
“Let everybody leave this room, while I am talking to the queen,” said he; and all the attendants left the room, except one, and she wouldn’t go, for she wouldn’t leave her mistress.

===Chinese===
“我与王后说话时，屋里的人都退出去。”他说。侍女们纷纷退出，只留下一个——她不肯走，因为不肯丢下自家主母。

===Original===
Then Mongan opened his eyes and he saw Duv Laca, and he made a great bound to her and took her in his arms, and mac an Dáv made a savage and vicious and terrible jump at the attendant, and took her in his arms, and bit her ear and kissed her neck and wept down into her back.

===Chinese===
蒙根这才睁开眼。他一望见杜夫·拉卡，纵身一跃便把她搂进怀里；麦克·安·达夫也朝那侍女又凶又狠又吓人地一扑，把她搂进怀里，咬她的耳朵，亲她的脖颈，眼泪扑簌簌落在她背上。

===Original===
“Go away,” said the girl, “unhand me, villain,” said she.

===Chinese===
“走开，”那姑娘说，“放开我，你这恶棍。”她说。

===Original===
“I will not,” said mac an Dáv, “for I’m your own husband, I’m your own mac, your little mac, your macky-wac-wac.” Then the attendant gave a little squeal, and she bit him on each ear and kissed his neck and wept down into his back, and said that it wasn’t true and that it was.

===Chinese===
“我不放，”麦克·安·达夫说，“我是你自家的丈夫，你自家的麦克，你的小麦克，你的麦克乖乖。”侍女一声尖叫，也咬了他两边耳朵，亲他的脖颈，眼泪落在他背上，嘴里说这不是真的，又说是真的。

### XVII / XVII

===Original===
But they were not alone, although they thought they were. The hag that guarded the jewels was in the room. She sat hunched up against the wail, and as she looked like a bundle of rags they did not notice her. She began to speak then.

===Chinese===
可屋里并不是只有他们，虽然他们自以为如此。看守珠宝的老妪就在屋里。她缩成一团靠墙坐着，看上去就像一堆破布，谁也没留意她。这时她开口了。

===Original===
“Terrible are the things I see,” said she. “Terrible are the things I see.”

===Chinese===
“我看见的东西，可怕呀。”她说。“我看见的东西，可怕呀。”

===Original===
Mongan and his servant gave a jump of surprise, and their two wives jumped and squealed. Then Mongan puffed out his cheeks till his face looked like a bladder, and he blew a magic breath at the hag, so that she seemed to be surrounded by a fog, and when she looked through that breath everything seemed to be different to what she had thought. Then she began to beg everybody’s pardon.

===Chinese===
蒙根主仆惊得跳起，他们俩的妻子也惊叫着蹦起来。蒙根鼓起腮帮，把脸憋成一只尿脬，朝老妪吹出一口法气；老妪顿时像被一团雾围住，透过那口气再看，样样东西都同她方才想的不一样了。她便赶忙向满屋的人告罪。

===Original===
“I had an evil vision,” said she, “I saw crossways. How sad it is that I should begin to see the sort of things I thought I saw.”

===Chinese===
“我见了邪祟的幻象，”她说，“我看岔了。人到了我这个岁数，竟开始看见我自以为看见的那类东西，多么可悲呀。”

===Original===
“Sit in this chair, mother,” said Mongan, “and tell me what you thought you saw,” and he slipped a spike under her, and mac an Dáv pushed her into the seat, and she died on the spike.

===Chinese===
“老婆婆，坐这把椅子，”蒙根说，“给我讲讲你自以为看见了什么。”他把一根尖桩悄悄安在椅上，麦克·安·达夫把她按进椅子，她便死在了尖桩上。

===Original===
Just then there came a knocking at the door. Mac an Dáv opened it, and there was Tibraidè, standing outside, and twenty-nine of his men were with him, and they were all laughing.

===Chinese===
正这时有人敲门。麦克·安·达夫开门一看，蒂布拉德站在外面，身后跟着他的二十九个人，个个笑呵呵的。

===Original===
“A mile was not half enough,” said mac an Dáv reproachfully.

===Chinese===
“一里地连一半都不够。”麦克·安·达夫责怪道。

===Original===
The Chamberlain of the fortress pushed into the room and he stared from one Tibraidè to the other.

===Chinese===
堡寨的内侍总管挤进屋来，眼珠子从这个蒂布拉德骨碌到那个蒂布拉德。

===Original===
“This is a fine growing year,” said he. “There never was a year when Tibraidès were as plentiful as they are this year. There is a Tibraidè outside and a Tibraidè inside, and who knows but there are some more of them under the bed. The place is crawling with them,” said he.

===Chinese===
“今年年成真好，”他说，“蒂布拉德从来没像今年这样丰产。外头一个蒂布拉德，里头一个蒂布拉德，谁知道床底下是不是还窝着几个。这地方遍地都是他们了。”他说。

===Original===
Mongan pointed at Tibraidè.

===Chinese===
蒙根一指蒂布拉德。

===Original===
“Don’t you know who that is?” he cried.

===Chinese===
“你不知道那是谁吗？”他喊道。

===Original===
“I know who he says he is,” said the Chamberlain.

===Chinese===
“他自称是谁，我知道。”总管说。

===Original===
“Well, he is Mongan,” said Mongan, “and these twenty-nine men are twenty-nine of his nobles from Ulster.”

===Chinese===
“告诉你吧，他是蒙根，”蒙根说，“这二十九个人，是他从阿尔斯特带来的二十九名贵族。”

===Original===
At that news the men of the household picked up clubs and cudgels and every kind of thing that was near, and made a violent and woeful attack on Tibraidè’s men The King of Leinster came in then, and when he was told Tibraidè was Mongan he attacked them as well, and it was with difficulty that Tibraidè got away to Cell Camain with nine of his men and they all wounded.

===Chinese===
这消息一出，家丁们抄起棍棒，抄起手边一切顺手的物件，朝蒂布拉德的人劈头盖脸打去，打得又狠又惨。伦斯特王随后进来，一听人说这蒂布拉德是蒙根，也挥拳加入围攻。蒂布拉德好不容易才脱身逃回凯尔·卡曼，身边只剩九个人，个个带伤。

===Original===
The King of Leinster came back then. He went to Duv Laca’s room.

===Chinese===
伦斯特王回过身来，径直去了杜夫·拉卡的房间。

===Original===
“Where is Tibraidè?” said he.

===Chinese===
“蒂布拉德呢？”他说。

===Original===
“It wasn’t Tibraidè was here,” said the hag who was still sitting on the spike, and was not half dead, “it was Mongan.”

===Chinese===
“来的才不是蒂布拉德，”那还坐在尖桩上、连一半都没死成的老妪说，“来的是蒙根。”

===Original===
“Why did you let him near you?” said the king to Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
“你怎么容他挨近你？”王问杜夫·拉卡。

===Original===
“There is no one has a better right to be near me than Mongan has,” said Duv Laca, “he is my own husband,” said she.

===Chinese===
“这世上谁挨近我的名分，也大不过蒙根的，”杜夫·拉卡说，“他是我的亲丈夫。”她说。

===Original===
And then the king cried out in dismay:

===Chinese===
王顿时懊悔得大叫：

===Original===
“I have beaten Tibraidè’s people.”

===Chinese===
“我打的是蒂布拉德的人啊！”

===Original===
He rushed from the room.

===Chinese===
他冲出房去。

===Original===
“Send for Tibraidè till I apologise,” he cried. “Tell him it was all a mistake. Tell him it was Mongan.”

===Chinese===
“快去请蒂布拉德，容我赔罪，”他喊道，“告诉他全是弄错了。告诉他那是蒙根。”

### XVIII / XVIII

===Original===
Mongan and his servant went home, and (for what pleasure is greater than that of memory exercised in conversation?) for a time the feeling of an adventure well accomplished kept him in some contentment. But at the end of a time that pleasure was worn out, and Mongan grew at first dispirited and then sullen, and after that as ill as he had been on the previous occasion. For he could not forget Duv Laca of the White Hand, and he could not remember her without longing and despair.

===Chinese===
蒙根主仆回了家。（谈话里翻检旧日的记忆，天下还有比这更大的乐事吗？）有一阵子，大功告成的快意还让他存着几分满足。可过了一段时日，这点乐子也磨光了，蒙根先是打不起精神，继而闷闷不乐，末了又病得同上一回一样沉。因为白手杜夫·拉卡，他忘不掉；一想起她，渴慕与绝望便一齐涌上心头。

===Original===
It was in the illness which comes from longing and despair that he sat one day looking on a world that was black although the sun shone, and that was lean and unwholesome although autumn fruits were heavy on the earth and the joys of harvest were about him.

===Chinese===
就在这种由渴慕与绝望生出的病症里，有一天他坐着，望着一个世界——太阳明明照着，那世界却是黑的；秋果沉沉压满大地，丰收的欢悦近在身旁，那世界却又瘦瘠又败坏。

===Original===
“Winter is in my heart,” quoth he, “and I am cold already.”

===Chinese===
“我的心里是冬天，”他叹道，“我已经冷了。”

===Original===
He thought too that some day he would die, and the thought was not unpleasant, for one half of his life was away in the territories of the King of Leinster, and the half that he kept in himself had no spice in it.

===Chinese===
他又想，自己总有一日要死；这念头竟不叫人难受——他的生命已有一半远在伦斯特王的封地里，留在自己身上的那一半，也早已没了滋味。

===Original===
He was thinking in this way when mac an Dáv came towards him over the lawn, and he noticed that mac an Dáv was walking like an old man.

===Chinese===
他正这么想着，麦克·安·达夫穿过草地朝他走来；他注意到，麦克·安·达夫走起路来像个老头儿。

===Original===
He took little slow steps, and he did not loosen his knees when he walked, so he went stiffly. One of his feet turned pitifully outwards, and the other turned lamentably in. His chest was pulled inwards, and his head was stuck outwards and hung down in the place where his chest should have been, and his arms were crooked in front of him with the hands turned wrongly, so that one palm was shown to the east of the world and the other one was turned to the west.

===Chinese===
他迈着细碎迟缓的步子，走路时膝盖不打弯，浑身僵直；一只脚可怜巴巴地朝外撇，另一只脚凄凄惨惨地朝里勾；胸口往里塌，脑袋朝前探，垂在胸口本该在的地方；两条胳膊弯在身前，手势也是拧着的——一只手掌摊向世界的东边，另一只翻向世界的西边。

===Original===
“How goes it, mac an Dáv?” said the king.

===Chinese===
“你这是怎么了，麦克·安·达夫？”王说。

===Original===
“Bad,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“糟透了。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“Is that the sun I see shining, my friend?” the king asked.

===Chinese===
“我看见的这个，是太阳在照吗，我的朋友？”王问。

===Original===
“It may be the sun,” replied mac an Dáv, peering curiously at the golden radiance that dozed about them, “but maybe it’s a yellow fog.”

===Chinese===
“兴许是太阳，”麦克·安·达夫答道，古怪地眯眼望着四周打盹的金光，“也兴许是一团黄雾。”

===Original===
“What is life at all?” said the king.

===Chinese===
“人生到底是什么？”王说。

===Original===
“It is a weariness and a tiredness,” said mac an Dáv. “It is a long yawn without sleepiness. It is a bee, lost at midnight and buzzing on a pane. It is the noise made by a tied-up dog. It is nothing worth dreaming about. It is nothing at all.”

===Chinese===
“是一场困乏，一场倦怠，”麦克·安·达夫说，“是一个不困的人打的长长的哈欠。是一只午夜迷路、趴在窗玻璃上嗡嗡的蜜蜂。是一条拴住的狗发出的动静。是一场不值得做的梦。什么也不是。”

===Original===
“How well you explain my feelings about Duv Laca,” said the king.

===Chinese===
“你把我对杜夫·拉卡的心情，解释得多透彻啊。”王说。

===Original===
“I was thinking about my own lamb,” said mac an Dáv. “I was thinking about my own treasure, my cup of cheeriness, and the pulse of my heart.” And with that he burst into tears.

===Chinese===
“我想的是我自家的小羊羔，”麦克·安·达夫说，“想我自家的宝贝，我那杯欢喜，我心口的搏动。”话音未落，他放声大哭。

===Original===
“Alas!” said the king.

===Chinese===
“唉！”王说。

===Original===
“But,” sobbed mac an Dáv, “what right have I to complain? I am only the servant, and although I didn’t make any bargain with the King of Leinster or with any king of them all, yet my wife is gone away as if she was the consort of a potentate the same as Duv Laca is.”

===Chinese===
“可是，”麦克·安·达夫抽噎道，“我有什么资格抱怨？我不过是个仆人。虽说同伦斯特王也好，同他们哪一国的王也好，我都没立过什么契，我的老婆却走得同杜夫·拉卡一样，倒像是哪位大人物的王妃似的。”

===Original===
Mongan was sorry then for his servant, and he roused himself.

===Chinese===
蒙根怜惜起仆人来，重新振作了。

===Original===
“I am going to send you to Duv Laca.”

===Chinese===
“我要派你去见杜夫·拉卡。”

===Original===
“Where the one is the other will be,” cried mac an Dáv joyously.

===Chinese===
“一个在哪儿，另一个必在哪儿！”麦克·安·达夫欢喜地叫道。

===Original===
“Go,” said Mongan, “to Rath Descirt of Bregia; you know that place?”

===Chinese===
“去，”蒙根说，“去布里吉亚（Bregia）的拉斯·德斯卡特（Rath Descirt）；你知道那地方？”

===Original===
“As well as my tongue knows my teeth.”

===Chinese===
“熟得就像我的舌头认得我的牙。”

===Original===
“Duv Laca is there; see her, and ask her what she wants me to do.”

===Chinese===
“杜夫·拉卡在那里。见着她，问问她要我做什么。”

===Original===
Mac an Dáv went there and returned.

===Chinese===
麦克·安·达夫去了，又回来了。

===Original===
“Duv Laca says that you are to come at once, for the King of Leinster is journeying around his territory, and Kevin Cochlach, the charioteer, is making bitter love to her and wants her to run away with him.”

===Chinese===
“杜夫·拉卡说请您马上去，因为伦斯特王正巡行封地，那御者凯文·科克拉克正对她苦苦求爱，要诱她一同私奔。”

===Original===
Mongan set out, and in no great time, for they travelled day and night, they came to Bregla, and gained admittance to the fortress, but just as he got in he had to go out again, for the King of Leinster had been warned of Mongan’s journey, and came back to his fortress in the nick of time.

===Chinese===
蒙根当即出发。一行昼夜兼程，没几日便到了布里吉亚，进了堡寨；可他才进门就得再出门——伦斯特王得了蒙根动身的信，恰好赶回堡寨，早不早，晚不晚，正撞上。

===Original===
When the men of Ulster saw the condition into which Mongan fell they were in great distress, and they all got sick through compassion for their king. The nobles suggested to him that they should march against Leinster and kill that king and bring back Duv Laca, but Mongan would not consent to this plan.

===Chinese===
阿尔斯特人见蒙根落到这般田地，个个悲痛，人人因怜惜他们的王而害起病来。贵族们献策：进兵伦斯特，杀了那王，把杜夫·拉卡夺回来。蒙根却不肯依。

===Original===
“For,” said he, “the thing I lost through my own folly I shall get back through my own craft.”

===Chinese===
“因为，”他说，“我因自己的愚拙丢掉的东西，要凭自己的巧计赢回来。”

===Original===
And when he said that his spirits revived, and he called for mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
这话一出，他的精神竟回来了，便唤麦克·安·达夫。

===Original===
“You know, my friend,” said Mongan, “that I can’t get Duv Laca back unless the King of Leinster asks me to take her back, for a bargain is a bargain.”

===Chinese===
“你晓得，我的朋友，”蒙根说，“除非伦斯特王亲口求我接回杜夫·拉卡，我便要不回她——立了约的，就是立了约。”

===Original===
“That will happen when pigs fly,” said mac an Dáv, “and,” said he, “I did not make any bargain with any king that is in the world.”

===Chinese===
“那得等到猪飞上天，”麦克·安·达夫说，“再说，”他说，“普天之下的王，随便哪一个，我都没同他立过什么契。”

===Original===
“I heard you say that before,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“这话你早说过了。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“I will say it till Doom,” cried his servant, “for my wife has gone away with that pestilent king, and he has got the double of your bad bargain.”

===Chinese===
“我要把这话一直说到劫数临头！”他的仆人喊道，“我的老婆跟着那个瘟王跑了，你那桩亏本买卖，倒叫他一个人赚了双份。”

===Original===
Mongan and his servant then set out for Leinster.

===Chinese===
蒙根主仆于是动身去伦斯特。

===Original===
When they neared that country they found a great crowd going on the road with them, and they learned that the king was giving a feast in honour of his marriage to Duv Laca, for the year of waiting was nearly out, and the king had sworn he would delay no longer.

===Chinese===
临近那国时，只见大路上人流滚滚，与他们同向而行。一打听才知：王要设大宴，庆贺他与杜夫·拉卡的婚事——因为一年之期将满，王已立誓不再拖延。

===Original===
They went on, therefore, but in low spirits, and at last they saw the walls of the king’s castle towering before them, and a noble company going to and fro on the lawn.

===Chinese===
两人只得继续前行，一路蔫头耷脑。终于，王的城堡高墙巍然耸立在眼前，草坪上一队队贵人往来穿梭。

### XIX / XIX

===Original===
They sat in a place where they could watch the castle and compose themselves after their journey.

===Chinese===
两人寻了个地方坐下，好守望城堡，也歇一歇旅途的劳顿。

===Original===
“How are we going to get into the castle?” asked mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“咱们怎么进城堡呢？”麦克·安·达夫问。

===Original===
For there were hatchetmen on guard in the big gateway, and there were spearmen at short intervals around the walls, and men to throw hot porridge off the roof were standing in the right places.

===Chinese===
城堡大门有斧手把守，四墙每隔不远便立着枪兵，屋顶上该站人的位置，站着浇热粥的汉子。

===Original===
“If we cannot get in by hook, we will get in by crook,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“直路进不去，我们就拐着弯进。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“They are both good ways,” said Mac an Dáv, “and whichever of them you decide on I’ll stick by.”

===Chinese===
“两条都是好路，”麦克·安·达夫说，“您挑中哪条，我跟定哪条。”

===Original===
Just then they saw the Hag of the Mill coming out of the mill which was down the road a little.

===Chinese===
正这时，他们看见磨坊老妪（the Hag of the Mill）从磨坊里出来——那磨坊就在沿路下去不远的地方。

===Original===
Now the Hag of the Mill was a bony, thin pole of a hag with odd feet. That is, she had one foot that was too big for her, so that when she lifted it up it pulled her over; and she had one foot that was too small for her, so that when she lifted it up she didn’t know what to do with it. She was so long that you thought you would never see the end of her, and she was so thin that you thought you didn’t see her at all. One of her eyes was set where her nose should be and there was an ear in its place, and her nose itself was hanging out of her chin, and she had whiskers round it. She was dressed in a red rag that was really a hole with a fringe on it, and she was singing “Oh, hush thee, my one love” to a cat that was yelping on her shoulder.

===Chinese===
这磨坊老妪是一根骨头架子似的瘦长条老妪，生着两只怪脚：一只脚太大，不合她的身量，一抬起来便把她整个人拽倒；另一只脚又太小，也不合身量，抬起来了，她竟不知拿它怎么办。她长得那样长，你以为一辈子也望不到她的头；又瘦得那样瘦，你以为根本没看见她。一只眼睛安在鼻子该在的地方，原位上却蹲着一只耳朵；鼻子自己挂在下巴外头，四围还长着一圈颊须。她身上披一块红破布——那实在是一个窟窿，边上缀了点穗子——肩上蹲着一只嚎叫的猫，她正对着它唱“哦，别作声，我的独一爱人”。

===Original===
She had a tall skinny dog behind her called Brotar. It hadn’t a tooth in its head except one, and it had the toothache in that tooth. Every few steps it used to sit down on its hunkers and point its nose straight upwards, and make a long, sad complaint about its tooth; and after that it used to reach its hind leg round and try to scratch out its tooth; and then it used to be pulled on again by the straw rope that was round its neck, and which was tied at the other end to the hag’s heaviest foot.

===Chinese===
她身后跟着一条精瘦的高狗，名叫布罗塔尔（Brotar）。它满脑袋只剩一颗牙，偏偏那颗牙还疼。每走几步，它就要蹲下屁股，鼻尖直指青天，为那颗牙发出一段悠长悲切的诉苦；诉完，再腾出后腿绕到头边，挠那颗牙；挠罢，又被脖子上的草绳拽着往前走——草绳另一头，拴在老妪那只最沉的脚上。

===Original===
There was an old, knock-kneed, rawboned, one-eyed, little-winded, heavy-headed mare with her also. Every time it put a front leg forward it shivered all over the rest of its legs backwards, and when it put a hind leg forward it shivered all over the rest of its legs frontwards, and it used to give a great whistle through its nose when it was out of breath, and a big, thin hen was sitting on its croup.

===Chinese===
她还有一匹老母马：罗圈腿，骨头架子，独眼，气短，头重。它每迈一条前腿，其余的腿便一齐朝后打颤；每迈一条后腿，其余的腿又一齐朝前打颤；气接不上的时候，鼻子里便吹出老大一声呼哨。马臀上还蹲着一只干瘦的大母鸡。

===Original===
Mongan looked on the Hag of the Mill with delight and affection.

===Chinese===
蒙根望着磨坊老妪，眼里满是欢喜和怜爱。

===Original===
“This time,” said he to mac an Dáv, “I’ll get back my wife.”

===Chinese===
“这一回，”他对麦克·安·达夫说，“我准能把妻子弄回来。”

===Original===
“You will indeed,” said mac an Dáv heartily, “and you’ll get mine back too.”

===Chinese===
“准能，”麦克·安·达夫衷心地说，“连我那个也一并弄回来。”

===Original===
“Go over yonder,” said Mongan, “and tell the Hag of the Mill that I want to talk to her.”

===Chinese===
“到那边去，”蒙根说，“告诉磨坊老妪，我要同她说话。”

===Original===
Mac an Dáv brought her over to him.

===Chinese===
麦克·安·达夫便把她领了过来。

===Original===
“Is it true what the servant man said?” she asked.

===Chinese===
“仆人小哥说的话，是真的吗？”她问。

===Original===
“What did he say?” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“他说了什么？”蒙根说。

===Original===
“He said you wanted to talk to me.”

===Chinese===
“他说你要同我说话。”

===Original===
“It is true,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“是真的。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“This is a wonderful hour and a glorious minute,” said the hag, “for this is the first time in sixty years that anyone wanted to talk to me. Talk on now,” said she, “and I’ll listen to you if I can remember how to do it. Talk gently,” said she, “the way you won’t disturb the animals, for they are all sick.”

===Chinese===
“这可真是个奇妙的时辰、光荣的分秒，”老妪说，“六十年了，头一回有人要同我说话。你说吧，”她说，“我这就听——只要我还记得怎么个听法。说得轻些，”她说，“省得惊了这些牲口，它们全都病着呢。”

===Original===
“They are sick indeed,” said mac an Dáv pityingly.

===Chinese===
“病得不轻呢。”麦克·安·达夫怜悯地说。

===Original===
“The cat has a sore tail,” said she, “by reason of sitting too close to a part of the hob that was hot. The dog has a toothache, the horse has a pain in her stomach, and the hen has the pip.”

===Chinese===
“猫尾巴烫坏了，”她说，“谁叫它贴着炉台一块滚烫的地方坐。狗牙疼，马肚子疼，母鸡害了嗉囊病。”

===Original===
“Ah, it’s a sad world,” said mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
“唉，这世道真叫人伤心。”麦克·安·达夫说。

===Original===
“There you are!” said the hag.

===Chinese===
“可不是嘛！”老妪说。

===Original===
“Tell me,” Mongan commenced, “if you got a wish, what it is you would wish for?”

===Chinese===
“告诉我，”蒙根开腔道，“若给你一个愿望，你愿意要什么？”

===Original===
The hag took the cat off her shoulder and gave it to mac an Dáv.

===Chinese===
老妪把猫从肩上抱下来，递给麦克·安·达夫。

===Original===
“Hold that for me while I think,” said she.

===Chinese===
“替我拿着，容我想想。”她说。

===Original===
