“Would you like to be a lovely young girl?” asked Mongan.

===Chinese===
“你愿意变成一个妙龄的俏姑娘吗？”蒙根问。

===Original===
“I’d sooner be that than a skinned eel,” said she.

===Chinese===
“比起当一条剥了皮的鳗鱼，我可太愿意了。”她说。

===Original===
“And would you like to marry me or the King of Leinster?”

===Chinese===
“那你愿意嫁我呢，还是嫁伦斯特王？”

===Original===
“I’d like to marry either of you, or both of you, or whichever of you came first.”

===Chinese===
“你们俩哪个我都愿意嫁，俩都嫁也行，谁先来就嫁谁。”

===Original===
“Very well,” said Mongan, “you shall have your wish.”

===Chinese===
“很好，”蒙根说，“你的愿望会实现的。”

===Original===
He touched her with his finger, and the instant he touched her all dilapidation and wryness and age went from her, and she became so beautiful that one dared scarcely look on her, and so young that she seemed but sixteen years of age.

===Chinese===
他伸指一点。指头才沾着她，一切残破、歪斜和老迈便从她身上退去了；她变得那样美，美得叫人不敢直视；又那样年轻，看上去不过十六岁。

===Original===
“You are not the Hag of the Mill any longer,” said Mongan, “you are Ivell of the Shining Cheeks, daughter of the King of Munster.”

===Chinese===
“你不再是磨坊老妪了，”蒙根说，“你是双颊生辉的伊薇尔（Ivell of the Shining Cheeks），芒斯特（Munster）王的女儿。”

===Original===
He touched the dog too, and it became a little silky lapdog that could nestle in your palm. Then he changed the old mare into a brisk, piebald palfrey. Then he changed himself so that he became the living image of Ae, the son of the King of Connaught, who had just been married to Ivell of the Shining Cheeks, and then he changed mac an Dáv into the likeness of Ae’s attendant, and then they all set off towards the fortress, singing the song that begins:

===Chinese===
他又点了点那条狗，狗变成一只丝毛小狗，能蜷在你掌心里打盹。他又把老母马变作一匹轻快的花斑骏马。再变化自己，变作康诺特（Connaught）王之子艾（Ae）的活脱脱的模样——这位艾刚刚娶了双颊生辉的伊薇尔；随后又把麦克·安·达夫变成艾的侍从的模样。于是众人朝堡寨进发，唱起了那支开头是这样的歌：

===Original===
> My wife is nicer than anyone’s wife,
 

 Anyone’s wife, anyone’s wife,
 

 My wife is nicer than anyone’s wife,
 

 Which nobody can deny.


===Chinese===
> 我的妻子比谁家的妻子都可爱，
 

 谁家的妻子，谁家的妻子，
 

 我的妻子比谁家的妻子都可爱，
 

 这一点谁也不能否认。

### XX / XX

===Original===
The doorkeeper brought word to the King of Leinster that the son of the King of Connaught, Ae the Beautiful, and his wife, Ivell of the Shining Cheeks, were at the door, that they had been banished from Connaught by Ae’s father, and they were seeking the protection of the King of Leinster.

===Chinese===
守门人进来禀报伦斯特王：康诺特王之子、美男子艾（Ae the Beautiful）与他的妻子双颊生辉的伊薇尔到了门前，说他们被艾的父亲逐出了康诺特，特来求伦斯特王庇护。

===Original===
Branduv came to the door himself to welcome them, and the minute he looked on Ivell of the Shining Cheeks it was plain that he liked looking at her.

===Chinese===
布兰杜夫亲自到门前迎接。他一望见双颊生辉的伊薇尔，那副神情分明写着：这一眼，看得很受用。

===Original===
It was now drawing towards evening, and a feast was prepared for the guests with a banquet to follow it. At the feast Duv Laca sat beside the King of Leinster, but Mongan sat opposite him with Ivell, and Mongan put more and more magic into the hag, so that her cheeks shone and her eyes gleamed, and she was utterly bewitching to the eye; and when Branduv looked at her she seemed to grow more and more lovely and more and more desirable, and at last there was not a bone in his body as big as an inch that was not filled with love and longing for the girl.

===Chinese===
这时天色向晚，为来客备下了盛宴，宴后还有华筵。席上，杜夫·拉卡坐在伦斯特王身边，蒙根却带着伊薇尔坐在他对面。蒙根把一层又一层的魔法注进老妪身上，她双颊生光，眼波流转，望去勾魂摄魄；布兰杜夫越看她，她仿佛越可爱、越叫人心里发痒，到后来，他浑身上下，凡是长过一寸的骨头，没有一根不灌满了对这姑娘的爱恋与渴慕。

===Original===
Every few minutes he gave a great sigh as if he had eaten too much, and when Duv Laca asked him if he had eaten too much he said he had but that he had not drunk enough, and by that he meant that he had not drunk enough from the eyes of the girl before him.

===Chinese===
每隔几分钟，他便长叹一声，像是吃得太撑。杜夫·拉卡问他是不是撑着了，他说吃着了，只是还没喝够——意思是，还没从眼前这姑娘的眼睛里喝够。

===Original===
At the banquet which was then held he looked at her again, and every time he took a drink he toasted Ivell across the brim of his goblet, and in a little while she began to toast him back across the rim of her cup, for he was drinking ale, but she was drinking mead. Then he sent a messenger to her to say that it was a far better thing to be the wife of the King of Leinster than to be the wife of the son of the King of Connaught, for a king is better than a prince, and Ivell thought that this was as wise a thing as anybody had ever said. And then he sent a message to say that he loved her so much that he would certainly burst of love if it did not stop.

===Chinese===
接着的华筵上，他又望她。每饮一口，便隔着酒盏的边沿向伊薇尔致意；不多时，她也隔着杯沿回敬起来——他饮的是麦酒，她饮的是蜜酒。随后他遣人传话给她，说做伦斯特王的妻子，远胜过做康诺特王之子的妻子，因为王比王子强；伊薇尔一听，觉得这话是天下人有史以来讲过的最智慧的话。接着他又传话说，他爱她爱得太厉害，再不住口，必定要为爱炸裂。

===Original===
Mongan heard the whispering, and he told the hag that if she did what he advised she would certainly get either himself or the King of Leinster for a husband.

===Chinese===
蒙根听见这些咬耳朵的话，便告诉老妪：只要照他的主意行事，她准能弄到一个丈夫——不是他自己，便是伦斯特王。

===Original===
“Either of you will be welcome,” said the hag.

===Chinese===
“你们哪位来都欢迎。”老妪说。

===Original===
“When the king says he loves you, ask him to prove it by gifts; ask for his drinking-horn first.”

===Chinese===
“等王说他爱你，就叫他拿礼物作证；先讨他的饮酒号角。”

===Original===
She asked for that, and he sent it to her filled with good liquor; then she asked for his girdle, and he sent her that.

===Chinese===
她讨了号角，他灌满了美酒给她送来；她又讨他的腰带，他也送来了。

===Original===
His people argued with him and said it was not right that he should give away the treasures of Leinster to the wife of the King of Connaught’s son; but he said that it did not matter, for when he got the girl he would get his treasures with her. But every time he sent anything to the hag, mac an Dáv snatched it out of her lap and put it in his pocket.

===Chinese===
他的臣下苦劝，说把伦斯特的珍宝送给康诺特王之子的妻子，于理不合。他却说无妨——人一到手，珍宝自然跟着回来。可他每送老妪一件东西，麦克·安·达夫便从她膝上一把抢走，塞进自己口袋。

===Original===
“Now,” said Mongan to the hag, “tell the servant to say that you would not leave your own husband for all the wealth of the world.”

===Chinese===
“现在，”蒙根对老妪说，“叫那侍从去传话，就说纵有天下的财富，你也不肯离开自己的丈夫。”

===Original===
She told the servant that, and the servant told it to the king.

===Chinese===
她这般吩咐了侍从，侍从便这般禀了王。

===Original===
When Branduv heard it he nearly went mad with love and longing and jealousy, and with rage also, because of the treasure he had given her and might not get back. He called Mongan over to him, and spoke to him very threateningly and ragingly.

===Chinese===
布兰杜夫一听，又爱又渴又妒，几乎发狂，还添了一层怒——珍宝已经送出去，怕是要不回来了。他把蒙根叫到跟前，声色俱厉，怒气冲冲地说话。

===Original===
“I am not one who takes a thing without giving a thing,” said he.

===Chinese===
“我这人，拿人一物，必还一物。”他说。

===Original===
“Nobody could say you were,” agreed Mongan.

===Chinese===
“谁也不能说您是。”蒙根附和道。

===Original===
“Do you see this woman sitting beside me?” he continued, pointing to Duv Laca.

===Chinese===
“你看见坐在我身边的这个女人了吗？”他指着杜夫·拉卡，接着说。

===Original===
“I do indeed,” said Mongan.

===Chinese===
“看见了，千真万确。”蒙根说。

===Original===
“Well,” said Branduv, “this woman is Duv Laca of the White Hand that I took away from Mongan; she is just going to marry me, but if you will make an exchange, you can marry this Duv Laca here, and I will marry that Ivell of the Shining Cheeks yonder.”

===Chinese===
“告诉你，”布兰杜夫说，“这个女人，是我从蒙根手里夺来的白手杜夫·拉卡；她即刻就要嫁我。不过你若肯交换——你可以娶这边的杜夫·拉卡，我娶那边的双颊生辉的伊薇尔。”

===Original===
Mongan pretended to be very angry then.

===Chinese===
蒙根顿时装出大怒的样子。

===Original===
“If I had come here with horses and treasure you would be in your right to take these from me, but you have no right to ask for what you are now asking.”

===Chinese===
“我若带马来、带财宝来，你要夺去，那是你的道理；可你现在讨的东西，你却没有道理讨。”

===Original===
“I do ask for it,” said Branduv menacingly, “and you must not refuse a lord.”

===Chinese===
“我偏要讨，”布兰杜夫威逼道，“君上开口，你不得回绝。”

===Original===
“Very well,” said Mongan reluctantly, and as if in great fear; “if you will make the exchange I will make it, although it breaks my heart.”

===Chinese===
“好吧，”蒙根勉为其难地说，还装得惶恐万分，“您既执意交换，我便换吧——虽然这一换，碎的是我的心。”

===Original===
He brought Ivell over to the king then and gave her three kisses.

===Chinese===
随后他把伊薇尔领到王跟前，吻了她三下。

===Original===
“The king would suspect something if I did not kiss you,” said he, and then he gave the hag over to the king.

===Chinese===
“我不吻你，王该起疑了。”他说。说罢，便把老妪交给了王。

===Original===
After that they all got drunk and merry, and soon there was a great snoring and snorting, and very soon all the servants fell asleep also, so that Mongan could not get anything to drink. Mac an Dáv said it was a great shame, and he kicked some of the servants, but they did not budge, and then he slipped out to the stables and saddled two mares. He got on one with his wife behind him and Mongan got on the other with Duv Laca behind him, and they rode away towards Ulster like the wind, singing this song:

===Chinese===
之后众人喝得酩酊大醉，欢天喜地；不多时鼾声、鼻息声此起彼伏，很快连仆役们也都睡着了，蒙根竟连一口酒也讨不着了。麦克·安·达夫说这真是太不像话，踢了几个仆役，一个个纹丝不动；他便溜进马厩，备好两匹母马。他同妻子共乘一匹，蒙根载着杜夫·拉卡骑另一匹，一行朝阿尔斯特疾驰而去，快得像风，嘴里还唱着这支歌：

===Original===
> The King of Leinster was married today,
 

 Married today, married today,
 

 The King of Leinster was married today,
 

 And everyone wishes him joy.


===Chinese===
> 伦斯特王今天娶了亲，
 

 娶了亲，娶了亲，
 

 伦斯特王今天娶了亲，
 

 人人都向他道喜。

===Original===
In the morning the servants came to waken the King of Leinster, and when they saw the face of the hag lying on the pillow beside the king, and her nose all covered with whiskers, and her big foot and little foot sticking away out at the end of the bed, they began to laugh, and poke one another in the stomachs and thump one another on the shoulders, so that the noise awakened the king, and he asked what was the matter with them at all. It was then he saw the hag lying beside him, and he gave a great screech and jumped out of the bed.

===Chinese===
早晨，仆役们来唤醒伦斯特王。他们看见老妪的脸躺在王身边的枕头上，鼻子上覆满颊须，一只大脚一只小脚直挺挺戳在床尾，便哄然大笑，你捅我的肚子，我捶你的肩膀，闹得醒了王，他还问他们到底犯了什么病。这一问，他才看见老妪躺在身边，登时一声惨叫，蹦下了床。

===Original===
“Aren’t you the Hag of the Mill?” said he.

===Chinese===
“你不就是磨坊老妪么？”他说。

===Original===
“I am indeed,” she replied, “and I love you dearly.”

===Chinese===
“正是我呀，”她答道，“我可疼你呢。”

===Original===
“I wish I didn’t see you,” said Branduv.

===Chinese===
“真希望我从没看见过你。”布兰杜夫说。

---

===Original===
That was the end of the story, and when he had told it Mongan began to laugh uproariously and called for more wine. He drank this deeply, as though he was full of thirst and despair and a wild jollity, but when the Flame Lady began to weep he took her in his arms and caressed her, and said that she was the love of his heart and the one treasure of the world.

===Chinese===
故事到此为止。讲完之后，蒙根纵声大笑，又吩咐上酒。他深杯痛饮，仿佛胸中满是焦渴、绝望和一种狂野的欢腾；可火焰夫人一哭，他便把她搂进怀里温存抚慰，说她是他的心头之爱，是世上独一无二的珍宝。

===Original===
After that they feasted in great contentment, and at the end of the feasting they went away from Faery and returned to the world of men.

===Chinese===
此后他们心满意足地欢宴了一场；宴罢，众人离开仙境，回到了世人的世界。

===Original===
They came to Mongan’s palace at Moy Linney, and it was not until they reached the palace that they found they had been away one whole year, for they had thought they were only away one night. They lived then peacefully and lovingly together, and that ends the story, but Brótiarna did not know that Mongan was Fionn.

===Chinese===
他们回到蒙根在莫伊·林尼（Moy Linney）的王宫，直到进了宫门才发现，他们这一去竟是整整一年——原以为只离开了一夜。此后他们安宁恩爱地过活，故事至此完结。只是布罗蒂亚娜始终不知道，蒙根就是芬恩（Fionn）。

---

===Original===
The abbot leaned forward.

===Chinese===
院长俯身向前。

===Original===
“Was Mongan Fionn?” he asked in a whisper.

===Chinese===
“蒙根就是芬恩么？”他低声问。

===Original===
“He was,” replied Cairidè.

===Chinese===
“正是。”凯里德答道。

===Original===
“Indeed, indeed!” said the abbot.

===Chinese===
“果然，果然！”院长说。

===Original===
After a while he continued: “There is only one part of your story that I do not like.”

===Chinese===
过了一会儿，他又说：“你的故事里，只有一处我不喜欢。”

===Original===
“What part is that?” asked Cairidè.

===Chinese===
“哪一处？”凯里德问。

===Original===
“It is the part where the holy man Tibraidè was ill treated by that rap﻿—by that﻿—by Mongan.”

===Chinese===
“就是圣者蒂布拉德遭作弄的那一段——被那个匪……被那个……被蒙根。”

===Original===
Cairidè agreed that it was ill done, but to himself he said gleefully that whenever he was asked to tell the story of how he told the story of Mongan he would remember what the abbot said.

===Chinese===
凯里德承认那事做得不对；可他心里乐滋滋地想：往后凡有人请他讲“他如何讲蒙根的故事”的故事，他总要记得院长这句话。

## Oisín’s Mother / 奥辛的母亲

### I / I

===Original===
Evening was drawing nigh, and the Fianna-Finn had decided to hunt no more that day. The hounds were whistled to heel, and a sober, homeward march began. For men will walk soberly in the evening, however they go in the day, and dogs will take the mood from their masters.

===Chinese===
夜色渐渐降临，芬尼亚勇士团（Fianna-Finn）决定这一天不再行猎了。呼哨一响，猎犬都被唤回脚边，一行人马踏上沉稳的归途。因为人不论白天怎样奔走，到了傍晚总会走得沉稳，而狗呢，主人是什么心绪，它们便是什么心绪。

===Original===
They were pacing so, through the golden-shafted, tender-coloured eve, when a fawn leaped suddenly from covert, and, with that leap, all quietness vanished: the men shouted, the dogs gave tongue, and a furious chase commenced.

===Chinese===
他们正这样走着，穿过道道金光、色彩柔嫩的黄昏，忽然一头小鹿从隐蔽处跃出——随着这一跃，一切安宁霎时无踪：人们呼喊，群犬出声，一场狂烈的追逐开始了。

===Original===
Fionn loved a chase at any hour, and, with Bran and Sceólan, he outstripped the men and dogs of his troop, until nothing remained in the limpid world but Fionn, the two hounds, and the nimble, beautiful fawn. These, and the occasional boulders, round which they raced, or over which they scrambled; the solitary tree which dozed aloof and beautiful in the path, the occasional clump of trees that hived sweet shadow as a hive hoards honey, and the rustling grass that stretched to infinity, and that moved and crept and swung under the breeze in endless, rhythmic billowings.

===Chinese===
芬恩（Fionn）无论什么时辰都爱一场追逐。他带着布兰（Bran）和斯科兰（Sceólan），把队伍里的人和狗远远甩在身后，直到那澄明的天地间只剩下芬恩、两只猎犬，和那头轻捷美丽的小鹿。再有，便是偶尔的巨石，供他们绕着疾驰，或攀上翻越；是一棵孤零零的树，在路旁独自打盹，美丽而清远；是一簇簇的树丛，像蜂巢贮蜜一般贮满甜荫；还有那窸窣作响的青草，铺展到无穷无尽，在微风里涌动、蔓爬、摇荡，化作无休无止、一浪逐一浪的起伏。

===Original===
In his wildest moment Fionn was thoughtful, and now, although running hard, he was thoughtful. There was no movement of his beloved hounds that he did not know; not a twitch or fling of the head, not a cock of the ears or tail that was not significant to him. But on this chase whatever signs the dogs gave were not understood by their master.

===Chinese===
芬恩哪怕在最狂野的时刻，心里也存着思量；此刻他虽然拼命奔跑，心里仍存着思量。他心爱的猎犬，没有一个动作是他不认得的：脑袋每一次抽动、每一次扬甩，耳朵或尾巴每一次竖立，对他都有含义。可这一场追逐里，狗儿们无论递出什么信号，主人却都读不懂了。

===Original===
He had never seen them in such eager flight. They were almost utterly absorbed in it, but they did not whine with eagerness, nor did they cast any glance towards him for the encouraging word which he never failed to give when they sought it.

===Chinese===
他从没见过它们追得这样急切。它们几乎全然忘我地扑在这追逐上，却不因急切而呜咽，也不朝他递来一眼，讨那句鼓劲的话——往常它们一讨，他从没有不给的。

===Original===
They did look at him, but it was a look which he could not comprehend. There was a question and a statement in those deep eyes, and he could not understand what that question might be, nor what it was they sought to convey. Now and again one of the dogs turned a head in full flight, and stared, not at Fionn, but distantly backwards, over the spreading and swelling plain where their companions of the hunt had disappeared.

===Chinese===
它们确实望了他，可那是他读不懂的一望。那双深邃的眼睛里有一个问题、一句陈说，他既不明白那问题会是什么，也不明白它们想传达的是什么。疾奔之中，两只狗偶尔有一颗回过头来，凝望的不是芬恩，而是远远的后方，望着同伴们消失于其上的那片铺展起伏的平原。

===Original===
“They are looking for the other hounds,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“它们是在找别的猎犬。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“And yet they do not give tongue! Tongue it, a Vran!” he shouted, “Bell it out, a Heólan!”

===Chinese===
“可它们竟不出声！叫起来呀，布兰！”他喊道，“吼出声来，斯科兰！”

===Original===
It was then they looked at him, the look which he could not understand and had never seen on a chase. They did not tongue it, nor bell it, but they added silence to silence and speed to speed, until the lean grey bodies were one pucker and lashing of movement.

===Chinese===
正是在这时，它们望了他——那望不透的一望，是他在追逐场上从未见过的。它们不叫，也不吼，只是把沉默叠给沉默，把速度叠给速度，直到那精瘦的灰色身躯，化作一团拧紧了又抽掠的疾动。

===Original===
Fionn marvelled.

===Chinese===
芬恩惊异不已。

===Original===
“They do not want the other dogs to hear or to come on this chase,” he murmured, and he wondered what might be passing within those slender heads.

===Chinese===
“它们不想让别的狗听见，不想让它们加入这场追逐。”他喃喃道，心里纳罕，那两颗修长的头颅里，究竟转着什么念头。

===Original===
“The fawn runs well,” his thought continued. “What is it, a Vran, my heart? After her, a Heólan! Hist and away, my loves!”

===Chinese===
“这小鹿跑得真好，”他的念头接着转，“怎么回事，布兰，我的心肝？追上她，斯科兰！嗬——追吧，我的宝贝们！”

===Original===
“There is going and to spare in that beast yet,” his mind went on. “She is not stretched to the full, nor half stretched. She may outrun even Bran,” he thought ragingly.

===Chinese===
“这畜生还有的是脚力，”他的心思继续道，“她还没使出全力，连一半也没使出。她说不定连布兰都能跑赢。”他这样想着，心头冒火。

===Original===
They were racing through a smooth valley in a steady, beautiful, speedy flight when, suddenly, the fawn stopped and lay on the grass, and it lay with the calm of an animal that has no fear, and the leisure of one that is not pressed.

===Chinese===
他们正以平稳、优美、迅疾的疾驰穿过一道平缓的山谷，小鹿却突然收步，卧在了草地上。它卧得那样安静，像一头毫无恐惧的兽；卧得那样从容，像一只不曾被逼迫的生灵。

===Original===
“Here is a change,” said Fionn, staring in astonishment. “She is not winded,” he said. “What is she lying down for?”

===Chinese===
“这可变了样了，”芬恩瞪着眼，惊诧不已。“她连气都不喘，”他说，“她卧下做什么？”

===Original===
But Bran and Sceólan did not stop; they added another inch to their long-stretched easy bodies, and came up on the fawn.

===Chinese===
布兰和斯科兰却没有停；它们舒展的长身子又拉长了一寸，朝小鹿逼了过去。

===Original===
“It is an easy kill,” said Fionn regretfully. “They have her,” he cried.

===Chinese===
“这一下唾手可得了，”芬恩惋惜地说。“它们逮住她了。”他喊道。

===Original===
But he was again astonished, for the dogs did not kill. They leaped and played about the fawn, licking its face, and rubbing delighted noses against its neck.

===Chinese===
可他又一次惊住了：狗没有扑杀。它们绕着小鹿又蹦又耍，舔它的脸，把欢喜的鼻子往它颈子上蹭。

===Original===
Fionn came up then. His long spear was lowered in his fist at the thrust, and his sharp knife was in its sheath, but he did not use them, for the fawn and the two hounds began to play round him, and the fawn was as affectionate towards him as the hounds were; so that when a velvet nose was thrust in his palm, it was as often a fawn’s muzzle as a hound’s.

===Chinese===
芬恩随即赶了上来。他拳中攥着的长矛已压低待刺，利刀却还留在鞘里，可他一样也没有用——因为小鹿和两只猎犬绕着他耍闹起来，小鹿待他的亲热，竟不输猎犬；于是每当一只天鹅绒似的鼻子伸进他的掌心，那鼻子有时是猎犬的，有时便是小鹿的。

---

===Original===
In that joyous company he came to wide Allen of Leinster, where the people were surprised to see the hounds and the fawn and the Chief and none other of the hunters that had set out with them.

===Chinese===
他就在这支欢欢喜喜的队伍陪伴下，回到了伦斯特（Leinster）广阔的阿伦（Allen）。那里的人们看见猎犬、小鹿与首领同归，同他们一道出发的猎人却一个也不见，都惊奇不已。

===Original===
When the others reached home, the Chief told of his chase, and it was agreed that such a fawn must not be killed, but that it should be kept and well treated, and that it should be the pet fawn of the Fianna. But some of those who remembered Brah’s parentage thought that as Bran herself had come from the Shí so this fawn might have come out of the Shí also.

===Chinese===
其余人回到家后，首领讲了他的追逐，众人议定：这样的小鹿万万杀不得，须得好好收养、好好相待，让它做勇士团的宝贝小鹿。不过，一些记得布兰出身来历的人心想：布兰既然本是从仙丘（the Shí）里来的，这头小鹿，说不定也是从仙丘里出来的。

### II / II

===Original===
Late that night, when he was preparing for rest, the door of Fionn’s chamber opened gently and a young woman came into the room. The captain stared at her, as he well might, for he had never seen or imagined to see a woman so beautiful as this was. Indeed, she was not a woman, but a young girl, and her bearing was so gently noble, her look so modestly high, that the champion dared scarcely look at her, although he could not by any means have looked away.

===Chinese===
深夜，他正预备歇息，芬恩寝室的门轻轻开了，一位年轻女子走进屋来。统领直望着她，这也难怪，因为他从未见过、也从未想象过能见到这样美的女子。说实在的，她还算不得女子，而是一位少女；她的仪态那样温婉高贵，她的神情那样矜持清华，这位大勇士几乎不敢看她，却又无论如何也移不开目光。

===Original===
As she stood within the doorway, smiling, and shy as a flower, beautifully timid as a fawn, the Chief communed with his heart.

===Chinese===
她立在门内，含着笑，像一朵花那样羞怯，像一头小鹿那样美得惹怜；首领便与自己的心商量起来。

===Original===
“She is the Sky-woman of the Dawn,” he said. “She is the light on the foam. She is white and odorous as an apple-blossom. She smells of spice and honey. She is my beloved beyond the women of the world. She shall never be taken from me.”

===Chinese===
“她是黎明的天上女子，”他说，“她是浪沫上的光。她像苹果花一样洁白芬芳。她身上带着香料和蜂蜜的气息。普天之下的女子，我最爱的是她。谁也别想把她从我身边夺走。”

===Original===
And that thought was delight and anguish to him: delight because of such sweet prospect, anguish because it was not yet realised, and might not be.

===Chinese===
这个念头于他是欢喜，也是煎熬：欢喜，是因为有这样甜美的指望；煎熬，是因为它还未成真，而且说不定成不了真。

===Original===
As the dogs had looked at him on the chase with a look that he did not understand, so she looked at him, and in her regard there was a question that baffled him and a statement which he could not follow.

===Chinese===
正如追逐场上猎犬曾用他读不懂的目光望他，此刻她也这样望他；她的凝注里有一个难住他的问题，有一句他无从领会的话。

===Original===
He spoke to her then, mastering his heart to do it.

===Chinese===
于是他按住心潮，开口对她说话。

===Original===
“I do not seem to know you,” he said.

===Chinese===
“我好像不认识你。”他说。

===Original===
“You do not know me indeed,” she replied.

===Chinese===
“你确实不认识我。”她答道。

===Original===
“It is the more wonderful,” he continued gently, “for I should know every person that is here. What do you require from me?”

===Chinese===
“这就更奇了，”他温和地接着说，“这里的人我本该个个认得。你有什么要求我的？”

===Original===
“I beg your protection, royal captain.”

===Chinese===
“我恳求您的保护，尊贵的大统领。”

===Original===
“I give that to all,” he answered. “Against whom do you desire protection?”

===Chinese===
“这保护我给每一个人，”他答道，“你要提防的是谁？”

===Original===
“I am in terror of the Fear Doirche.”

===Chinese===
“我怕的是费尔·多尔切（Fear Doirche）。”

===Original===
“The Dark Man of the Shí?”

===Chinese===
“仙丘的黑暗之人？”

===Original===
“He is my enemy,” she said.

===Chinese===
“他是我的仇人。”她说。

===Original===
“He is mine now,” said Fionn. “Tell me your story.”

===Chinese===
“从今往后，他就是我的仇人。”芬恩说，“把你的来历讲给我听。”

===Original===
“My name is Saeve, and I am a woman of Faery,” she commenced. “In the Shí many men gave me their love, but I gave my love to no man of my country.”

===Chinese===
“我叫赛芙（Saeve），是仙境（Faery）的一位女子，”她开口道，“在仙丘，许多男子把爱给我，可我的爱，没有给我族中的任何男子。”

===Original===
“That was not reasonable,” the other chided with a blithe heart.

===Chinese===
“这可说不过去，”那一位嗔怪道，心里却轻快。

===Original===
“I was contented,” she replied, “and what we do not want we do not lack. But if my love went anywhere it went to a mortal, a man of the men of Ireland.”

===Chinese===
“我本是知足的，”她答道，“我们不想望的东西，便也不欠缺。可若说我的爱情去了哪里，它去给了一个凡人，一个爱尔兰的男人。”

===Original===
“By my hand,” said Fionn in mortal distress, “I marvel who that man can be!”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”芬恩痛苦万分地说，“我真想知道那男人是谁！”

===Original===
“He is known to you,” she murmured. “I lived thus in the peace of Faery, hearing often of my mortal champion, for the rumour of his great deeds had gone through the Shí, until a day came when the Black Magician of the Men of God put his eye on me, and, after that day, in whatever direction I looked I saw his eye.”

===Chinese===
“那人是您认得的，”她低声道，“我便这样住在仙境的安宁里，时常听到我那位凡人勇士的消息，因为他伟大功业的传闻，早已传遍了仙丘。直到有一天，众神之民的那位黑魔法师，把他的眼睛放到了我身上；从那天起，无论我朝哪个方向看，我总看见他的眼睛。”

===Original===
She stopped at that, and the terror that was in her heart was on her face.

===Chinese===
讲到这儿她停住了，心里的恐怖，全涌到了脸上。

===Original===
“He is everywhere,” she whispered. “He is in the bushes, and on the hill. He looked up at me from the water, and he stared down on me from the sky. His voice commands out of the spaces, and it demands secretly in the heart. He is not here or there, he is in all places at all times. I cannot escape from him,” she said, “and I am afraid,” and at that she wept noiselessly and stared on Fionn.

===Chinese===
“他无处不在，”她低声说，“他在灌木丛里，在山冈上。他从水里仰起头看我，他从天上俯下眼盯我。他的声音从虚空里发号施令，又在人心里暗暗索求。他不在东也不在西，他时时处处都在。我逃不脱他，”她说，“我好怕。”说到这里，她无声地哭了起来，眼睛直直望着芬恩。

===Original===
“He is my enemy,” Fionn growled. “I name him as my enemy.”

===Chinese===
“他是我的仇敌，”芬恩低吼道，“我指名他做我的仇敌。”

===Original===
“You will protect me?” she implored.

===Chinese===
“您会保护我的，是吗？”她恳求道。

===Original===
“Where I am let him not come,” said Fionn. “I also have knowledge. I am Fionn, the son of Uail, the son of Baiscne, a man among men and a god where the gods are.”

===Chinese===
“有我在，便容不得他来，”芬恩说，“我也有我的智识。我是芬恩，尤尔（Uail）之子、巴斯克内（Baiscne）之子的芬恩；在人们中间，是人中丈夫；在神们中间，便是神。”

===Original===
“He asked me in marriage,” she continued, “but my mind was full of my own dear hero, and I refused the Dark Man.”

===Chinese===
“他向我求过婚，”她接着说，“可我心里装着我自己的亲爱英雄，我便回绝了那黑暗之人。”

===Original===
“That was your right, and I swear by my hand that if the man you desire is alive and unmarried he shall marry you or he will answer to me for the refusal.”

===Chinese===
“那是你的权利。凭我的手起誓：你属意的男人若还活着、还未成婚，他就得娶你；不然，他为这次回绝，得向我交代。”

===Original===
“He is not married,” said Saeve, “and you have small control over him.”

===Chinese===
“他没有成婚，”赛芙说，“可您对他，管束不了多少。”

===Original===
The Chief frowned thoughtfully.

===Chinese===
首领蹙眉沉思。

===Original===
“Except the High King and the kings I have authority in this land.”

===Chinese===
“在这片土地上，除了至高王和诸位国王，我都有权柄。”

===Original===
“What man has authority over himself?” said Saeve.

===Chinese===
“哪个男人，又管得住自己呢？”赛芙说。

===Original===
“Do you mean that I am the man you seek?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“你是说，你找的男人就是我？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“It is to yourself I gave my love,” she replied.

===Chinese===
“我把爱情给的，正是您自己。”她答道。

===Original===
“This is good news,” Fionn cried joyfully, “for the moment you came through the door I loved and desired you, and the thought that you wished for another man went into my heart like a sword.”

===Chinese===
“这是天大的好消息！”芬恩欢喜地喊道，“你一进门，我便爱了你，恋了你；一想到你属意的是另一个男人，那念头就像一柄剑，插进了我的心。”

===Original===
Indeed, Fionn loved Saeve as he had not loved a woman before and would never love one again. He loved her as he had never loved anything before. He could not bear to be away from her. When he saw her he did not see the world, and when he saw the world without her it was as though he saw nothing, or as if he looked on a prospect that was bleak and depressing. The belling of a stag had been music to Fionn, but when Saeve spoke that was sound enough for him. He had loved to hear the cuckoo calling in the spring from the tree that is highest in the hedge, or the blackbird’s jolly whistle in an autumn bush, or the thin, sweet enchantment that comes to the mind when a lark thrills out of sight in the air and the hushed fields listen to the song. But his wife’s voice was sweeter to Fionn than the singing of a lark. She filled him with wonder and surmise. There was magic in the tips of her fingers. Her thin palm ravished him. Her slender foot set his heart beating; and whatever way her head moved there came a new shape of beauty to her face.

===Chinese===
确实，芬恩爱赛芙，是他从前不曾爱过一个女子那样爱，往后也不会再这样去爱一个。他爱她，胜过他此前爱过的一切。与她分开，他一时一刻也受不得。他望着她，便望不见世界；没有她而望着世界，便仿佛什么也没有见，仿佛望着一片荒寒败落的景象。牡鹿的吼鸣，在芬恩听来原是音乐，可赛芙一开口，那声音于他便尽够了。他原爱听春天里杜鹃在树篱最高枝头的啼叫，爱听秋丛里乌鸫欢快的哨音，爱听云雀直窜上高空、唱到望不见影、静默的田野侧耳听它歌唱时，浮上心头的那一缕又细又甜的迷醉——可他妻子的声音，在芬恩听来比云雀的歌唱还甜。她让他心中盈满惊奇与悬想。她的指尖上有魔法。她薄薄的掌心令他销魂。她纤细的脚，叫他的心怦然跳动；她的头无论怎样一转，脸上便有一种新的美生出来。

===Original===
“She is always new,” said Fionn. “She is always better than any other woman; she is always better than herself.”

===Chinese===
“她永远是新的，”芬恩说，“她永远比任何别的女子都好；她永远比她自己还好。”

===Original===
He attended no more to the Fianna. He ceased to hunt. He did not listen to the songs of poets or the curious sayings of magicians, for all of these were in his wife, and something that was beyond these was in her also.

===Chinese===
勇士团的事，他不再操心了。他不再行猎。诗人的歌、术士的奇言，他也都不听了，因为这一切都在他妻子身上，而且她身上，还有超出这一切的东西。

===Original===
“She is this world and the next one; she is completion,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“她就是这个世界，也是下一个世界；她就是圆满。”芬恩说。

### III / III

===Original===
It happened that the men of Lochlann came on an expedition against Ireland. A monstrous fleet rounded the bluffs of Ben Edair, and the Danes landed there, to prepare an attack which would render them masters of the country. Fionn and the Fianna-Finn marched against them. He did not like the men of Lochlann at any time, but this time he moved against them in wrath, for not only were they attacking Ireland, but they had come between him and the deepest joy his life had known.

===Chinese===
话说洛克兰（Lochlann）人有一回兴兵征讨爱尔兰。一支庞大的舰队绕过本·埃代尔（Ben Edair）的峭壁，丹麦人在那里登陆，准备发动一场攻势，好教自己成为这个国家的主人。芬恩与芬尼亚勇士团进军迎击。洛克兰人他平日就从来没有喜欢过，这一回他更是挟怒而往：因为他们不但攻打爱尔兰，还横在了他和此生所知的最深的欢喜中间。

===Original===
It was a hard fight, but a short one. The Lochlannachs were driven back to their ships, and within a week the only Danes remaining in Ireland were those that had been buried there.

===Chinese===
那一仗打得苦，却打得短。洛克兰人被赶回了船上；不出一个星期，留在爱尔兰的丹麦人，只剩下埋在那里的那些。

===Original===
That finished, he left the victorious Fianna and returned swiftly to the plain of Allen, for he could not bear to be one unnecessary day parted from Saeve.

===Chinese===
战事一了，他便撇下得胜的勇士团，急急赶回阿伦平原——与赛芙分开，多一日也是他受不得的。

===Original===
“You are not leaving us!” exclaimed Goll mac Morna.

===Chinese===
“您不是要这就走吧！”戈尔·麦克·莫尔纳（Goll mac Morna）叫道。

===Original===
“I must go,” Fionn replied.

===Chinese===
“我必须走。”芬恩答道。

===Original===
“You will not desert the victory feast,” Conán reproached him.

===Chinese===
“您总不至于撇下得胜宴。”柯南（Conán）责怪他。

===Original===
“Stay with us, Chief,” Caelte begged.

===Chinese===
“同我们留下吧，首领。”卡尔特（Caelte）恳求。

===Original===
“What is a feast without Fionn?” they complained.

===Chinese===
“没有芬恩，算得什么宴席？”众人抱怨。

===Original===
But he would not stay.

===Chinese===
可他不肯留下。

===Original===
“By my hand,” he cried, “I must go. She will be looking for me from the window.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”他喊道，“我非走不可。这会儿她该在窗口望我了。”

===Original===
“That will happen indeed,” Goll admitted.

===Chinese===
“那是真会的。”戈尔承认。

===Original===
“That will happen,” cried Fionn. “And when she sees me far out on the plain, she will run through the great gate to meet me.”

===Chinese===
“就是真会的！”芬恩喊道，“她一望见我在远远的平原上，就会奔出大门来迎我。”

===Original===
“It would be the queer wife would neglect that run,” Conán growled.

===Chinese===
“舍不得去跑那一趟的，才真是怪婆娘哩。”柯南咕哝道。

===Original===
“I shall hold her hand again,” Fionn entrusted to Caelte’s ear.

===Chinese===
“我又要握她的手了。”芬恩把这句话悄悄托付给卡尔特的耳朵。

===Original===
“You will do that, surely.”

===Chinese===
“您自然会的。”

===Original===
“I shall look into her face,” his lord insisted.

===Chinese===
“我要看她的脸。”这位主公坚持道。

===Original===
But he saw that not even beloved Caelte understood the meaning of that, and he knew sadly and yet proudly that what he meant could not be explained by anyone and could not be comprehended by anyone.

===Chinese===
可他看出来，连心爱的卡尔特也不懂这话的意思；他悲哀，却又自豪地知道：他的意思，谁也讲解不了，谁也领会不了。

===Original===
“You are in love, dear heart,” said Caelte.

===Chinese===
“您这是恋爱了，亲爱的。”卡尔特说。

===Original===
“In love he is,” Conán grumbled. “A cordial for women, a disease for men, a state of wretchedness.”

===Chinese===
“可不是恋爱了，”柯南嘟囔道，“这东西，于女人是一剂甜酒，于男人是一场病，是一种受苦受难的境况。”

===Original===
“Wretched in truth,” the Chief murmured. “Love makes us poor We have not eyes enough to see all that is to be seen, nor hands enough to seize the tenth of all we want. When I look in her eyes I am tormented because I am not looking at her lips, and when I see her lips my soul cries out, ‘Look at her eyes, look at her eyes.’ ”

===Chinese===
“受苦受难，一点不假，”首领喃喃道，“爱情叫我们变穷。我们眼睛不够多，看不尽要看的一切；手也不够多，抓不满想要的十分之一。我望着她的眼睛，便因没有望着她的唇而煎熬；我望着她的唇，灵魂又喊起来：‘看她的眼睛，看她的眼睛。’”

===Original===
“That is how it happens,” said Goll rememberingly.

===Chinese===
“就是这么一回事。”戈尔说，想起了从前。

===Original===
“That way and no other,” Caelte agreed.

===Chinese===
“就是这么一回事，不会有别的样。”卡尔特附和。

===Original===
And the champions looked backwards in time on these lips and those, and knew their Chief would go.

===Chinese===
勇士们就这样回望时光里这些和那些嘴唇，并且知道，他们的首领是留不住了。

---

===Original===
When Fionn came in sight of the great keep his blood and his feet quickened, and now and again he waved a spear in the air.

===Chinese===
芬恩望见那座大堡时，他的血和他的脚步一齐加快了，他还不时把长矛抡向空中挥舞。

===Original===
“She does not see me yet,” he thought mournfully.

===Chinese===
“她还没望见我。”他怏怏地想。

===Original===
“She cannot see me yet,” he amended, reproaching himself.

===Chinese===
“她这是还望不见我。”他改口道，怪着自己。

===Original===
But his mind was troubled, for he thought also, or he felt without thinking, that had the positions been changed he would have seen her at twice the distance.

===Chinese===
可他心里乱，因为他同时想到——或者不假思索地觉到——倘若两人的位置换个儿，隔着两倍远，他也早望见她了。

===Original===
“She thinks I have been unable to get away from the battle, or that I was forced to remain for the feast.”

===Chinese===
“她准以为我脱不开战事，或者，被强留着赴宴了。”

===Original===
And, without thinking it, he thought that had the positions been changed he would have known that nothing could retain the one that was absent.

===Chinese===
他不曾这么想，却又想到了：倘若两人换个儿位置，他自然知道，什么也留不住那个不在身边的人。

===Original===
“Women,” he said, “are shamefaced, they do not like to appear eager when others are observing them.”

===Chinese===
“女人家，”他说，“脸皮薄；别人望着她们的时候，她们不愿显得心急。”

===Original===
But he knew that he would not have known if others were observing him, and that he would not have cared about it if he had known. And he knew that his Saeve would not have seen, and would not have cared for any eyes than his.

===Chinese===
可他知道，换了他，别人望着他，他不会知道；就是知道了，也不会放在心上。他也知道，他的赛芙什么也不会看见——除了他的眼睛，谁的目光她也不会在意。

===Original===
He gripped his spear on that reflection, and ran as he had not run in his life, so that it was a panting, dishevelled man that raced heavily through the gates of the great Dun.

===Chinese===
这样一想，他攥紧了长矛，撒腿飞奔，跑得他这辈子不曾那样跑过；就这样，一个气喘吁吁、衣发蓬乱的汉子，脚步沉沉地冲进了大堡的门。

===Original===
Within the Dun there was disorder. Servants were shouting to one another, and women were running to and fro aimlessly, wringing their hands and screaming; and, when they saw the Champion, those nearest to him ran away, and there was a general effort on the part of every person to get behind every other person. But Fionn caught the eye of his butler, Gariv Cronán, the Rough Buzzer, and held it.

===Chinese===
堡里一片大乱。仆人们彼此呼喊，女人们没头没脑地跑来跑去，绞着手尖叫；一见到大勇士，离他最近的那些人扭头便跑，人人都在使足了劲往别人身后藏。芬恩一眼逮住了他的司酒官——绰号“嗡嗡粗嗓”的加里夫·克罗南（Gariv Cronán）——的目光，牢牢不放。

===Original===
“Come you here,” he said.

===Chinese===
“你过来。”他说。

===Original===
And the Rough Buzzer came to him without a single buzz in his body.

===Chinese===
于是“嗡嗡粗嗓”走到他跟前，浑身没有一丝嗡嗡。

===Original===
“Where is the Flower of Allen?” his master demanded.

===Chinese===
“阿伦之花（the Flower of Allen）在哪里？”主人质问。

===Original===
“I do not know, master,” the terrified servant replied.

===Chinese===
“我不知道，主人。”吓坏了的仆人答道。

===Original===
“You do not know!” said Fionn. “Tell what you do know.”

===Chinese===
“你不知道！”芬恩说，“那就把你知道的说出来。”

===Original===
And the man told him this story.

===Chinese===
于是那人对他讲了这个故事。

### IV / IV

===Original===
“When you had been away for a day the guards were surprised. They were looking from the heights of the Dun, and the Flower of Allen was with them. She, for she had a quest’s eye, called out that the master of the Fianna was coming over the ridges to the Dun, and she ran from the keep to meet you.”

===Chinese===
“您走后刚满一天，守卫们吃了一惊。他们正从大堡高处瞭望，阿伦之花也在他们中间。她生就一双寻望的慧眼，一眼望见勇士团的主人正翻过山脊朝大堡来，便奔出堡楼迎您去了。”

===Original===
“It was not I,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“那不是我。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“It bore your shape,” replied Gariv Cronán. “It had your armour and your face, and the dogs, Bran and Sceólan, were with it.”

===Chinese===
“它顶着您的身形，”加里夫·克罗南答道，“它披着您的铠甲，生着您的脸，布兰和斯科兰那两只狗，也在它身边。”

===Original===
“They were with me,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“它们是跟着我的。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“They seemed to be with it,” said the servant humbly.

===Chinese===
“它们瞧着，倒像是跟定了它。”仆人低声下气地说。

===Original===
“Tell us this tale,” cried Fionn.

===Chinese===
“把这事的经过讲来。”芬恩喊道。

===Original===
“We were distrustful,” the servant continued. “We had never known Fionn to return from a combat before it had been fought, and we knew you could not have reached Ben Edar or encountered the Lochlannachs. So we urged our lady to let us go out to meet you, but to remain herself in the Dun.”

===Chinese===
“我们起先是不信的，”仆人接着说，“我们从来没见芬恩在仗还没打完时便先回来，我们也知道，您无论如何到不了本·埃代尔，碰不上洛克兰人。所以我们劝夫人让我们出堡迎您，她自己留在堡里。”

===Original===
“It was good urging,” Fionn assented.

===Chinese===
“劝得在理。”芬恩点头。

===Original===
“She would not be advised,” the servant wailed. “She cried to us, ‘Let me go to meet my love.’ ”

===Chinese===
“她哪里肯听劝，”仆人哭诉道，“她朝我们喊：‘放我去迎我的爱人。’”

===Original===
“Alas!” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“唉！”芬恩说。

===Original===
“She cried on us, ‘Let me go to meet my husband, the father of the child that is not born.’ ”

===Chinese===
“她朝我们哭喊：‘放我去迎我的丈夫，我那未出生的孩子的父亲。’”

===Original===
“Alas!” groaned deep-wounded Fionn.

===Chinese===
“唉！”芬恩呻吟一声，伤透了心。

===Original===
“She ran towards your appearance that had your arms stretched out to her.”

===Chinese===
“她朝您那身影跑了过去——那身影正向她张开双臂。”

===Original===
At that wise Fionn put his hand before his eyes, seeing all that happened.

===Chinese===
听到这里，芬恩抬手掩住眼睛，一切经过都如在眼前。

===Original===
“Tell on your tale,” said he.

===Chinese===
“接着讲。”他说。

===Original===
“She ran to those arms, and when she reached them the figure lifted its hand. It touched her with a hazel rod, and, while we looked, she disappeared, and where she had been there was a fawn standing and shivering. The fawn turned and bounded towards the gate of the Dun, but the hounds that were by flew after her.”

===Chinese===
“她跑进那双臂膀，刚一到，那身影便抬起了手。它用一根榛木杖触了她一下，我们眼睁睁望着，她便不见了；她方才立着的地方，站着一头小鹿，浑身发抖。小鹿转身朝堡门蹦跳而去，跟在近旁的猎犬，飞也似的追了上去。”

===Original===
Fionn stared on him like a lost man.

===Chinese===
芬恩直瞪瞪望着他，像个失了魂魄的人。

===Original===
“They took her by the throat﻿—” the shivering servant whispered.

===Chinese===
“它们咬住了她的喉咙——”发抖的仆人低声说。

===Original===
“Ah!” cried Fionn in a terrible voice.

===Chinese===
“啊！”芬恩发出一声可怕的喊叫。

===Original===
“And they dragged her back to the figure that seemed to be Fionn. Three times she broke away and came bounding to us, and three times the dogs took her by the throat and dragged her back.”

===Chinese===
“它们把她拖回了那个貌似芬恩的身影跟前。她三次挣脱开，蹦跳着朝我们奔来，三次都被狗咬住喉咙，拖了回去。”

===Original===
“You stood to look!” the Chief snarled.

===Chinese===
“你们就站着看！”首领厉声道。

===Original===
“No, master, we ran, but she vanished as we got to her; the great hounds vanished away, and that being that seemed to be Fionn disappeared with them. We were left in the rough grass, staring about us and at each other, and listening to the moan of the wind and the terror of our hearts.”

===Chinese===
“没有，主人，我们跑了；可我们刚赶到她跟前，她便没了踪影；那两条大猎犬倏忽消散，那个貌似芬恩的东西也同它们一起不见了。只剩下我们待在荒草里，四下张望，面面相觑，听着风的呜咽，听着我们心里的惊惶。”

===Original===
“Forgive us, dear master,” the servant cried.

===Chinese===
“饶了我们吧，亲爱的主人。”仆人喊道。

===Original===
But the great captain made him no answer. He stood as though he were dumb and blind, and now and again he beat terribly on his breast with his closed fist, as though he would kill that within him which should be dead and could not die. He went so, beating on his breast, to his inner room in the Dun, and he was not seen again for the rest of that day, nor until the sun rose over Moy Lifé in the morning.

===Chinese===
可大统领没有回答他。他站在那里，像又哑又盲，时不时抡起握紧的拳头，狠狠捶打自己的胸膛，仿佛要打死他心里那个早该死了、却又死不了的东西。他就这样捶着胸膛，走进堡中自己的内室；那一日余下的时光，再没有人见过他，直到第二天清晨，太阳升上莫伊·利菲（Moy Lifé）平原。

### V / V

===Original===
For many years after that time, when he was not fighting against the enemies of Ireland, Fionn was searching and hunting through the length and breadth of the country in the hope that he might again chance on his lovely lady from the Shí. Through all that time he slept in misery each night and he rose each day to grief. Whenever he hunted he brought only the hounds that he trusted, Bran and Sceólan, Lomaire, Brod, and Lomlu; for if a fawn was chased each of these five great dogs would know if that was a fawn to be killed or one to be protected, and so there was small danger to Saeve and a small hope of finding her.

===Chinese===
那以后的许多年里，凡是不在与爱尔兰的仇敌作战的日子，芬恩就走遍全国的角角落落，搜寻、行猎，盼着能再有幸撞见他那位仙丘来的可爱小姐。那些年里，他夜夜含悲就寝，日日怀忧起身。行猎时，他只带他信得过的狗：布兰、斯科兰，还有洛迈雷（Lomaire）、布罗德（Brod）和洛姆卢（Lomlu）；因为一旦追逐小鹿，这五条大狗，条条都分得出哪头是该杀的小鹿，哪头是该保护的小鹿；所以赛芙难得有什么危险，也难得有什么被找到的指望。

---

===Original===
Once, when seven years had passed in fruitless search, Fionn and the chief nobles of the Fianna were hunting Ben Gulbain. All the hounds of the Fianna were out, for Fionn had now given up hope of encountering the Flower of Allen. As the hunt swept along the sides of the hill there arose a great outcry of hounds from a narrow place high on the slope and, over all that uproar there came the savage baying of Fionn’s own dogs.

===Chinese===
有一回，七年寻访一无所获之后，芬恩和勇士团的主要贵族在本·古尔本（Ben Gulbain）行猎。勇士团的猎犬倾巢而出，因为芬恩这时已断了再遇上阿伦之花的念头。猎队扫过山腰时，高坡上一处窄地里，蓦地腾起一片群犬的大乱，而压过那片喧嚣的，是芬恩自家的狗凶狂暴烈的吼吠。

===Original===
“What is this for?” said Fionn, and with his companions he pressed to the spot whence the noise came.

===Chinese===
“这是为的什么？”芬恩说，带着同伴们朝声音起处挤去。

===Original===
“They are fighting all the hounds of the Fianna,” cried a champion.

===Chinese===
“它们在跟勇士团所有的狗打呢！”一位勇士喊道。

===Original===
And they were. The five wise hounds were in a circle and were giving battle to an hundred dogs at once. They were bristling and terrible, and each bite from those great, keen jaws was woe to the beast that received it. Nor did they fight in silence as was their custom and training, but between each onslaught the great heads were uplifted, and they pealed loudly, mournfully, urgently, for their master.

===Chinese===
一点不假。那五条灵慧的狗站成一圈，同时迎战一百条狗。它们毛倒竖，貌狰狞，那几张巨口利齿咬下的每一口，挨着的畜生都要遭殃。它们也不像素来的习性和训练那样，默不作声地打；每一轮猛攻的间隙，那几颗巨头便扬起来，响亮地、悲切地、急迫地，呼唤它们的主人。

===Original===
“They are calling on me,” he roared.

===Chinese===
“它们在唤我！”他吼道。

===Original===
And with that he ran, as he had only once before run, and the men who were nigh to him went racing as they would not have run for their lives.

===Chinese===
说罢他便跑了起来——这样跑，他一生中只跑过一回——他身边的人也跟着狂奔，逃命也不曾跑得这样快。

===Original===
They came to the narrow place on the slope of the mountain, and they saw the five great hounds in a circle keeping off the other dogs, and in the middle of the ring a little boy was standing. He had long, beautiful hair, and he was naked. He was not daunted by the terrible combat and clamour of the hounds. He did not look at the hounds, but he stared like a young prince at Fionn and the champions as they rushed towards him scattering the pack with the butts of their spears. When the fight was over, Bran and Sceólan ran whining to the little boy and licked his hands.

===Chinese===
他们赶到山坡上那处窄地，只见五条大狗围成一圈，挡开其余的狗，圈子正中立着一个小男孩。他有一头长长的美发，浑身赤裸。猎犬们那可怕的搏斗与喧嚷，没有吓倒他。他不看猎犬，只像一位小王子似的，望着芬恩和众勇士——他们正朝他奔来，用矛杆把狗群拨得四散。斗罢，布兰和斯科兰呜呜咽咽地跑到小男孩跟前，舔他的双手。

===Original===
“They do that to no one,” said a bystander. “What new master is this they have found?”

===Chinese===
“它们对谁也不这样啊，”旁边有人说，“它们这是寻着了怎样一位新主人？”

===Original===
Fionn bent to the boy.

===Chinese===
芬恩朝男孩俯下身去。

===Original===
“Tell me, my little prince and pulse, what your name is, and how you have come into the middle of a hunting-pack, and why you are naked?”

===Chinese===
“告诉我，我的小王子、我的心跳：你叫什么名字？你怎么会到了一群猎狗中间？你又为什么光着身子？”

===Original===
But the boy did not understand the language of the men of Ireland. He put his hand into Fionn’s, and the Chief felt as if that little hand had been put into his heart. He lifted the lad to his great shoulder.

===Chinese===
可男孩听不懂爱尔兰人的语言。他把自己的手放进芬恩的手里，首领便觉得，那只小手一直放进了他的心里。他把孩子举上了自己宽大的肩头。

===Original===
“We have caught something on this hunt,” said he to Caelte mac Ronán. “We must bring this treasure home. You shall be one of the Fianna-Finn, my darling,” he called upwards.

===Chinese===
“这一趟行猎，我们打着好东西了，”他对卡尔特·麦克·罗南（Caelte mac Ronán）说，“这件宝贝，得带回家去。你就要做芬尼亚勇士团的一员啦，我的宝贝。”他仰起头朝上喊道。

===Original===
The boy looked down on him, and in the noble trust and fearlessness of that regard Fionn’s heart melted away.

===Chinese===
男孩低头看他，那目光里高贵的信赖与毫无畏惧，把芬恩的心整个儿融化了。

===Original===
“My little fawn!” he said.

===Chinese===
“我的小鹿！”他说。

===Original===
And he remembered that other fawn. He set the boy between his knees and stared at him earnestly and long.

===Chinese===
他想起了另一头小鹿。他把孩子夹在两膝之间，恳切地、久久地端详着他。

===Original===
“There is surely the same look,” he said to his wakening heart; “that is the very eye of Saeve.”

===Chinese===
“这眉眼真是像极了，”他对自家那颗惊醒的心说，“这正是赛芙的那双眼睛。”

===Original===
The grief flooded out of his heart as at a stroke, and joy foamed into it in one great tide. He marched back singing to the encampment, and men saw once more the merry Chief they had almost forgotten.

===Chinese===
悲愁仿佛一刀挥去，一泻而空；欢喜像一股大潮，翻腾着灌进心房。他一路唱着歌走回营地，人们又见到了那位几乎已经忘却的快活首领。

### VI / VI

===Original===
Just as at one time he could not be parted from Saeve, so now he could not be separated from this boy. He had a thousand names for him, each one more tender than the last: “My Fawn, My Pulse, My Secret Little Treasure,” or he would call him “My Music, My Blossoming Branch, My Store in the Heart, My Soul.” And the dogs were as wild for the boy as Fionn was. He could sit in safety among a pack that would have torn any man to pieces, and the reason was that Bran and Sceólan, with their three whelps, followed him about like shadows. When he was with the pack these five were with him, and woeful indeed was the eye they turned on their comrades when these pushed too closely or were not properly humble. They thrashed the pack severally and collectively until every hound in Fionn’s kennels knew that the little lad was their master, and that there was nothing in the world so sacred as he was.

===Chinese===
从前他一时离不开赛芙，如今他一时离不开这个孩子。他给这孩子起过一千个名字，一个比一个更疼：“我的小鹿，我的心跳，我秘密的小珍宝”；要不然，便唤他“我的乐音，我繁花满枝的枝条，我心底的宝藏，我的灵魂”。狗们疼这孩子，也疼得同芬恩一样疯魔。他可以安安稳稳坐在一群本会把任何人大卸八块的猎狗中间，只因布兰和斯科兰带着它们的三只幼崽，像影子一样寸步不离地跟着他。他在狗群里时，这五只便在他身边；同伴们若是挤得太近，或是谦卑得不够分寸，它们转过头去望同伴的那一眼，可真叫它们受用不起。它们把狗群一条条地揍，也成群地揍，直到芬恩狗栏里的每一条猎狗都知道：这小家伙是它们的主人，世上再没有什么，比他更神圣。

===Original===
In no long time the five wise hounds could have given over their guardianship, so complete was the recognition of their young lord. But they did not so give over, for it was not love they gave the lad but adoration.

===Chinese===
用不了多久，这五条灵慧的狗本可以把守护的差事交出去——狗群认它们的小主人，已经认得再齐全不过。可它们没有交出去，因为它们给这孩子的，不是爱，是崇拜。

===Original===
Fionn even may have been embarrassed by their too close attendance. If he had been able to do so he might have spoken harshly to his dogs, but he could not; it was unthinkable that he should; and the boy might have spoken harshly to him if he had dared to do it. For this was the order of Fionn’s affection: first there was the boy; next, Bran and Sceólan with their three whelps; then Caelte mac Ronán, and from him down through the champions. He loved them all, but it was along that precedence his affections ran. The thorn that went into Bran’s foot ran into Fionn’s also. The world knew it, and there was not a champion but admitted sorrowfully that there was reason for his love.

===Chinese===
它们跟得这样紧，连芬恩怕都有些窘了。倘若办得到，他也许会狠狠地说他的狗几句，可他办不到；那样想都是想不得的；那孩子呢，若是敢，倒也许会狠狠说他几句。因为芬恩的情爱有这样一个位次：第一是孩子；其次是布兰、斯科兰和它们的三只幼崽；再次是卡尔特·麦克·罗南，从他往下，才数到众勇士。他们他个个都爱，可他的情爱，正是顺着这个位次流的。扎进布兰脚里的刺，也扎进了芬恩的脚里。世人都知道这件事；勇士们没有一个不黯然承认：他这爱，爱得有理。

===Original===
Little by little the boy came to understand their speech and to speak it himself, and at last he was able to tell his story to Fionn.

===Chinese===
男孩一点一点听懂了他们的话，又一点一点自己学会了说；终于，他能把他的来历讲给芬恩听了。

===Original===
There were many blanks in the tale, for a young child does not remember very well. Deeds grow old in a day and are buried in a night. New memories come crowding on old ones, and one must learn to forget as well as to remember. A whole new life had come on this boy, a life that was instant and memorable, so that his present memories blended into and obscured the past, and he could not be quite sure if that which he told of had happened in this world or in the world he had left.

===Chinese===
那来历里有许多空白，因为小孩子是记不很清的。事迹一天就老，一夜便埋。新的记忆挤着旧的记忆，人除了学会记住，还得学会忘却。一种全新的生活落到这孩子身上，那生活一刻一新、事事难忘，于是眼前的记忆渐渐混进过去、把过去遮暗了，他自己也拿不准：他讲的那些事，究竟是发生在这个世界，还是发生在他离开的那个世界。

### VII / VII

===Original===
“I used to live,” he said, “in a wide, beautiful place. There were hills and valleys there, and woods and streams, but in whatever direction I went I came always to a cliff, so tall it seemed to lean against the sky, and so straight that even a goat would not have imagined to climb it.”

===Chinese===
“我从前住的，”他说，“是一片宽广美丽的地方。那里有山有谷，有树林有溪流；可无论我朝哪个方向走，最后总走到一面峭壁跟前——那壁高得像斜倚着天，直得连山羊也起不了攀爬的念头。”

===Original===
“I do not know of any such place,” Fionn mused.

===Chinese===
“我不知道有这样的地方。”芬恩沉吟道。

===Original===
“There is no such place in Ireland,” said Caelte, “but in the Shí there is such a place.”

===Chinese===
“爱尔兰没有这样的地方，”卡尔特说，“可仙丘里有。”

===Original===
“There is in truth,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“确实有。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“I used to eat fruits and roots in the summer,” the boy continued, “but in the winter food was left for me in a cave.”

===Chinese===
“夏天我吃野果和草根，”男孩接着说，“可一到冬天，便有吃食留在一个山洞里给我。”

===Original===
“Was there no one with you?” Fionn asked.

===Chinese===
“没有人同你在一起吗？”芬恩问。

===Original===
“No one but a deer that loved me, and that I loved.”

===Chinese===
“没有；只有一头爱我的母鹿，我也爱她。”

===Original===
“Ah me!” cried Fionn in anguish, “tell me your tale, my son.”

===Chinese===
“哎呀，我啊！”芬恩痛楚地喊道，“把你的事讲给我听，我的儿。”

===Original===
“A dark stern man came often after us, and he used to speak with the deer. Sometimes he talked gently and softly and coaxingly, but at times again he would shout loudly and in a harsh, angry voice. But whatever way he talked the deer would draw away from him in dread, and he always left her at last furiously.”

===Chinese===
“有一个黑黢黢、恶狠狠的男人，常常来追我们，他总跟那母鹿说话。有时他讲得温温软软、千哄万哄，有时又扯开嗓子，恶声恶气地嚷。可无论他怎样讲，母鹿总是吓得躲开他；他到最后，总是怒冲冲地离开她。”

===Original===
“It is the Dark Magician of the Men of God,” cried Fionn despairingly.

===Chinese===
“这是众神之民的那位黑魔法师！”芬恩绝望地喊道。

===Original===
“It is indeed, my soul,” said Caelte.

===Chinese===
“正是他，我的魂灵。”卡尔特说。

===Original===
“The last time I saw the deer,” the child continued, “the dark man was speaking to her. He spoke for a long time. He spoke gently and angrily, and gently and angrily, so that I thought he would never stop talking, but in the end he struck her with a hazel rod, so that she was forced to follow him when he went away. She was looking back at me all the time and she was crying so bitterly that anyone would pity her. I tried to follow her also, but I could not move, and I cried after her too, with rage and grief, until I could see her no more and hear her no more. Then I fell on the grass, my senses went away from me, and when I awoke I was on the hill in the middle of the hounds where you found me.”

===Chinese===
“我最后一回见到那母鹿时，”孩子接着说，“那黑人正在对她说话。他说了很久很久。他一会儿温和，一会儿忿怒，温和了又忿怒，忿怒了又温和，说得我以为他永远不会住口了。可末了他举起榛木杖打了她；他走的时候，她只得跟了他去。她一路回头望着我，哭得那样苦，谁见了都要心疼她。我也想跟了她去，可我动弹不得；我也在她身后哭喊，又是怒，又是悲，直到再也望不见她，再也听不见她。后来我倒在草地上，知觉离开了我；等我醒来，我已经在山上，在狗群当中，就在你们找到我的地方。”

===Original===
That was the boy whom the Fianna called Oisín, or the Little Fawn. He grew to be a great fighter afterwards, and he was the chief maker of poems in the world. But he was not yet finished with the Shí. He was to go back into Faery when the time came, and to come thence again to tell these tales, for it was by him these tales were told.

===Chinese===
这就是勇士团的人唤作奥辛（Oisín）、又叫作“小鹿”的那个孩子。他后来长成了一位了不起的战士，又是世上首屈一指的作诗人。可他同仙丘的因缘，还没有了呢结。时候一到，他还要回仙境去，日后还要从那里再来，讲这些故事——因为这些故事，正是由他讲出来的。

## The Birth of Bran / 布兰的诞生

### I / I

===Original===
There are people who do not like dogs a bit﻿—they are usually women﻿—but in this story there is a man who did not like dogs. In fact, he hated them. When he saw one he used to go black in the face, and he threw rocks at it until it got out of sight. But the Power that protects all creatures had put a squint into this man’s eye, so that he always threw crooked.

===Chinese===
世上有一些人一点儿也不喜欢狗——这种人通常是女人——不过在这个故事里，却有一个不喜欢狗的男人。何止是不喜欢，他恨狗。一看见狗，他的脸就会黑下来，还会朝它扔石头，一直扔到它逃出视线才罢。好在庇佑众生的大能者给这个人的一只眼睛安上了斜视，所以他扔石头向来扔歪。

===Original===
This gentleman’s name was Fergus Fionnliath, and his stronghold was near the harbour of Galway. Whenever a dog barked he would leap out of his seat, and he would throw everything that he owned out of the window in the direction of the bark. He gave prizes to servants who disliked dogs, and when he heard that a man had drowned a litter of pups he used to visit that person and try to marry his daughter.

===Chinese===
这位先生名叫费格斯·芬利亚斯（Fergus Fionnliath），他的城堡就在戈尔韦（Galway）港湾附近。只要有一声狗叫，他立刻从座位上蹦起来，把自家的全部家当一股脑儿朝狗叫的方向扔出窗外。谁家的仆人不喜欢狗，他赏谁奖品；听说有谁淹死过一窝小狗崽，他必定登门拜访，还想方设法要娶那人的女儿。

===Original===
Now Fionn, the son of Uail, was the reverse of Fergus Fionnliath in this matter, for he delighted in dogs, and he knew everything about them from the setting of the first little white tooth to the rocking of the last long yellow one. He knew the affections and antipathies which are proper in a dog; the degree of obedience to which dogs may be trained without losing their honourable qualities or becoming servile and suspicious; he knew the hopes that animate them, the apprehensions which tingle in their blood, and all that is to be demanded from, or forgiven in, a paw, an ear, a nose, an eye, or a tooth; and he understood these things because he loved dogs, for it is by love alone that we understand anything.

===Chinese===
芬恩（Fionn），尤尔（Uail）之子，在这件事上恰恰与费格斯·芬利亚斯相反，因为他天性爱狗：从狗长出第一颗小小的白牙，到最后一颗长长的黄牙松动，狗的事他无所不晓。他懂得狗天性里该有的亲爱与嫌恶；懂得狗可以驯到几分顺从，才既不失体面，又不至于落得奴颜婢膝、疑神疑鬼；他知道它们怀着怎样的希望，知道怎样的惶恐在它们的血液里嗡嗡震颤；一只爪子、一只耳朵、一个鼻子、一只眼睛、一颗牙齿，能要求些什么、又能原谅些什么，他全都有数。他懂得这一切，是因为他爱狗——天下的事，唯有凭着爱，才能真正懂得。

===Original===
Among the three hundred dogs which Fionn owned there were two to whom he gave an especial tenderness, and who were his daily and nightly companions. These two were Bran and Sceólan, but if a person were to guess for twenty years he would not find out why Fionn loved these two dogs and why he would never be separated from them.

===Chinese===
芬恩养的狗有三百条，其中有两只他疼得格外深，日夜与它们相伴。这两只，就是布兰（Bran）和斯科兰（Sceólan）。可是芬恩为什么偏偏爱这两只狗、为什么永远不肯与它们分开——任谁猜上二十年，也猜不出个所以然来。

---

===Original===
Fionn’s mother, Muirne, went to wide Allen of Leinster to visit her son, and she brought her young sister Tuiren with her. The mother and aunt of the great captain were well treated among the Fianna, first, because they were parents to Fionn, and second, because they were beautiful and noble women.

===Chinese===
芬恩的母亲穆尔娜（Muirne）到伦斯特（Leinster）广阔的阿伦（Allen）去看望儿子，还带上了自己年轻的妹妹蒂伦（Tuiren）。大统领的母亲和姨母在芬尼亚勇士团（Fianna）里备受礼遇：一来她们是芬恩的亲长，二来她们本就是又美丽又高贵的女子。

===Original===
No words can describe how delightful Muirne was﻿—she took the branch; and as to Tuiren, a man could not look at her without becoming angry or dejected. Her face was fresh as a spring morning; her voice more cheerful than the cuckoo calling from the branch that is highest in the hedge; and her form swayed like a reed and flowed like a river, so that each person thought she would surely flow to him.

===Chinese===
穆尔娜可爱到什么地步，没有言语能够形容——花枝全让她一人摘了去。至于蒂伦，男人只要看她一眼，不是心头冒火，便是垂头丧气。她的脸像春天的清晨一样新鲜；她的声音比树篱最高枝上啼叫的杜鹃还要欢快；她的身姿像芦苇般摇曳，像河水般流淌，人人都觉得她一定会朝自己流过来。

===Original===
Men who had wives of their own grew moody and downcast because they could not hope to marry her, while the bachelors of the Fianna stared at each other with truculent, bloodshot eyes, and then they gazed on Tuiren so gently that she may have imagined she was being beamed on by the mild eyes of the dawn.

===Chinese===
有妻室的男人们因为再无指望娶她，一个个郁郁寡欢、没精打采；勇士团的单身汉们则先是你瞪我、我瞪你，一双双凶眼布满血丝、杀气腾腾，随后望向蒂伦时，目光却又那般温柔，弄得她多半以为自己正沐在黎明那双温和的眼睛的光里。

===Original===
It was to an Ulster gentleman, Iollan Eachtach, that she gave her love, and this chief stated his rights and qualities and asked for her in marriage.

===Chinese===
她把爱情给了阿尔斯特（Ulster）的一位绅士，约兰·埃赫塔赫（Iollan Eachtach）；这位首领便陈述了自己的身份、权利和种种好处，向她求婚。

===Original===
Now Fionn did not dislike the man of Ulster, but either he did not know them well or else he knew them too well, for he made a curious stipulation before consenting to the marriage. He bound Iollan to return the lady if there should be occasion to think her unhappy, and Iollan agreed to do so. The sureties to this bargain were Caelte mac Ronan, Goll mac Morna, and Lugaidh. Lugaidh himself gave the bride away, but it was not a pleasant ceremony for him, because he also was in love with the lady, and he would have preferred keeping her to giving her away. When she had gone he made a poem about her, beginning:

===Chinese===
芬恩倒不是讨厌这个阿尔斯特人，可要么是他不甚了解阿尔斯特人，要么是了解得太透，总之他在应允婚事之前，提出了一条奇怪的条件：日后若发觉小姐过得不幸福，约兰必须把她送还。约兰应承了下来。这笔契约的担保人是卡尔特·麦克·罗南（Caelte mac Ronan）、戈尔·麦克·莫尔纳（Goll mac Morna）和卢盖德（Lugaidh）。新娘便由卢盖德亲手交了出去，可这场典礼对他实在算不得愉快，因为他也爱着这位小姐；比起把她交出去，他宁愿把她留下。她走之后，他为她作了一首诗，开头一句是：

===Original===
> “There is no more light in the sky﻿—”

===Chinese===
> “天空里再没有光亮——”

===Original===
And hundreds of sad people learned the poem by heart.

===Chinese===
数百个伤心人把这首诗背了下来。

### II / II

===Original===
When Iollan and Tuiren were married they went to Ulster, and they lived together very happily. But the law of life is change; nothing continues in the same way for any length of time; happiness must become unhappiness, and will be succeeded again by the joy it had displaced. The past also must be reckoned with; it is seldom as far behind us as we could wish: it is more often in front, blocking the way, and the future trips over it just when we think that the road is clear and joy our own.

===Chinese===
约兰和蒂伦成婚后去了阿尔斯特，两人过得非常幸福。然而生命的法则就是变化；没有什么能长长久久一成不变；幸福必会变成不幸，而被幸福挤走的那份欢乐，日后又会回来接替不幸。过去也是一笔要清算的账；它很少像我们指望的那样远远落在身后：它更多的时候横在前头，挡住去路，正当我们以为道路已清、欢乐在握，未来却在它身上绊了一跤。

===Original===
Iollan had a past. He was not ashamed of it; he merely thought it was finished, although in truth it was only beginning, for it is that perpetual beginning of the past that we call the future.

===Chinese===
约兰就有那么一段过去。他并不为之羞愧；他只是以为它已经完结，殊不知它才刚刚开始，因为过去那永无休止的重新开始，正是我们所谓的未来。

===Original===
Before he joined the Fianna he had been in love with a lady of the Shí, named Uct Dealv (Fair Breast), and they had been sweethearts for years. How often he had visited his sweetheart in Faery! With what eagerness and anticipation he had gone there; the lover’s whistle that he used to give was known to every person in that Shí, and he had been discussed by more than one of the delicate sweet ladies of Faery.

===Chinese===
加入勇士团之前，他爱过仙丘（the Shí）的一位女子，名叫乌克特·迪亚尔夫（Uct Dealv），意即“玉乳”；两人做了多年的情人。他曾多少次去仙境（Faery）探望心上人啊！去时怀着怎样的急切与期盼；他惯打的那声情郎呼哨，那座仙丘里人人耳熟；仙境里娇美甜柔的淑女，也不止一位谈论过他。

===Original===
“That is your whistle, Fair Breast,” her sister of the Shí would say.

===Chinese===
“那是吹给你的呼哨了，‘玉乳’。”她仙丘里的姊妹总会这么说。

===Original===
And Uct Dealv would reply:

===Chinese===
乌克特·迪亚尔夫便回答：

===Original===
“Yes, that is my mortal, my lover, my pulse, and my one treasure.”

===Chinese===
“是啊，那是我的凡人，我的情郎，我的脉搏，我的独一珍宝。”

===Original===
She laid her spinning aside, or her embroidery if she was at that, or if she were baking a cake of fine wheaten bread mixed with honey she would leave the cake to bake itself and fly to Iollan. Then they went hand in hand in the country that smells of apple-blossom and honey, looking on heavy-boughed trees and on dancing and beaming clouds. Or they stood dreaming together, locked in a clasping of arms and eyes, gazing up and down on each other, Iollan staring down into sweet grey wells that peeped and flickered under thin brows, and Uct Dealv looking up into great black ones that went dreamy and went hot in endless alternation.

===Chinese===
她放下手里的纺活，若正做着刺绣，便放下刺绣；若正烤一块掺了蜂蜜的精白麦面饼，便随那饼自己去烤，人已飞到约兰身边。随后他们手牵着手，走在弥漫着苹果花香与蜂蜜香的乡野里，看枝头沉沉的大树，看翩翩起舞、流光溢彩的云。再不然，他们便相拥而立，同做一场梦：臂膀相缠，目光相扣，彼此上下打量——约兰俯视着细眉底下两汪窥望闪烁的甜甜灰井，乌克特·迪亚尔夫仰望着两口大黑井，那井一阵恍惚，一阵炽热，循环不休。

===Original===
Then Iollan would go back to the world of men, and Uct Dealv would return to her occupations in the Land of the Ever Young.

===Chinese===
而后约兰便回到人类的世界，乌克特·迪亚尔夫便回到永青之国（the Land of the Ever Young），继续操持她的活计。

===Original===
“What did he say?” her sister of the Shí would ask.

===Chinese===
“他说了什么？”她仙丘里的姊妹会问。

===Original===
“He said I was the Berry of the Mountain, the Star of Knowledge, and the Blossom of the Raspberry.”

===Chinese===
“他说我是山间的莓果，是知识的星辰，是覆盆子的花朵。”

===Original===
“They always say the same thing,” her sister pouted.

===Chinese===
“他们说的永远是这一套。”姊妹撅着嘴说。

===Original===
“But they look other things,” Uct Dealv insisted. “They feel other things,” she murmured; and an endless conversation recommenced.

===Chinese===
“可他们眼睛里说的是另一套，”乌克特·迪亚尔夫不依道，“他们心里觉着的是另一套。”她低声说；于是一场没完没了的谈话又开了头。

===Original===
Then for some time Iollan did not come to Faery, and Uct Dealv marvelled at that, while her sister made an hundred surmises, each one worse than the last.

===Chinese===
后来有一阵子，约兰不来仙境了，乌克特·迪亚尔夫深为纳罕，她的姊妹则一口气做了一百种猜测，一种比一种不堪。

===Original===
“He is not dead or he would be here,” she said. “He has forgotten you, my darling.”

===Chinese===
“他没死，死了他也会到这儿来，”她说，“他是把你忘了，我的宝贝。”

===Original===
News was brought to Tlr na n-Og of the marriage of Iollan and Tuiren, and when Uct Dealv heard that news her heart ceased to beat for a moment, and she closed her eyes.

===Chinese===
约兰和蒂伦成婚的消息传到了提尔纳诺格（Tír na n-Og）。乌克特·迪亚尔夫一听见这消息，心口停跳了一瞬，随即闭上了眼睛。

===Original===
“Now!” said her sister of the Shí. “That is how long the love of a mortal lasts,” she added, in the voice of sad triumph which is proper to sisters.

===Chinese===
“瞧见了吧！”她仙丘里的姊妹说。“凡人的爱情，就长这么久。”她又添了一句，用的是姊妹们当有的那种悲哀的得意腔调。

===Original===
But on Uct Dealv there came a rage of jealousy and despair such as no person in the Shí had ever heard of, and from that moment she became capable of every ill deed; for there are two things not easily controlled, and they are hunger and jealousy. She determined that the woman who had supplanted her in Iollan’s affections should rue the day she did it. She pondered and brooded revenge in her heart, sitting in thoughtful solitude and bitter collectedness until at last she had a plan.

===Chinese===
可是一股妒火与绝望涌上了乌克特·迪亚尔夫的心头，那烈焰之猛，仙丘里闻所未闻；从那一刻起，她变得什么坏事都干得出来；世上有两样东西最难得管束，一是饥饿，二是嫉妒。她打定了主意：那个在约兰心里取她而代之的女人，定要叫她为自己干下的事悔之无及。她独坐凝思，孤寂沉沉，苦涩里透着一股镇定，把复仇在心里掂了又掂、酿了又酿，终于想出了一条计策。

===Original===
She understood the arts of magic and shape-changing, so she changed her shape into that of Fionn’s female runner, the best-known woman in Ireland; then she set out from Faery and appeared in the world. She travelled in the direction of Iollan’s stronghold.

===Chinese===
她精通法术与变形之道，于是把自己变作芬恩的女信使——爱尔兰最有名的女子——的模样；随后她动身离开仙境，现身人间，朝约兰的城堡一路行去。

===Original===
Iollan knew the appearance of Fionn’s messenger, but he was surprised to see her.

===Chinese===
约兰认得芬恩这位信使的模样，可见了她还是吃了一惊。

===Original===
She saluted him.

===Chinese===
她向他行礼问安。

===Original===
“Health and long life, my master.”

===Chinese===
“愿您康健长寿，我的主人。”

===Original===
“Health and good days,” he replied. “What brings you here, dear heart?”

===Chinese===
“愿您康健，日日安好，”他答道，“什么风把您吹来了，亲爱的？”

===Original===
“I come from Fionn.”

===Chinese===
“我从芬恩那里来。”

===Original===
“And your message?” said he.

===Chinese===
“带的什么口信？”他说。

===Original===
“The royal captain intends to visit you.”

===Chinese===
“尊贵的大统领要来拜访您了。”

===Original===
“He will be welcome,” said Iollan. “We shall give him an Ulster feast.”

===Chinese===
“欢迎之至，”约兰说，“我们要请他吃一顿阿尔斯特盛宴。”

===Original===
“The world knows what that is,” said the messenger courteously. “And now,” she continued, “I have messages for your queen.”

===Chinese===
“那是怎样一顿盛宴，普天之下无人不晓，”信使彬彬有礼地说，“眼下嘛，”她接着说，“我还有口信要带给您的王后。”

===Original===
Tuiren then walked from the house with the messenger, but when they had gone a short distance Uct Dealv drew a hazel rod from beneath her cloak and struck it on the queen’s shoulder, and on the instant Tuiren’s figure trembled and quivered, and it began to whirl inwards and downwards, and she changed into the appearance of a hound.

===Chinese===
于是蒂伦随信使走出了屋子。两人走了没多远，乌克特·迪亚尔夫便从斗篷底下抽出一根榛木杖，击在王后肩头。刹那之间，蒂伦的身形颤抖、战栗起来，开始向里、向下旋卷，转眼变了猎犬的模样。

===Original===
It was sad to see the beautiful, slender dog standing shivering and astonished, and sad to see the lovely eyes that looked out pitifully in terror and amazement. But Uct Dealv did not feel sad. She clasped a chain about the hound’s neck, and they set off westward towards the house of Fergus Fionnliath, who was reputed to be the unfriendliest man in the world to a dog. It was because of his reputation that Uct Dealv was bringing the hound to him. She did not want a good home for this dog: she wanted the worst home that could be found in the world, and she thought that Fergus would revenge for her the rage and jealousy which she felt towards Tuiren.

===Chinese===
看着那美丽纤瘦的狗站在那里瑟瑟发抖、一脸愕然，叫人心酸；看着那双可爱的眼睛在恐惧与惊惶中向外投来哀怜的一瞥，也叫人心酸。乌克特·迪亚尔夫却半点不心酸。她把一条链子扣上猎犬的脖子，动身向西，往费格斯·芬利亚斯的宅子去了——论对狗的不友善，此人在天底下是出了名的第一。乌克特·迪亚尔夫把猎犬往他那儿送，图的正是这名声。她不想给这只狗找个好归宿：她要给它找全天下最坏的归宿；她还指望，费格斯能替她报掉她对蒂伦的那一腔妒火与怨毒。

### III / III

===Original===
As they paced along Uct Dealv railed bitterly against the hound, and shook and jerked her chain. Many a sharp cry the hound gave in that journey, many a mild lament.

===Chinese===
一路行去，乌克特·迪亚尔夫恶狠狠地数落猎犬，又扯又拽它的链子。那一程路上，猎犬发出了一阵阵尖利的哀叫，一阵阵低低的悲鸣。

===Original===
“Ah, supplanter! Ah, taker of another girl’s sweetheart!” said Uct Dealv fiercely. “How would your lover take it if he could see you now? How would he look if he saw your pointy ears, your long thin snout, your shivering, skinny legs, and your long grey tail. He would not love you now, bad girl!”

===Chinese===
“好啊，你这取而代之的！好啊，你这抢人家情郎的！”乌克特·迪亚尔夫狠声骂道。“你的情郎要是瞧见你这副模样，会怎么想？他要是看见你这对尖耳朵、你这又长又尖的嘴筒子、你这两条打颤的瘦腿、你这条长长的灰尾巴，他会是什么脸色？他如今可不爱你了，坏丫头！”

===Original===
“Have you heard of Fergus Fionnliath,” she said again, “the man who does not like dogs?”

===Chinese===
“你听说过费格斯·芬利亚斯吗？”她又说，“那个不喜欢狗的人？”

===Original===
Tuiren had indeed heard of him.

===Chinese===
蒂伦确实听说过他。

===Original===
“It is to Fergus I shall bring you,” cried Uct Dealv. “He will throw stones at you. You have never had a stone thrown at you. Ah, bad girl! You do not know how a stone sounds as it nips the ear with a whirling buzz, nor how jagged and heavy it feels as it thumps against a skinny leg. Robber! Mortal! Bad girl! You have never been whipped, but you will be whipped now. You shall hear the song of a lash as it curls forward and bites inward and drags backward. You shall dig up old bones stealthily at night, and chew them against famine. You shall whine and squeal at the moon, and shiver in the cold, and you will never take another girl’s sweetheart again.”

===Chinese===
“我就是要带你去费格斯那儿，”乌克特·迪亚尔夫嚷道。“他要朝你扔石头。你可从来没挨过石头砸。啊，坏丫头！你不知道石头打着旋儿贴着耳朵呼啸掠过时是什么声响，也不知道它砸上一条瘦腿时又是什么滋味——又棱又沉。强盗！凡人！坏丫头！你没挨过鞭子，可你这就快挨上了。你会听见鞭子的歌——它朝前卷，往里咬，向后拖。你要在夜里偷偷刨出陈年的老骨头，嚼着它们熬饥荒。你要对着月亮呜咽哀号，在寒风里发抖——看你往后还敢不敢再抢人家姑娘的情郎！”

===Original===
And it was in those terms and in that tone that she spoke to Tuiren as they journeyed forward, so that the hound trembled and shrank, and whined pitifully and in despair.

===Chinese===
一路上，她便是用这样的话语、这样的腔调对蒂伦说话，说得那猎犬又抖又缩，呜呜哀叫，叫得可怜，叫得绝望。

===Original===
They came to Fergus Fionnliath’s stronghold, and Uct Dealv demanded admittance.

===Chinese===
两人来到费格斯·芬利亚斯的城堡，乌克特·迪亚尔夫要求进门。

===Original===
“Leave that dog outside,” said the servant.

===Chinese===
“把那狗留在外头。”仆人说。

===Original===
“I will not do so,” said the pretended messenger.

===Chinese===
“我不把它留下。”假信使说。

===Original===
“You can come in without the dog, or you can stay out with the dog,” said the surly guardian.

===Chinese===
“你要么丢下狗自己进来，要么就带着狗待在外头。”没好气的看门人说。

===Original===
“By my hand,” cried Uct Dealv, “I will come in with this dog, or your master shall answer for it to Fionn.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”乌克特·迪亚尔夫嚷道，“我非要带着这条狗进去不可，不然你家主人就得去跟芬恩交代！”

===Original===
At the name of Fionn the servant almost fell out of his standing. He flew to acquaint his master, and Fergus himself came to the great door of the stronghold.

===Chinese===
一听芬恩的名字，那仆人险些没站稳。他飞奔去禀报主人，费格斯亲自来到城堡的大门前。

===Original===
“By my faith,” he cried in amazement, “it is a dog.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的信义，”他惊呼道，“这是条狗。”

===Original===
“A dog it is,” growled the glum servant.

===Chinese===
“是条狗。”阴沉沉的仆人咕哝道。

===Original===
“Go you away,” said Fergus to Uct Dealv, “and when you have killed the dog come back to me and I will give you a present.”

===Chinese===
“你走吧，”费格斯对乌克特·迪亚尔夫说，“等把狗弄死了，再回来见我，我送你一件礼物。”

===Original===
“Life and health, my good master, from Fionn, the son of Uail, the son of Baiscne,” said she to Fergus.

===Chinese===
“我的好主人，尤尔之子、巴斯克内（Baiscne）之子芬恩，祝您生命与康健。”她对费格斯说。

===Original===
“Life and health back to Fionn,” he replied. “Come into the house and give your message, but leave the dog outside, for I don’t like dogs.”

===Chinese===
“也祝芬恩生命与康健，”他答道，“进屋传你的口信吧，可把狗留在外头，我不喜欢狗。”

===Original===
“The dog comes in,” the messenger replied.

===Chinese===
“狗要进来。”信使答道。

===Original===
“How is that?” cried Fergus angrily.

===Chinese===
“这算什么话！”费格斯怒道。

===Original===
“Fionn sends you this hound to take care of until he comes for her,” said the messenger.

===Chinese===
“芬恩把这条猎犬托付给您照看，等他来领它。”信使说。

===Original===
“I wonder at that,” Fergus growled, “for Fionn knows well that there is not a man in the world has less of a liking for dogs than I have.”

===Chinese===
“这就怪了，”费格斯咕哝道，“芬恩明明知道，天底下再没有第二个人比我更不喜欢狗了。”

===Original===
“However that may be, master, I have given Fionn’s message, and here at my heel is the dog. Do you take her or refuse her?”

===Chinese===
“不管怎么说，主人，芬恩的口信我带到了，狗就在我脚边。您是收下它，还是回绝它？”

===Original===
“If I could refuse anything to Fionn it would be a dog,” said Fergus, “but I could not refuse anything to Fionn, so give me the hound.”

===Chinese===
“芬恩的事若有一件我回绝得了，那准是狗，”费格斯说，“可芬恩的事我一件也回绝不了。把猎犬给我吧。”

===Original===
Uct Dealv put the chain in his hand.

===Chinese===
乌克特·迪亚尔夫把链子放进他手里。

===Original===
“Ah, bad dog!” said she.

===Chinese===
“哼，坏狗！”她说。

===Original===
And then she went away well satisfied with her revenge, and returned to her own people in the Shí.

===Chinese===
随后她扬长而去，对这报复十二分满意，回仙丘她的族人那里去了。

### IV / IV

===Original===
On the following day Fergus called his servant.

===Chinese===
第二天，费格斯唤来仆人。

===Original===
“Has that dog stopped shivering yet?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“那条狗还抖不抖了？”他问。

===Original===
“It has not, sir,” said the servant.

===Chinese===
“还抖着呢，老爷。”仆人说。

===Original===
“Bring the beast here,” said his master, “for whoever else is dissatisfied Fionn must be satisfied.”

===Chinese===
“把那畜生带来，”主人说，“旁人满不满意且不管，芬恩可得满意。”

===Original===
The dog was brought, and he examined it with a jaundiced and bitter eye.

===Chinese===
狗带来了，他用一双挑剔刻毒的眼睛把它打量了一番。

===Original===
“It has the shivers indeed,” he said.

===Chinese===
“它果真在抖呢。”他说。

===Original===
“The shivers it has,” said the servant.

===Chinese===
“抖是抖的。”仆人说。

===Original===
“How do you cure the shivers?” his master demanded, for he thought that if the animal’s legs dropped off Fionn would not be satisfied.

===Chinese===
“这抖可怎么治？”主人质问，因为他寻思，这畜生的腿要是抖掉了，芬恩可就不会满意了。

===Original===
“There is a way,” said the servant doubtfully.

===Chinese===
“倒有个法子。”仆人迟疑着说。

===Original===
“If there is a way, tell it to me,” cried his master angrily.

===Chinese===
“有法子就快说！”主人生气地喊道。

===Original===
“If you were to take the beast up in your arms and hug it and kiss it, the shivers would stop,” said the man.

===Chinese===
“您要是把那畜生抱进怀里，搂搂它，亲亲它，抖就止住了。”那人说。

===Original===
“Do you mean﻿—?” his master thundered, and he stretched his hand for a club.

===Chinese===
“你是说——？”主人雷霆大发，伸手便去够棍子。

===Original===
“I heard that,” said the servant humbly.

===Chinese===
“我是听人这么说的。”仆人低声下气地说。

===Original===
“Take that dog up,” Fergus commanded, “and hug it and kiss it, and if I find a single shiver left in the beast I’ll break your head.”

===Chinese===
“把那狗抱起来，”费格斯喝令，“搂它，亲它；这畜生身上我若再找出半点儿哆嗦，就敲碎你的脑袋。”

===Original===
The man bent to the hound, but it snapped a piece out of his hand, and nearly bit his nose off as well.

===Chinese===
那人俯身去抱猎犬，它一口咬掉他手上一块肉，差点连鼻子也咬了下来。

===Original===
“That dog doesn’t like me,” said the man.

===Chinese===
“这狗不喜欢我。”那人说。

===Original===
“Nor do I,” roared Fergus; “get out of my sight.”

===Chinese===
“我也不喜欢它，”费格斯吼道，“滚出我的视线！”

===Original===
The man went away and Fergus was left alone with the hound, but the poor creature was so terrified that it began to tremble ten times worse than before.

===Chinese===
那人走了，屋里只剩费格斯和猎犬。可这可怜的生灵吓坏了，抖得比先前还厉害十倍。

===Original===
“Its legs will drop off,” said Fergus. “Fionn will blame me,” he cried in despair.

===Chinese===
“它的腿要抖掉了，”费格斯说，“芬恩要怪罪我了。”他绝望地喊道。

===Original===
He walked to the hound.

===Chinese===
他朝猎犬走去。

===Original===
“If you snap at my nose, or if you put as much as the start of a tooth into the beginning of a finger!” he growled.

===Chinese===
“你敢咬我的鼻子，敢让半颗牙尖碰上半个指头尖！”他低吼道。

===Original===
He picked up the dog, but it did not snap, it only trembled. He held it gingerly for a few moments.

===Chinese===
他抱起了狗。狗没有咬，只是发抖。他小心翼翼地捧了它片刻。

===Original===
“If it has to be hugged,” he said, “I’ll hug it. I’d do more than that for Fionn.”

===Chinese===
“要是非搂不可，”他说，“那我就搂。为了芬恩，比这更过分的事我也干得出来。”

===Original===
He tucked and tightened the animal into his breast, and marched moodily up and down the room. The dog’s nose lay along his breast under his chin, and as he gave it dutiful hugs, one hug to every five paces, the dog put out its tongue and licked him timidly under the chin.

===Chinese===
他把那畜生掖进怀里搂紧，闷闷地在屋里来回踱步。狗鼻子贴着他的胸膛，卧在他下巴底下；他每走五步，便尽责地搂它一记，狗则伸出舌头，怯怯地舔他下巴底下。

===Original===
“Stop,” roared Fergus, “stop that forever,” and he grew very red in the face, and stared truculently down along his nose. A soft brown eye looked up at him and the shy tongue touched again on his chin.

===Chinese===
“停下，”费格斯吼道，“这一套永远给我停下，”他的脸涨得通红，顺着鼻梁凶巴巴地朝下瞪。一只温柔的棕色眼睛仰头望着他，那羞怯的舌头又在他下巴上碰了一下。

===Original===
“If it has to be kissed,” said Fergus gloomily, “I’ll kiss it; I’d do more than that for Fionn,” he groaned.

===Chinese===
“要是非亲不可，”费格斯阴郁地说，“那我就亲；为了芬恩，比这更过分的事我也干得出来。”他哼哼道。

===Original===
He bent his head, shut his eyes, and brought the dog’s jaw against his lips. And at that the dog gave little wriggles in his arms, and little barks, and little licks, so that he could scarcely hold her. He put the hound down at last.

===Chinese===
他低下头，闭上眼，把狗嘴贴上了自己的嘴唇。那狗顿时在他怀里扭动起来，小声地汪汪，轻轻地舔他，闹得他险些抱它不住。他终于把猎犬放了下来。

===Original===
“There is not a single shiver left in her,” he said.

===Chinese===
“它身上一抖也不剩了。”他说。

===Original===
And that was true.

===Chinese===
这话不假。

===Original===
Everywhere he walked the dog followed him, giving little prances and little pats against him, and keeping her eyes fixed on his with such eagerness and intelligence that he marvelled.

===Chinese===
他走到哪儿，狗跟到哪儿，一路小蹦小跳，不时轻轻扑蹭他，一双眼睛急切又灵慧地盯住他的眼睛，看得他啧啧称奇。

===Original===
“That dog likes me,” he murmured in amazement.

===Chinese===
“这狗喜欢我。”他喃喃自语，惊诧不已。

===Original===
“By my hand,” he cried next day, “I like that dog.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”第二天他嚷道，“我也喜欢这狗了。”

===Original===
The day after that he was calling her “My One Treasure, My Little Branch.” And within a week he could not bear her to be out of his sight for an instant.

===Chinese===
又过一天，他已经“我的独一珍宝”“我的小花枝”地唤它了。不出一个星期，他连让它离开自己视线一瞬都受不了了。

===Original===
He was tormented by the idea that some evil person might throw a stone at the hound, so he assembled his servants and retainers and addressed them.

===Chinese===
有个念头老折磨着他：生怕哪个歹人朝猎犬扔石头。于是他把仆人门客统统召来，对他们训话。

===Original===
He told them that the hound was the Queen of Creatures, the Pulse of his Heart, and the Apple of his Eye, and he warned them that the person who as much as looked sideways on her, or knocked one shiver out of her, would answer for the deed with pains and indignities. He recited a list of calamities which would befall such a miscreant, and these woes began with flaying and ended with dismemberment, and had inside bits of such complicated and ingenious torment that the blood of the men who heard it ran chill in their veins, and the women of the household fainted where they stood.

===Chinese===
他告诉他们，这条猎犬是万灵的女王，是他心尖的搏动，是他眼中的明珠；他又警告众人：谁敢斜眼瞟它一下，敢把它吓得掉出一丝颤，谁就要为这行径领受皮肉之苦和种种凌辱。他历数了将降临到这等恶徒头上的灾祸——这些祸事从剥皮开始，以分尸收场，中间还夹着一段又一段精巧繁复的酷刑——听得男人们血液在血管里发凉，女眷们当场晕倒在地。

### V / V

===Original===
In course of time the news came to Fionn that his mother’s sister was not living with Iollan. He at once sent a messenger calling for fulfilment of the pledge that had been given to the Fianna, and demanding the instant return of Tuiren. Iollan was in a sad condition when this demand was made. He guessed that Uct Dealv had a hand in the disappearance of his queen, and he begged that time should be given him in which to find the lost girl. He promised if he could not discover her within a certain period that he would deliver his body into Fionn’s hands, and would abide by whatever judgement Fionn might pronounce. The great captain agreed to that.

===Chinese===
后来，消息传到芬恩那里：他的姨母没有和约兰住在一起。他当即派出信使，要求兑现当年向勇士团立下的担保，责令即刻送还蒂伦。这道要求传来，约兰的处境好不凄惶。他猜到王后的失踪必有乌克特·迪亚尔夫的手脚，便恳求宽限些时日，容他去寻找失踪的小姐。他许诺：若在期限之内找不回她，便把自己的身体交到芬恩手里，任凭芬恩发落，怎样裁决都甘受。大统领应允了。

===Original===
“Tell the wife-loser that I will have the girl or I will have his head,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“告诉那个丢了老婆的家伙：我要不到人，就要他的脑袋。”芬恩说。

===Original===
Iollan set out then for Faery. He knew the way, and in no great time he came to the hill where Uct Dealv was.

===Chinese===
约兰随即动身前往仙境。他认得路，没过多久便到了乌克特·迪亚尔夫所住的山丘。

===Original===
It was hard to get Uct Dealv to meet him, but at last she consented, and they met under the apple boughs of Faery.

===Chinese===
乌克特·迪亚尔夫好说歹说不肯见他，最后总算应允了，两人在仙境的苹果树枝下见了面。

===Original===
“Well!” said Uct Dealv. “Ah! Breaker of Vows and Traitor to Love,” said she.

===Chinese===
“好啊！”乌克特·迪亚尔夫说。“啊！背誓的人，爱情的叛徒。”她说。

===Original===
“Hail and a blessing,” said Iollan humbly.

===Chinese===
“向您问安，求您赐福。”约兰谦卑地说。

===Original===
“By my hand,” she cried, “I will give you no blessing, for it was no blessing you left with me when we parted.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”她嚷道，“我一个祝福也不会给你，当初我们分手，你留给我的可不是祝福。”

===Original===
“I am in danger,” said Iollan.

===Chinese===
“我大难临头了。”约兰说。

===Original===
“What is that to me?” she replied fiercely.

===Chinese===
“那与我何干？”她狠狠地答道。

===Original===
“Fionn may claim my head,” he murmured.

===Chinese===
“芬恩兴许要取我的脑袋。”他低声道。

===Original===
“Let him claim what he can take,” said she.

===Chinese===
“他拿得动什么，就让他要什么。”她说。

===Original===
“No,” said Iollan proudly, “he will claim what I can give.”

===Chinese===
“不，”约兰傲然道，“他要的，得是我给得起的。”

===Original===
“Tell me your tale,” said she coldly.

===Chinese===
“把你的事讲来听听。”她冷冷地说。

===Original===
Iollan told his story then, and, he concluded, “I am certain that you have hidden the girl.”

===Chinese===
于是约兰讲了自己的遭遇，末了说道：“我敢肯定，是你把小姐藏起来了。”

===Original===
“If I save your head from Fionn,” the woman of the Shí replied, “then your head will belong to me.”

===Chinese===
“要是我从芬恩手里救下你的脑袋，”那位仙丘女子答道，“你的脑袋可就归我了。”

===Original===
“That is true,” said Iollan.

===Chinese===
“这话不假。”约兰说。

===Original===
“And if your head is mine, the body that goes under it is mine. Do you agree to that?”

===Chinese===
“脑袋既然归我，底下安着的身子也就归我。你应不应承？”

===Original===
“I do,” said Iollan.

===Chinese===
“应承。”约兰说。

===Original===
“Give me your pledge,” said Uct Dealv, “that if I save you from this danger you will keep me as your sweetheart until the end of life and time.”

===Chinese===
“给我立个誓约，”乌克特·迪亚尔夫说，“倘若我救你脱了这场大难，你要留我做你的心上人，直到生命与时间的尽头。”

===Original===
“I give that pledge,” said Iollan.

===Chinese===
“我立这个誓约。”约兰说。

===Original===
Uct Dealv went then to the house of Fergus Fionnliath, and she broke the enchantment that was on the hound, so that Tuiren’s own shape came back to her; but in the matter of two small whelps, to which the hound had given birth, the enchantment could not be broken, so they had to remain as they were. These two whelps were Bran and Sceólan. They were sent to Fionn, and he loved them for ever after, for they were loyal and affectionate, as only dogs can be, and they were as intelligent as human beings. Besides that, they were Fionn’s own cousins.

===Chinese===
随后乌克特·迪亚尔夫来到费格斯·芬利亚斯的宅子，解开了加在猎犬身上的魔法，蒂伦便找回了自己的身形；只是猎犬生下的那两只小犬崽，魔法在它们身上解不开，它们只好永远保持原样。这两只犬崽，就是布兰和斯科兰。它们被送去给芬恩，从此永远得了他的疼爱：它们忠心耿耿，一往情深，唯有狗才能那样；它们又跟人一样聪明。不但如此，它们还是芬恩的嫡亲表亲呢。

===Original===
Tuiren was then asked in marriage by Lugaidh who had loved her so long. He had to prove to her that he was not any other woman’s sweetheart, and when he proved that they were married, and they lived happily ever after, which is the proper way to live. He wrote a poem beginning:

===Chinese===
随后，爱了她那么久的卢盖德向蒂伦求婚了。他得向她证明，自己不是任何别的女人的情郎；证明之后，两人成了婚，从此幸福地生活在一起——日子原该这么过。他写了一首诗，开头是：

===Original===
> “Lovely the day. Dear is the eye of the dawn﻿—”

===Chinese===
> “白昼何其可爱。黎明的眼睛何其珍贵——”

===Original===
And a thousand merry people learned it after him.
