“朋友们，勇士们，”康恩说，“今夜，米德纳之子艾伦将从斯利夫·富阿德（Slieve Fuaid）携着玄冥可怕的火，前来进犯我们的城。你们中间，可有热爱塔拉与国王的人，肯挺身担当，替我们抵御那个东西？”

===Original===
He spoke in silence, and when he had finished he listened to the same silence, but it was now deep, ominous, agonized. Each man glanced uneasily on his neighbour and then stared at his wine-cup or his fingers. The hearts of young men went hot for a gallant moment and were chilled in the succeeding one, for they had all heard of Aillen out of Shí Finnachy in the north. The lesser gentlemen looked under their brows at the greater champions, and these peered furtively at the greatest of all. Art og mac Morna of the Hard Strokes fell to biting his fingers, Conán the Swearer and Garra mac Morna grumbled irritably to each other and at their neighbours, even Caelte, the son of Ronán, looked down into his own lap, and Goll Mor sipped at his wine without any twinkle in his eye. A horrid embarrassment came into the great hall, and as the High King stood in that palpitating silence his noble face changed from kindly to grave and from that to a terrible sternness. In another moment, to the undying shame of every person present, he would have been compelled to lift his own challenge and declare himself the champion of Tara for that night, but the shame that was on the faces of his people would remain in the heart of their king. Goll’s merry mind would help him to forget, but even his heart would be wrung by a memory that he would not dare to face. It was at that terrible moment that Fionn stood up.

===Chinese===
他在寂静里说了话；说完了，他听见的还是那片寂静，只是这寂静如今深了，不祥了，痛楚了。人人先不安地瞟一眼身旁的人，随即盯住自己的酒杯或手指。年轻人们的心豪迈地热了一瞬，下一瞬又凉了，因为北方芬纳希仙丘的艾伦，他们全都听说过。次一等的绅士们压着眉去看大勇士，大勇士们又偷偷去觑最最大的那几位。“重击”的阿特·奥格·麦克·莫尔纳低头咬起手指，起誓者柯南和加拉·麦克·莫尔纳烦躁地互相嘀咕，又冲着邻座嘀咕；连罗南之子卡尔特都低头看自己的膝盖了；戈尔·麦克·莫尔纳抿着酒，眼里不见一丝笑意。一场可怕的难堪落进了大厅。至高王站在那片颤巍巍的寂静里，高贵的脸从和蔼转为凝重，又从凝重转为可怕的严峻。再有一瞬，他就不得不自己接下这挑战、自封今夜的塔拉勇士了——在场的每一个人，将为此蒙受不灭的羞耻；而这份涂在他子民脸上的羞耻，也会留在他们王的心里。戈尔那副快活心肠会帮他忘掉，可就连他的心，也会被一段他不敢面对的记忆揪痛。正是在那可怕的一刻，芬恩站了起来。

===Original===
“What,” said he, “will be given to the man who undertakes this defence?”

===Chinese===
“敢问，”他说，“担当这场守御的人，给的是什么？”

===Original===
“All that can be rightly asked will be royally bestowed,” was the king’s answer.

===Chinese===
“凡问得名正言顺的，都将以王者之度相赐。”王这样回答。

===Original===
“Who are the sureties?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“担保人是谁？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“The kings of Ireland, and Red Cith with his magicians.”

===Chinese===
“爱尔兰的诸王，还有红发基斯（Red Cith）和他的术士们。”

===Original===
“I will undertake the defence,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“这守御，我来担当。”芬恩说。

===Original===
And on that, the kings and magicians who were present bound themselves to the fulfilment of the bargain.

===Chinese===
当下，在场的诸王与术士便立约担保，必履行这笔交易。

===Original===
Fionn marched from the banqueting hall, and as he went, all who were present of nobles and retainers and servants acclaimed him and wished him luck. But in their hearts they were bidding him goodbye, for all were assured that the lad was marching to a death so unescapeable that he might already be counted as a dead man.

===Chinese===
芬恩大步走出宴厅。一路上，在场的贵族、门客、仆役无不向他欢呼祝运。可他们心里都在跟他道别，因为人人都拿得稳：这少年走向的是一场逃不掉的死，干脆现在就把他算作死人好了。

===Original===
It is likely that Fionn looked for help to the people of the Shí themselves, for, through his mother, he belonged to the tribes of Dana, although, on the father’s side, his blood was well compounded with mortal clay. It may be, too, that he knew how events would turn, for he had eaten the Salmon of Knowledge. Yet it is not recorded that on this occasion he invoked any magical art as he did on other adventures.

===Chinese===
芬恩多半是指望仙丘中人亲自来相助的，因为论母系，他原属达南神族的部族，虽然父系这一半，他的血里也实实在在掺着凡人的泥土。也可能，事情将怎样收场他早已知道，因为他吃过智慧鲑鱼。然而记载上说，这一回他并没有像别的历险里那样，动用什么法术。

===Original===
Fionn’s way of discovering whatever was happening and hidden was always the same and is many times referred to. A shallow, oblong dish of pure, pale gold was brought to him. This dish was filled with clear water. Then Fionn would bend his head and stare into the water, and as he stared he would place his thumb in his mouth under his “Tooth of Knowledge,” his “wisdom tooth.”

===Chinese===
芬恩窥探一切正在发生与隐藏之事的法子，永远是一个，记载里提过许多回。有人给他端来一只浅浅的长方形盘子，纯的淡金打的。盘里盛满清水。于是芬恩低下头，凝望水中；望着望着，他把大拇指衔进嘴里，压在那颗“智慧之牙”底下——他的那颗“智齿”。

===Original===
Knowledge, may it be said, is higher than magic and is more to be sought. It is quite possible to see what is happening and yet not know what is forward, for while seeing is believing it does not follow that either seeing or believing is knowing. Many a person can see a thing and believe a thing and know just as little about it as the person who does neither. But Fionn would see and know, or he would understand a decent ratio of his visions. That he was versed in magic is true, for he was ever known as the Knowledgeable man, and later he had two magicians in his household named Dirim and mac-Reith to do the rough work of knowledge for their busy master.

===Chinese===
要说一句：知识高过法术，更值得追求。看见正在发生的事，却不知道前头是什么，这是大有可能的；眼见为实是不错，可眼见也罢，相信也罢，都不等于知晓。多少人看得见一件事、也相信一件事，对它的了解却同既不看也不信的人一样少。可芬恩是既看且知的；就算不能全知，他也能从幻象里读出个像样的成数。他精通法术，这不假——他向来以“智者”闻名；后来他家里还养着两名术士，一个叫迪里姆（Dirim），一个叫麦克-雷斯（mac-Reith），替他们忙碌的主人干知识里的粗活。

===Original===
It was not from the Shí, however, that assistance came to Fionn.

===Chinese===
然而，来助芬恩的，却不是仙丘。

### XIII / XIII

===Original===
He marched through the successive fortifications until he came to the outer, great wall, the boundary of the city, and when he had passed this he was on the wide plain of Tara.

===Chinese===
他穿过一道又一道的堡垒，来到最外面的大墙——京城的边界；跨出墙，他便站上了塔拉宽阔的平原。

===Original===
Other than himself no person was abroad, for on the night of the Feast of Samhain none but a madman would quit the shelter of a house even if it were on fire; for whatever disasters might be within a house would be as nothing to the calamities without it.

===Chinese===
除了他，没有一个人在外头走动；因为萨温节庆的夜里，除非是疯子，谁也不会离开屋子的庇护，哪怕屋子着了火。屋里无论藏着什么灾祸，跟屋外的祸殃一比，都不算什么。

===Original===
The noise of the banquet was not now audible to Fionn﻿—it is possible, however, that there was a shamefaced silence in the great hall﻿—and the lights of the city were hidden by the successive great ramparts. The sky was over him; the earth under him; and than these there was nothing, or there was but the darkness and the wind.

===Chinese===
宴席的喧闹，这时芬恩已听不见了——不过大厅里说不定正是一片羞惭的寂静——城里的灯火，也叫一道道大垒遮没了。天在他头顶上；地在他脚底下；除了这些，再没有别的，或者说，只剩下黑暗和风。

===Original===
But darkness was not a thing to terrify him, bred in the nightness of a wood and the very fosterling of gloom; nor could the wind afflict his ear or his heart. There was no note in its orchestra that he had not brooded on and become, which becoming is magic. The long-drawn moan of it; the thrilling whisper and hush; the shrill, sweet whistle, so thin it can scarcely be heard, and is taken more by the nerves than by the ear; the screech, sudden as a devil’s yell and loud as ten thunders; the cry as of one who flies with backward look to the shelter of leaves and darkness; and the sob as of one stricken with an age-long misery, only at times remembered, but remembered then with what a pang! His ear knew by what successions they arrived, and by what stages they grew and diminished. Listening in the dark to the bundle of noises which make a noise he could disentangle them and assign a place and a reason to each gradation of sound that formed the chorus: there was the patter of a rabbit, and there the scurrying of a hare; a bush rustled yonder, but that brief rustle was a bird; that pressure was a wolf, and this hesitation a fox; the scraping yonder was but a rough leaf against bark, and the scratching beyond it was a ferret’s claw.

===Chinese===
可黑暗吓不着他：他是在林子的夜色里生、幽暗的膝下养大的；风也折磨不了他的耳朵和心。风的乐队里没有一个音，他不曾细细揣摩过、不曾化进去过——而这样的化入，便是法术。风那拖长的呜咽；那颤栗的低语与屏息；那尖细甜润的呼哨，细得几乎听不见，神经收到的比耳朵更多；那声尖啸，突兀如魔鬼的吼，响亮如十个雷；那一声哭喊，像有人频频回头、逃向叶影与黑暗的庇护；还有那一声呜咽，像有人害着一场与生俱来的长苦，平日里不常想起，可一旦想起，是怎样一阵锥心！这些声音循着怎样的次第到来，又循着怎样的阶梯起落消长，他的耳朵一清二楚。在黑暗里听着那一束汇成一片响动的声响，他能把它们一一解开，给合唱里每一层声响派定位置、派定缘由：那儿是兔子轻轻的扑踏，那儿是野兔急急的窜奔；那边的灌木响了一下，可那一小声是只鸟；那一记沉稳的压步是狼，这一下踌躇是狐狸；那边刮的一声不过是一片枯叶擦过树皮，再过去一点的窸窣，是一只雪貂的爪子。

===Original===
Fear cannot be where knowledge is, and Fionn was not fearful.

===Chinese===
知识所在之处容不下恐惧，而芬恩不恐惧。

===Original===
His mind, quietly busy on all sides, picked up one sound and dwelt on it. “A man,” said Fionn, and he listened in that direction, back towards the city.

===Chinese===
他的心思在四面八方安静地忙碌，拣起了一个声音，停在它上面。“有个人。”芬恩说，便朝那个方向——朝城的方向——听去。

===Original===
A man it was, almost as skilled in darkness as Fionn himself.

===Chinese===
确实是个人，在黑暗里几乎同芬恩本人一样老练。

===Original===
“This is no enemy,” Fionn thought; “his walking is open.”

===Chinese===
“这不是敌人，”芬恩想，“他走路是敞亮的。”

===Original===
“Who comes?” he called.

===Chinese===
“来者何人？”他喊道。

===Original===
“A friend,” said the newcomer.

===Chinese===
“是朋友。”来人说。

===Original===
“Give a friend’s name,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“是朋友就报上名来。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“Fiacuil mac Cona,” was the answer.

===Chinese===
“菲亚库尔·麦克·科纳。”对方答道。

===Original===
“Ah, my pulse and heart!” cried Fionn, and he strode a few paces to meet the great robber who had fostered him among the marshes.

===Chinese===
“啊，我的脉搏，我的心肝！”芬恩喊道，大步迎上那位在沼泽间把他养大的大盗。

===Original===
“So you are not afraid,” he said joyfully.

===Chinese===
“原来你不怕啊。”他欢欢喜喜地说。

===Original===
“I am afraid in good truth,” Fiacuil whispered, “and the minute my business with you is finished I will trot back as quick as legs will carry me. May the gods protect my going as they protected my coming,” said the robber piously.

===Chinese===
“我怕，怕得货真价实，”菲亚库尔悄声道，“跟你的事一了，我这双腿能跑多快就跑多快。愿诸神保佑我回去，像保佑我来时一样。”大盗虔敬地说。

===Original===
“Amen,” said Fionn, “and now, tell me what you have come for?”

===Chinese===
“阿门，”芬恩说，“现在说说吧，你为什么来？”

===Original===
“Have you any plan against this lord of the Shl?” Fiacuil whispered.

===Chinese===
“对这位仙丘之主，你有打算吗？”菲亚库尔低声问。

===Original===
“I will attack him,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“我要攻击他。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“That is not a plan,” the other groaned, “we do not plan to deliver an attack but to win a victory.”

===Chinese===
“那不算打算，”对方哼道，“我们筹算的，不是打出一场攻击，而是赢下一场胜利。”

===Original===
“Is this a very terrible person?” Fionn asked.

===Chinese===
“这是个非常可怕的人物吗？”芬恩问。

===Original===
“Terrible indeed. No one can get near him or away from him. He comes out of the Shí playing sweet, low music on a timpan and a pipe, and all who hear this music fall asleep.”

===Chinese===
“可怕极了。没人近得了他的身，也没人脱得了他的身。他从仙丘里出来，用一张蒂姆潘琴（timpan）和一支管笛，奏着又甜又低的曲子，谁听见这音乐，谁就睡着。”

===Original===
“I will not fall asleep,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“我不会睡着。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“You will indeed, for everybody does.”

===Chinese===
“你一定会的，人人如此。”

===Original===
“What happens then?” Fionn asked.

===Chinese===
“然后呢？”芬恩问。

===Original===
“When all are asleep Aillen mac Midna blows a dart of fire out of his mouth, and everything that is touched by that fire is destroyed, and he can blow his fire to an incredible distance and to any direction.”

===Chinese===
“等人都睡熟了，艾伦·麦克·米德纳就从嘴里喷出一镖火，火沾着什么，什么就毁了；他那一口火，喷得出叫人不敢信的远，还随他高兴朝哪个方向喷。”

===Original===
“You are very brave to come to help me,” Fionn murmured, “especially when you are not able to help me at all.”

===Chinese===
“你真勇敢，肯来帮我，”芬恩喃喃道，“尤其是你压根儿帮不上忙。”

===Original===
“I can help,” Fiacuil replied, “but I must be paid.”

===Chinese===
“帮得上，”菲亚库尔答道，“可我得收酬劳。”

===Original===
“What payment?”

===Chinese===
“什么酬劳？”

===Original===
“A third of all you earn and a seat at your council.”

===Chinese===
“你挣来的一切，三分之一归我，外加你议事会里一个席位。”

===Original===
“I grant that,” said Fionn, “and now, tell me your plan?”

===Chinese===
“准了，”芬恩说，“现在，说说你的打算？”

===Original===
“You remember my spear with the thirty rivets of Arabian gold in its socket?”

===Chinese===
“还记得我那杆枪吗？枪库里钉着三十颗阿拉伯金铆钉的？”

===Original===
“The one,” Fionn queried, “that had its head wrapped in a blanket and was stuck in a bucket of water and was chained to a wall as well﻿—the venomous Birgha?”

===Chinese===
“就是那杆，”芬恩问，“枪头裹着毯子、插在水桶里、还要在墙上拴一道链子的——那杆毒物比尔加（Birgha）？”

===Original===
“That one,” Fiacuil replied.

===Chinese===
“就是那杆。”菲亚库尔答。

===Original===
“It is Aillen mac Midna’s own spear,” he continued, “and it was taken out of his Shí by your father.”

===Chinese===
“它是艾伦·麦克·米德纳自家的枪，”他接着说，“是你父亲从他的仙丘里取出来的。”

===Original===
“Well?” said Fionn, wondering nevertheless where Fiacuil got the spear, but too generous to ask.

===Chinese===
“哦？”芬恩说。他心里纳闷菲亚库尔这杆枪是哪里来的，只是气量大，不问。

===Original===
“When you hear the great man of the Shí coming, take the wrappings off the head of the spear and bend your face over it; the heat of the spear, the stench of it, all its pernicious and acrid qualities will prevent you from going to sleep.”

===Chinese===
“等你听见那位仙丘大人来了，就把枪头的包裹拆开，把脸俯上去；枪的热气、枪的恶臭、它那一身毒辣呛人的品性，保你睡不着。”

===Original===
“Are you sure of that?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“这个你有把握？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“You couldn’t go to sleep close to that stench; nobody could,” Fiacuil replied decidedly.

===Chinese===
“挨着那股恶臭你睡不着的；谁也睡不着。”菲亚库尔斩钉截铁地答道。

===Original===
He continued: “Aillen mac Midna will be off his guard when he stops playing and begins to blow his fire; he will think everybody is asleep; then you can deliver the attack you were speaking of, and all good luck go with it.”

===Chinese===
他接着说：“艾伦·麦克·米德纳停下演奏、开始喷火的时候，必然不设防；他会以为人人都睡了；那时你就可以打出你方才说的那场攻击了——愿好运一路随行。”

===Original===
“I will give him back his spear,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“我要把枪还给他。”芬恩说。

===Original===
“Here it is,” said Fiacuil, taking the Birgha from under his cloak. “But be as careful of it, my pulse, be as frightened of it as you are of the man of Dana.”

===Chinese===
“枪在这儿。”菲亚库尔说着，从斗篷底下抽出比尔加。“不过对它要千万小心，我的脉搏；要怕它，像怕那位达南神人一样怕。”

===Original===
“I will be frightened of nothing,” said Fionn, “and the only person I will be sorry for is that Aillen mac Midna, who is going to get his own spear back.”

===Chinese===
“我什么也不怕，”芬恩说，“唯一叫我心里过不去的，是那位艾伦·麦克·米德纳——他就要领回自家的枪了。”

===Original===
“I will go away now,” his companion whispered, “for it is growing darker where you would have thought there was no more room for darkness, and there is an eerie feeling abroad which I do not like. That man from the Shí may come any minute, and if I catch one sound of his music I am done for.”

===Chinese===
“我这就走，”同伴悄声道，“天色黑得更狠了，就在你原以为再黑不下去的地方；四下里还有一种阴森森的气息，我不喜欢。那仙丘里的人随时会来，我只要沾着他音乐的一个音，我就完了。”

===Original===
The robber went away and again Fionn was alone.

===Chinese===
大盗走了，芬恩又是一个人了。

### XIV / XIV

===Original===
He listened to the retreating footsteps until they could be heard no more, and the one sound that came to his tense ears was the beating of his own heart.

===Chinese===
他听着那远去的脚步声，听到再也听不见；这时进到他紧绷的耳朵里的唯一声响，是他自己心的跳动。

===Original===
Even the wind had ceased, and there seemed to be nothing in the world but the darkness and himself. In that gigantic blackness, in that unseen quietude and vacancy, the mind could cease to be personal to itself. It could be overwhelmed and merged in space, so that consciousness would be transferred or dissipated, and one might sleep standing; for the mind fears loneliness more than all else, and will escape to the moon rather than be driven inwards on its own being.

===Chinese===
连风也停了。天地间仿佛只剩黑暗和他。在那份浩大的漆黑里，在那看不见的静与空里，心智可以不再专属它自己。它会被吞没、会融进太虚，知觉随之前移或者散掉，人可以站着睡去；因为心智怕孤独怕过一切，宁可逃到月亮上去，也不肯被赶回自己的深处。

===Original===
But Fionn was not lonely, and he was not afraid when the son of Midna came.

===Chinese===
可芬恩不孤独。米德纳之子到来的时候，他也不怕。

===Original===
A long stretch of the silent night had gone by, minute following minute in a slow sequence, wherein as there was no change there was no time; wherein there was no past and no future, but a stupefying, endless present which is almost the annihilation of consciousness. A change came then, for the clouds had also been moving and the moon at last was sensed behind them﻿—not as a radiance, but as a percolation of light, a gleam that was strained through matter after matter and was less than the very wraith or remembrance of itself; a thing seen so narrowly, so sparsely, that the eye could doubt if it was or was not seeing, and might conceive that its own memory was recreating that which was still absent.

===Chinese===
静夜的漫长一截过去了，一分钟跟着一分钟，缓缓相续；其中没有变化，便也没有时间；没有过去，也没有将来，只有一段叫人麻木的、无尽的长今——几乎是意识的寂灭。随后变化来了：云也一直在走，月亮终于能在云后觉出来了——不是一团光亮，而是一线渗漏的光，一缕从一层又一层物质里滤出来的微芒，比它自己的残魂、比它自己的记忆还要淡；那东西被看得这样窄、这样稀薄，眼睛竟拿不准自己是看见了还是没有看见，甚至会疑心，是自己的记忆正在再造那尚且缺席的东西。

===Original===
But Fionn’s eye was the eye of a wild creature that spies on darkness and moves there wittingly. He saw, then, not a thing but a movement; something that was darker than the darkness it loomed on; not a being but a presence, and, as it were, impending pressure. And in a little he heard the deliberate pace of that great being.

===Chinese===
可芬恩的眼睛，是野生生灵窥伺黑暗、并在其中了然行动的眼睛。于是他看见的不是一件东西，而是一段移动；某种比它所隐现的那片黑暗更黑的东西；不是一个形体，而是一股临在，一种仿佛正在压来的重压。不多时，他听见了那位伟大存在的、不慌不忙的脚步。

===Original===
Fionn bent to his spear and unloosed its coverings.

===Chinese===
芬恩俯身向他的枪，解开了枪上的包裹。

===Original===
Then from the darkness there came another sound; a low, sweet sound; thrillingly joyous, thrillingly low; so low the ear could scarcely note it, so sweet the ear wished to catch nothing else and would strive to hear it rather than all sounds that may be heard by man: the music of another world! the unearthly, dear melody of the Shí! So sweet it was that the sense strained to it, and having reached must follow drowsily in its wake, and would merge in it, and could not return again to its own place until that strange harmony was finished and the ear restored to freedom.

===Chinese===
随后，黑暗里传来另一个声音；一个低而甜的声音；欢畅得叫人颤栗，也低得叫人颤栗；低得耳朵几乎捕捉不到，甜得耳朵再不想听别的，宁可竭力去听它，也不想听人间一切听得见的声响：那是另一个世界的音乐！是仙丘那不属于人世的、无比可亲的旋律！它甜到这个地步，感官便绷紧了朝它去；一经到达，就只得昏昏然随它而行，就要融进它里头，再回不了自己的原地——直到那奇异的和声奏完，耳朵才恢复了自由。

===Original===
But Fionn had taken the covering from his spear, and with his brow pressed close to it he kept his mind and all his senses engaged on that sizzling, murderous point.

===Chinese===
可芬恩已经拆了枪上的包裹，额头紧贴着枪，把心思和全部感官都钉在那个嘶嘶作响、杀气腾腾的枪尖上。

===Original===
The music ceased and Aillen hissed a fierce blue flame from his mouth, and it was as though he hissed lightning.

===Chinese===
音乐停了，艾伦从嘴里嘶地喷出一道凶猛的蓝火——他这一嘶，仿佛嘶出的是闪电。

===Original===
Here it would seem that Fionn used magic, for spreading out his fringed mantle he caught the flame. Rather he stopped it, for it slid from the mantle and sped down into the earth to the depth of twenty-six spans; from which that slope is still called the Glen of the Mantle, and the rise on which Aillen stood is known as the Ard of Fire.

===Chinese===
看到这里，倒像是芬恩用了法术：他张开镶着流苏的披篷，把火焰接住了。说接住，不如说截住——火从披篷上一滑，直钻进地里，钻下去有二十六拃深；从此，那道坡至今还叫斗篷峡谷（Glen of the Mantle），艾伦站过的那道高冈，就叫火之高地（Ard of Fire）。

===Original===
One can imagine the surprise of Aillen mac Midna, seeing his fire caught and quenched by an invisible hand. And one can imagine that at this check he might be frightened, for who would be more terrified than a magician who sees his magic fail, and who, knowing of power, will guess at powers of which he has no conception and may well dread.

===Chinese===
想象得出艾伦·麦克·米德纳的惊愕：他的火，竟被一只看不见的手接住、掐灭了。也想象得出，这一挫之下他也许害怕了；一个眼睁睁看着自己法术失灵的术士，还有谁比他更惊恐？他既然识得权能，就必然会揣想出他全无概念的种种权能，而且多半会怕。

===Original===
Everything had been done by him as it should be done. His pipe had been played and his timpan, all who heard that music should be asleep, and yet his fire was caught in full course and was quenched.

===Chinese===
他该做的都按部就班做足了。管笛吹了，蒂姆潘琴也奏了，听见那音乐的本该都睡着，可他的火偏偏正在半途被截住，被掐灭。

===Original===
Aillen, with all the terrific strength of which he was master, blew again, and the great jet of blue flame came roaring and whistling from him and was caught and disappeared.

===Chinese===
艾伦使出他掌握的全部可怕气力，又喷了一次；那股蓝火的大喷流吼着、啸着从他体内冲出，又被接住，消失了。

===Original===
Panic swirled into the man from Faery; he turned from that terrible spot and fled, not knowing what might be behind, but dreading it as he had never before dreaded anything, and the unknown pursued him; that terrible defence became offence and hung to his heel as a wolf pads by the flank of a bull.

===Chinese===
一阵恐慌灌进了这位仙境来客。他掉头逃离那个可怕的地点，不知道背后可能是什么，却怕它怕得超过了平生所怕的一切；那未知在后头追他；那可怕的守备变成了攻杀，咬住他的脚跟，像狼贴着公牛的侧腹一路轻步追赶。

===Original===
And Aillen was not in his own world! He was in the world of men, where movement is not easy and the very air a burden. In his own sphere, in his own element, he might have outrun Fionn, but this was Fionn’s world, Fionn’s element, and the flying god was not gross enough to outstrip him. Yet what a race he gave, for it was but at the entrance to his own Shí that the pursuer got close enough. Fionn put a finger into the thong of the great spear, and at that cast night fell on Aillen mac Midna. His eyes went black, his mind whirled and ceased, there came nothingness where he had been, and as the Birgha whistled into his shoulder-blades he withered away, he tumbled emptily and was dead. Fionn took his lovely head from its shoulders and went back through the night to Tara.

===Chinese===
何况艾伦已不在自己的世界！他这是在人的世界，在这里行动不爽利，连空气都是重负。在他自己的疆界、自己的元素里，他说不定跑得过芬恩；可这是芬恩的世界，芬恩的元素，这位飞逃的神祇血肉不够浊重，在这样的世界里施展不开，甩不掉他。饶是这样，他也跑出了一场好竞逐——直到他自己仙丘的入口，追击者才逼得够近。芬恩把一根手指扣进大枪的皮索，就在那一掷里，黑夜落到了艾伦·麦克·米德纳身上。他的眼睛黑了，他的心智旋了一下，停了；他所在的地方来了一场空无；比尔加呼啸着钉进他的肩胛，他枯萎下去，空空地跌倒，死了。芬恩从他肩上取下那颗可爱的头颅，穿夜回塔拉去了。

===Original===
Triumphant Fionn, who had dealt death to a god, and to whom death would be dealt, and who is now dead!

===Chinese===
得胜的芬恩啊——他曾把死亡送给一位神，死亡也将被送给他，而如今，他已经死了！

===Original===
He reached the palace at sunrise.

===Chinese===
日出时分，他到了王宫。

===Original===
On that morning all were astir early. They wished to see what destruction had been wrought by the great being, but it was young Fionn they saw and that redoubtable head swinging by its hair.

===Chinese===
那天早晨，人人起得早。他们本想看看那位伟大存在造成了怎样的毁灭，可他们看见的，是年轻的芬恩，和那颗提在头发里悠荡的狰狞头颅。

===Original===
“What is your demand?” said the Ard-Rí.

===Chinese===
“你有什么要求？”至高王说。

===Original===
“The thing that it is right I should ask,” said Fionn: “the command of the Fianna of Ireland.”

===Chinese===
“问我该问的那一件，”芬恩说，“爱尔兰芬尼亚勇士团的统率之权。”

===Original===
“Make your choice,” said Conn to Goll Mor; “you will leave Ireland, or you will place your hand in the hand of this champion and be his man.”

===Chinese===
“你自己挑，”康恩对戈尔·麦克·莫尔纳说，“要么离开爱尔兰，要么把手放进这位勇士的手里，做他的人。”

===Original===
Goll could do a thing that would be hard for another person, and he could do it so beautifully that he was not diminished by any action.

===Chinese===
别人做来千难万难的事，戈尔做得来；而且做得那样漂亮，无论做什么，都不折损他一分。

===Original===
“Here is my hand,” said Goll.

===Chinese===
“我的手在这儿。”戈尔说。

===Original===
And he twinkled at the stern, young eyes that gazed on him as he made his submission.

===Chinese===
他俯首称臣的时候，朝那双瞪视着他的严厉的年轻眼睛，眨了眨。

## The Carl of the Drab Coat / 褐衣卡尔

### I / I

===Original===
One day something happened to Fionn, the son of Uail; that is, he departed from the world of men, and was set wandering in great distress of mind through Faery. He had days and nights there and adventures there, and was able to bring back the memory of these.

===Chinese===
有一天，尤尔（Uail）之子芬恩（Fionn）遇上一桩奇事：他离开了人类的世界，满怀愁苦，被抛进仙境（Faery）里游荡。他在那里度过了日日夜夜，历经种种奇遇，而且还能把这些统统装回记忆里带走。

===Original===
That, by itself, is wonderful, for there are few people who remember that they have been to Faery or aught of all that happened to them in that state.

===Chinese===
单是这一点，就已了不得了；因为记得自己去过仙境、记得那种境界里发生过什么的人，世上寥寥无几。

===Original===
In truth we do not go to Faery, we become Faery, and in the beating of a pulse we may live for a year or a thousand years. But when we return the memory is quickly clouded, and we seem to have had a dream or seen a vision, although we have verily been in Faery.

===Chinese===
说实在的，我们并不是“去”仙境，而是自己化作了仙境；脉搏一跳之间，我们便可能活上一年，或者一千年。只是归来之后，记忆很快便蒙上云翳，我们只道自己做了一场梦、见了一幅幻象，殊不知我们真真切切到过仙境。

===Original===
It was wonderful, then, that Fionn should have remembered all that happened to him in that wide-spun moment, but in this tale there is yet more to marvel at; for not only did Fionn go to Faery, but the great army which he had marshalled to Ben Edair4 were translated also, and neither he nor they were aware that they had departed from the world until they came back to it.

===Chinese===
芬恩居然记得在那绵长一瞬里发生在自己身上的一切，这已经是奇事；可这个故事里还有更奇的：不但芬恩去了仙境，连他召集到本·埃代尔4（Ben Edair）的大军也一并被挪了过去；而且无论他还是他们，直到回到人间的那一刻，都不曾察觉自己离开过这个世界。

---

===Original===
Fourteen battles, seven of the reserve and seven of the regular Fianna, had been taken by the Chief on a great march and manoeuvre. When they reached Ben Edair it was decided to pitch camp so that the troops might rest in view of the warlike plan which Fionn had imagined for the morrow. The camp was chosen, and each squadron and company of the host were lodged into an appropriate place, so there was no overcrowding and no halt or interruption of the march; for where a company halted that was its place of rest, and in that place it hindered no other company, and was at its own ease.

===Chinese===
统帅率领十四支战队——七支后备，七支芬尼亚勇士团（Fianna）正役——进行了一场大规模的行军演习。抵达本·埃代尔后，众人决定扎营，让部队歇息，因为芬恩已为明日想好了一个杀气腾腾的计划。营地选定之后，大军的每一队每一哨都安置在合适的去处，既不拥挤，行军也无停顿阻滞；因为一队人马停在哪里，哪里便是它的宿营地，在那个地方它不妨碍任何旁的队伍，自家也落得自在。

===Original===
When this was accomplished the leaders of battalions gathered on a level, grassy plateau overlooking the sea, where a consultation began as to the next day’s manoeuvres, and during this discussion they looked often on the wide water that lay wrinkling and twinkling below them.

===Chinese===
这些安排停当，各营首领便聚到一处俯瞰大海的平展草坡上，商议起明日的演习来；商议期间，他们时常眺望脚下那片起着皱、闪着光的辽阔海水。

===Original===
A roomy ship under great press of sail was bearing on Ben Edair from the east.

===Chinese===
一艘宽敞的大船满帆张足，正从东方向本·埃代尔驶来。

===Original===
Now and again, in a lull of the discussion, a champion would look and remark on the hurrying vessel; and it may have been during one of these moments that the adventure happened to Fionn and the Fianna.

===Chinese===
商议偶有间歇，便有一位好汉抬眼望望那艘匆匆赶路的大船，议论几句；那桩奇遇降临到芬恩和勇士团头上的时刻，多半正是这样的一瞬。

===Original===
“I wonder where that ship comes from?” said Conán idly.

===Chinese===
“不知道这船是从哪儿开来的？”柯南（Conán）漫不经心地说。

===Original===
But no person could surmise anything about it beyond that it was a vessel well equipped for war.

===Chinese===
只是任谁也猜不出别的名堂，只看得出这是一艘装备精良的战船。

===Original===
As the ship drew by the shore the watchers observed a tall man swing from the side by means of his spear shafts, and in a little while this gentleman was announced to Fionn, and was brought into his presence.

===Chinese===
船沿岸驶过时，眺望的人们看见一个高个子男人攀着矛杆从船舷荡了下来；不多时，这位先生便通了名，被带到芬恩面前。

===Original===
A sturdy, bellicose, forthright personage he was indeed. He was equipped in a wonderful solidity of armour, with a hard, carven helmet on his head, a splendid red-bossed shield swinging on his shoulder, a wide-grooved, straight sword clashing along his thigh. On his shoulders under the shield he carried a splendid scarlet mantle; over his breast was a great brooch of burnt gold, and in his fist he gripped a pair of thick-shafted, unburnished spears.

===Chinese===
这位人物当真魁梧结实、好斗直率。他披挂着一身厚实得惊人的甲胄：头上扣着一顶坚硬的雕纹头盔，肩上荡着一面钉着红色凸饰的华美大盾，一柄宽槽直剑顺着大腿铿锵作响。盾牌底下，他肩上还披着一件绚烂的猩红披风；胸前别着一枚烈火烧炼的黄金大胸针，拳头里攥着一对杆粗身砺、未经打磨的长矛。

===Original===
Fionn and the champions looked on this gentleman, and they admired exceedingly his bearing and equipment.

===Chinese===
芬恩和众好汉打量着这位先生，对他的气度与披挂都艳羡不已。

===Original===
“Of what blood are you, young gentleman?” Fionn demanded, “and from which of the four corners of the world do you come?”

===Chinese===
“你是何家血脉，年轻的先生？”芬恩问道，“又从世界四角中的哪一角来？”

===Original===
“My name is Cael of the Iron,” the stranger answered, “and I am son to the King of Thessaly.”

===Chinese===
“我的名字叫铁凯尔（Cael of the Iron），”陌生人答道，“我是色萨利（Thessaly）国王的儿子。”

===Original===
“What errand has brought you here?”

===Chinese===
“什么差事把你送到这儿来了？”

===Original===
“I do not go on errands,” the man replied sternly, “but on the affairs that please me.”

===Chinese===
“我不办什么差事，”那人声色俱厉地答道，“我只办我自己乐意办的事。”

===Original===
“Be it so. What is the pleasing affair which brings you to this land?”

===Chinese===
“那就随你。是什么乐意的事，把你引到这片土地上来了？”

===Original===
“Since I left my own country I have not gone from a land or an island until it paid tribute to me and acknowledged my lordship.”

===Chinese===
“自我离开故国，还从来没有一片土地、一座岛屿，不曾向我纳贡、不曾承认我的宗主权，就让我走掉的。”

===Original===
“And you have come to this realm﻿—” cried Fionn, doubting his ears.

===Chinese===
“而你竟然是冲着这个王国来的——”芬恩嚷道，简直不敢相信自己的耳朵。

===Original===
“For tribute and sovereignty,” growled that other, and he struck the haft of his spear violently on the ground.

===Chinese===
“就是冲着贡赋和宗主权来的。”那人怒声道，把矛柄狠狠往地上一顿。

===Original===
“By my hand,” said Conán, “we have never heard of a warrior, however great, but his peer was found in Ireland, and the funeral songs of all such have been chanted by the women of this land.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”柯南说，“多大的武士我们都听说过，可他的对手总能在爱尔兰找得出来；这一类人的哀歌，也全由这片土地上的女人们唱遍了。”

===Original===
“By my hand and word,” said the harsh stranger, “your talk makes me think of a small boy or of an idiot.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手和我的话，”语气生硬的陌生人说，“你们这些话让我想起一个小娃娃，要么就是个傻子。”

===Original===
“Take heed, sir,” said Fionn, “for the champions and great dragons of the Gael are standing by you, and around us there are fourteen battles of the Fianna of Ireland.”

===Chinese===
“放尊重点，先生，”芬恩说，“盖尔人的好汉与巨龙就站在你身旁；环顾我们四周，还有爱尔兰勇士团的十四支战队。”

===Original===
“If all the Fianna who have died in the last seven years were added to all that are now here,” the stranger asserted, “I would treat all of these and those grievously, and would curtail their limbs and their lives.”

===Chinese===
“就算把过去七年里死掉的勇士团人马，全都添到眼下在场的这些人里头，”陌生人断言，“我也要把这两拨人统统狠狠教训一顿，削短他们的手脚，也削短他们的性命。”

===Original===
“It is no small boast,” Conán murmured, staring at him.

===Chinese===
“这口气可不小。”柯南盯着他，喃喃道。

===Original===
“It is no boast at all,” said Cael, “and, to show my quality and standing, I will propose a deed to you.”

===Chinese===
“这根本算不上夸口，”凯尔说，“为了显示我的身份和地位，我来向你们提一桩事。”

===Original===
“Give out your deed,” Fionn commanded.

===Chinese===
“把你那桩事讲出来。”芬恩命令道。

===Original===
“Thus,” said Cael with cold savagery. “If you can find a man among your fourteen battalions who can outrun or outwrestle or outfight me, I will take myself off to my own country, and will trouble you no more.”

===Chinese===
“是这样，”凯尔带着冷冷的凶横说，“你们那十四营人马里，若能找出一个跑得过我、摔得过我、打得过我的人，我便动身回我的故国，从此不再来搅扰你们。”

===Original===
And so harshly did he speak, and with such a belligerent eye did he stare, that dismay began to seize on the champions, and even Fionn felt that his breath had halted.

===Chinese===
他说得那样狠戾，瞪着的那样一双挑衅的眼睛，弄得众好汉心里渐渐发慌，连芬恩都觉得自己呼吸停住了。

===Original===
“It is spoken like a hero,” he admitted after a moment, “and if you cannot be matched on those terms it will not be from a dearth of applicants.”

===Chinese===
“这话说得像条好汉，”他过了一会儿才承认，“倘若按这样的条件找不出你的对手，那绝不会是因为没人应战。”

===Original===
“In running alone,” Fionn continued thoughtfully, “we have a notable champion, Caelte mac Ronán.”

===Chinese===
“单说跑步，”芬恩若有所思地接着说，“我们有一位赫赫有名的健将，卡尔特·麦克·罗南（Caelte mac Ronán）。”

===Original===
“This son of Ronán will not long be notable,” the stranger asserted.

===Chinese===
“这位罗南之子风光不了多久了。”陌生人断言。

===Original===
“He can outstrip the red deer,” said Conán.

===Chinese===
“赤鹿都追不上他。”柯南说。

===Original===
“He can outrun the wind,” cried Fionn.

===Chinese===
“他连风都跑得过。”芬恩喊道。

===Original===
“He will not be asked to outrun the red deer or the wind,” the stranger sneered. “He will be asked to outrun me,” he thundered. “Produce this runner, and we shall discover if he keeps as great heart in his feet as he has made you think.”

===Chinese===
“没人要他去跑赢赤鹿或者风，”陌生人讥笑道，“要他跑赢的是我，”他轰雷般吼道，“把这位飞毛腿叫出来吧，咱们瞧瞧，他脚底下是不是真像你们以为的那样，装着一颗大胆。”

===Original===
“He is not with us,” Conán lamented.

===Chinese===
“他眼下不在我们这儿。”柯南叹道。

===Original===
“These notable warriors are never with us when the call is made,” said the grim stranger.

===Chinese===
“这些赫赫有名的勇士，偏偏点将的时候从来不在。”阴沉沉的陌生人说。

===Original===
“By my hand,” cried Fionn, “he shall be here in no great time, for I will fetch him myself.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”芬恩喊道，“他很快就到，我这就亲自去接他。”

===Original===
“Be it so,” said Cael.

===Chinese===
“就这样吧。”凯尔说。

===Original===
“And during my absence,” Fionn continued, “I leave this as a compact, that you make friends with the Fianna here present, and that you observe all the conditions and ceremonies of friendship.”

===Chinese===
“还有，趁我不在，”芬恩接着说，“我立下这一条约定：你要与在场的勇士团交好，并且恪守交友的一切条款与礼数。”

===Original===
Cael agreed to that.

===Chinese===
凯尔应承了。

===Original===
“I will not hurt any of these people until you return,” he said.

===Chinese===
“在你回来之前，我不伤这些人里任何一个。”他说。

===Original===
Fionn then set out towards Tara of the Kings, for he thought Caelte mac Ronán would surely be there; “and if he is not there,” said the champion to himself, “then I shall find him at Cesh Corran of the Fianna.”

===Chinese===
于是芬恩动身往诸王的塔拉（Tara of the Kings）去，因为他料定卡尔特·麦克·罗南准在那里；“他若不在那儿，”这位好汉心里盘算，“我就去勇士团的克什科兰（Cesh Corran）找他。”

### II / II

===Original===
He had not gone a great distance from Ben Edair when he came to an intricate, gloomy wood, where the trees grew so thickly and the undergrowth was such a sprout and tangle that one could scarcely pass through it. He remembered that a path had once been hacked through the wood, and he sought for this. It was a deeply scooped, hollow way, and it ran or wriggled through the entire length of the wood.

===Chinese===
他离开本·埃代尔没走多远，就闯进一片幽深杂乱的树林：树木长得密不透风，树下灌木抽芽蔓生、纠结成一团，人几乎挤不过去。他记得林子里曾砍出过一条小径，便去寻它。那是一条掘得很深的洼道，纵贯整片林子，时而直走，时而蜿蜒。

===Original===
Into this gloomy drain Fionn descended and made progress, but when he had penetrated deeply in the dank forest he heard a sound of thumping and squelching footsteps, and he saw coming towards him a horrible, evil-visaged being; a wild, monstrous, yellow-skinned, big-boned giant, dressed in nothing but an ill-made, mud-plastered, drab-coloured coat, which swaggled and clapped against the calves of his big bare legs. On his stamping feet there were great brogues of boots that were shaped like, but were bigger than, a boat, and each time he put a foot down it squashed and squirted a barrelful of mud from the sunk road.

===Chinese===
芬恩下到这条阴暗的沟道里，一路前行；可等他深入湿漉漉的林子，就听见一阵噗噗唧唧的脚步声，只见一个面目可憎的凶物朝自己走来：一个野蛮狰狞、黄皮肤、大骨架的巨人，身上只披着一件做工粗劣、糊满泥浆的土褐色外衣，那外衣沉甸甸地晃荡着，拍打着他两条赤裸大腿的腿肚子。他跺步的双脚上套着巨大的粗革靴子，形状像一条船，只是比船还大；他的脚每落一下，就从这陷塌的路面上压挤出整整一桶泥浆。

===Original===
Fionn had never seen the like of this vast person, and he stood gazing on him, lost in a stare of astonishment.

===Chinese===
这样庞大的人物，芬恩生平未见，他站在那里呆望着，望得出神。

===Original===
The great man saluted him.

===Chinese===
那大个子向他行礼问安。

===Original===
“All alone, Fionn?” he cried. “How does it happen that not one Fenian of the Fianna is at the side of his captain?”

===Chinese===
“就你一个人吗，芬恩？”他喊道，“怎么会偌大一个勇士团，竟没有一个芬尼亚汉子守在自家统领身边？”

===Original===
At this inquiry Fionn got back his wits.

===Chinese===
这一问，芬恩才回过神来。

===Original===
“That is too long a story and it is too intricate and pressing to be told, also I have no time to spare now.”

===Chinese===
“那故事说来话长，又错综又紧急，讲不得，何况我眼下连一点工夫也匀不出。”

===Original===
“Yet tell it now,” the monstrous man insisted.

===Chinese===
“偏要现在讲。”那怪物不依道。

===Original===
Fionn, thus pressed, told of the coming of Cael of the Iron, of the challenge the latter had issued, and that he, Fionn, was off to Tara of the Kings to find Caelte mac Ronán.

===Chinese===
芬恩被他缠不过，便讲了铁凯尔如何到来、如何下战书，又说自己正要去诸王的塔拉找卡尔特·麦克·罗南。

===Original===
“I know that foreigner well,” the big man commented.

===Chinese===
“那外来人我熟得很。”大个子评点道。

===Original===
“Is he the champion he makes himself out to be?” Fionn inquired.

===Chinese===
“他真有他自吹的那样厉害吗？”芬恩问。

===Original===
“He can do twice as much as he said he would do,” the monster replied.

===Chinese===
“他夸下的海口，他做得出双倍。”怪物答道。

===Original===
“He won’t outrun Caelte mac Ronán,” Fionn asserted.

===Chinese===
“可他跑不过卡尔特·麦克·罗南。”芬恩断言。

===Original===
The big man jeered.

===Chinese===
大个子嗤笑了一声。

===Original===
“Say that he won’t outrun a hedgehog, dear heart. This Cael will end the course by the time your Caelte begins to think of starting.”

===Chinese===
“亲爱的，你还不如说他跑不过一只刺猬呢。等你的卡尔特刚开始琢磨该起跑了，这位凯尔已经跑完全程了。”

===Original===
“Then,” said Fionn, “I no longer know where to turn, or how to protect the honour of Ireland.”

===Chinese===
“这么说，”芬恩道，“我就不知该往哪儿指望，也不知该如何保全爱尔兰的体面了。”

===Original===
“I know how to do these things,” the other man commented with a slow nod of the head.

===Chinese===
“这些事我知道怎么办。”那人点着头，慢悠悠地评点道。

===Original===
“If you do,” Fionn pleaded, “tell it to me upon your honour.”

===Chinese===
“你若当真知道，”芬恩恳求道，“看在你的名誉份上，说给我听。”

===Original===
“I will do that,” the man replied.

===Chinese===
“我这就说。”那人答道。

===Original===
“Do not look any further for the rusty-kneed, slow-trotting son of Ronán,” he continued, “but ask me to run your race, and, by this hand, I will be first at the post.”

===Chinese===
“别再去找那个膝盖生锈、小步慢颠的罗南之子了，”他接着说，“请你让我来替你跑这一场，凭这只手起誓，头一个到终点柱的准是我。”

===Original===
At this the Chief began to laugh﻿—

===Chinese===
听了这话，大统领放声大笑——

===Original===
“My good friend, you have work enough to carry the two tons of mud that are plastered on each of your coattails, to say nothing of your weighty boots.”

===Chinese===
“我的好朋友，你外衣两片后摆上各糊着两吨泥，光是驮这些就够你忙的了，更不用说那双沉重的靴子。”

===Original===
“By my hand,” the man cried, “there is no person in Ireland but myself can win that race. I claim a chance.”

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓，”那人喊道，“这场比赛，爱尔兰除了我没别人赢得下来。我讨一个机会。”

===Original===
Fionn agreed then.

===Chinese===
芬恩于是应允了。

===Original===
“Be it so,” said he. “And now, tell me your name?”

===Chinese===
“就这样吧，”他说，“那么现在，告诉我你的名字？”

===Original===
“I am known as the Carl of the Drab Coat.”

===Chinese===
“人家都管我叫褐衣卡尔（the Carl of the Drab Coat）。”

===Original===
“All names are names,” Fionn responded, “and that also is a name.”

===Chinese===
“名字终归是名字，”芬恩答道，“这自然也算一个。”

===Original===
They returned then to Ben Edair.

===Chinese===
随后两人便回本·埃代尔去了。

### III / III

===Original===
When they came among the host the men of Ireland gathered about the vast stranger; and there were some who hid their faces in their mantles so that they should not be seen to laugh, and there were some who rolled along the ground in merriment, and there were others who could only hold their mouths open and crook their knees and hang their arms and stare dumbfoundedly upon the stranger, as though they were utterly dazed.

===Chinese===
两人回到大军中间，爱尔兰的汉子们纷纷围拢过来看这庞大的陌生人：有的把脸埋进披风里，免得被人瞧见自己在笑；有的乐得在地上打滚；还有的只是张着嘴、屈着膝、垂着胳膊，呆呆地瞪着那陌生人，仿佛整个人都懵了。

===Original===
Cael of the Iron came also on the scene, and he examined the stranger with close and particular attention.

===Chinese===
铁凯尔也到了场，把那陌生人仔仔细细、上上下下打量了一番。

===Original===
“What in the name of the devil is this thing?” he asked of Fionn.

===Chinese===
“这鬼东西是个什么玩意儿？”他问芬恩。

===Original===
“Dear heart,” said Fionn, “this is the champion I am putting against you in the race.”

===Chinese===
“亲爱的，”芬恩说，“这就是我安排来跟你赛跑的对手。”

===Original===
Cael of the Iron grew purple in the face, and he almost swallowed his tongue through wrath.

===Chinese===
铁凯尔的脸涨成了紫色，气得险些把自己的舌头吞下去。

===Original===
“Until the end of eternity,” he roared, “and until the very last moment of doom I will not move one foot in a race with this greasy, big-hoofed, ill-assembled resemblance of a beggarman.”

===Chinese===
“哪怕到永恒的尽头，”他吼道，“哪怕到劫数的最后一刻，我也不肯挪动一只脚，跟这么一个油乎乎、大蹄子、拼凑得七零八落的叫花子货赛跑。”

===Original===
But at this the Carl burst into a roar of laughter, so that the eardrums of the warriors present almost burst inside of their heads.

===Chinese===
可这话一出，卡尔放声大笑，笑声震得在场众好汉的耳膜几乎在脑袋里迸裂。

===Original===
“Be reassured, my darling, I am no beggarman, and my quality is not more gross than is the blood of the most delicate prince in this assembly. You will not evade your challenge in that way, my love, and you shall run with me or you shall run to your ship with me behind you. What length of course do you propose, dear heart?”

===Chinese===
“放心吧，我的心肝，我才不是什么叫花子，我的血统并不比这满场最娇贵的王子的血更粗贱。你想这么着就赖掉你的战约，我的宝贝，没门儿；你要么跟我跑，要么被我撵着往你那条船上跑。你要跑多远的赛程，亲爱的？”

===Original===
“I never run less than sixty miles,” Cael replied sullenly.

===Chinese===
“我跑步从来不少于六十英里。”凯尔绷着脸答道。

===Original===
“It is a small run,” said the Carl, “but it will do. From this place to the Hill of the Rushes, Slieve Luachra of Munster, is exactly sixty miles. Will that suit you?”

===Chinese===
“这赛程短了点儿，”卡尔说，“不过也凑合。从此地到灯心草山，芒斯特（Munster）的斯利夫·卢阿赫拉（Slieve Luachra），不多不少正好六十英里。合你的意吗？”

===Original===
“I don’t care how it is done,” Cael answered.

===Chinese===
“怎么都行，我不在乎。”凯尔答道。

===Original===
“Then,” said the Carl, “we may go off to Slieve Luachra now, and in the morning we can start our race there to here.”

===Chinese===
“那么，”卡尔说，“咱们这就动身去斯利夫·卢阿赫拉，明儿一早从那儿往这儿跑。”

===Original===
“Let it be done that way,” said Cael.

===Chinese===
“就这么办。”凯尔说。

===Original===
These two set out then for Munster, and as the sun was setting they reached Slieve Luachra and prepared to spend the night there.

===Chinese===
于是两人动身往芒斯特去；太阳落山时分，他们到了斯利夫·卢阿赫拉，准备在那里过夜。

### IV / IV

===Original===
“Cael, my pulse,” said the Carl, “we had better build a house or a hut to pass the night in.”

===Chinese===
“凯尔，我的脉搏，”卡尔说，“咱们顶好盖座房子、搭间棚子，好过这一夜。”

===Original===
“I’ll build nothing,” Cael replied, looking on the Carl with great disfavour.

===Chinese===
“我什么都不盖。”凯尔答道，满脸嫌恶地看着卡尔。

===Original===
“No!”

===Chinese===
“什么，不盖！”

===Original===
“I won’t build house or hut for the sake of passing one night here, for I hope never to see this place again.”

===Chinese===
“我才不肯为了在这儿过一夜就盖房搭棚，因为我巴不得这辈子再不见这地方。”

===Original===
“I’ll build a house myself,” said the Carl, “and the man who does not help in the building can stay outside of the house.”

===Chinese===
“那我自己盖，”卡尔说，“谁不肯搭手，谁就待在屋子外头。”

===Original===
The Carl stumped to a nearby wood, and he never rested until he had felled and tied together twenty-four couples of big timber. He thrust these under one arm and under the other he tucked a bundle of rushes for his bed, and with that one load he rushed up a house, well thatched and snug, and with the timber that remained over he made a bonfire on the floor of the house.

===Chinese===
卡尔蹬着大靴子走进附近的树林，一口气砍倒大树，捆成二十四对粗大的木料，中途片刻不停。他把木料往一只胳膊底下一夹，另一只胳膊底下再掖上一捆铺床用的灯心草，就凭这一趟料，风风火火架起一座房子，苫盖严实，暖和舒适；余下的木料，他就在屋里地上烧起一堆篝火。

===Original===
His companion sat at a distance regarding the work with rage and aversion.

===Chinese===
他的同伴坐在远处，又怒又厌地看着这一切。

===Original===
“Now Cael, my darling,” said the Carl, “if you are a man help me to look for something to eat, for there is game here.”

===Chinese===
“好了，凯尔，我的宝贝，”卡尔说，“你要还算条汉子，就帮我寻点吃的来，这儿有野味。”

===Original===
“Help yourself,” roared Cael, “for all that I want is not to be near you.”

===Chinese===
“你自己动手吧，”凯尔吼道，“我一心想要的，就是离你远远的。”

===Original===
“The tooth that does not help gets no helping,” the other replied.

===Chinese===
“不肯出力的牙，也别想得到帮衬。”那一位答道。

===Original===
In a short time the Carl returned with a wild boar which he had run down. He cooked the beast over his bonfire and ate one half of it, leaving the other half for his breakfast. Then he lay down on the rushes, and in two turns he fell asleep.

===Chinese===
没多大工夫，卡尔就追倒了一头野公猪，扛了回来。他把那畜生架在篝火上烤熟，吃掉一半，留下一半当早饭。随后他往灯心草上一躺，翻了两个身，就睡着了。

===Original===
But Cael lay out on the side of the hill, and if he went to sleep that night he slept fasting.

===Chinese===
凯尔却露宿在山坡上；那一夜他纵然睡着，也是饿着肚子睡的。

===Original===
It was he, however, who awakened the Carl in the morning.

===Chinese===
可到了早晨，把卡尔叫醒的偏偏是他。

===Original===
“Get up, beggarman, if you are going to run against me.”

===Chinese===
“起来吧，叫花子，你不是要跟我赛跑吗。”

===Original===
The Carl rubbed his eyes.

===Chinese===
卡尔揉了揉眼睛。

===Original===
“I never get up until I have had my fill of sleep, and there is another hour of it due to me. But if you are in a hurry, my delight, you can start running now with a blessing. I will trot on your track when I waken up.”

===Chinese===
“觉没睡够我从不起床，我还欠着一个钟头呢。不过你要是着急，我的欢喜，大可以带上我的祝福先跑。等我一觉醒来，自会顺着你的脚印小跑着追去。”

===Original===
Cael began to race then, and he was glad of the start, for his antagonist made so little account of him that he did not know what to expect when the Carl would begin to run.

===Chinese===
凯尔于是起跑；抢到这个先手他暗自庆幸，因为对手太不把他放在眼里，反倒叫他摸不准卡尔真跑起来会是什么光景。

===Original===
“Yet,” said Cael to himself, “with an hour’s start the beggarman will have to move his bones if he wants to catch on me,” and he settled down to a good, pelting race.

===Chinese===
“不过，”凯尔暗想，“我抢出了一个钟头，那叫花子想追上我，非得把他的老骨头挪出火星来不可。”他定下心，撒开一场痛痛快快的飞奔。

### V / V

===Original===
At the end of an hour the Carl awoke. He ate the second half of the boar, and he tied the unpicked bones in the tail of his coat. Then with a great rattling of the boar’s bones he started.

===Chinese===
一个钟头到了，卡尔醒来。他把野猪的另一半吃掉，把啃剩的骨头拴进外衣的后摆里。随后，伴着野猪骨一阵哗啦哗啦的大响，他出发了。

===Original===
It is hard to tell how he ran or at what speed he ran, but he went forward in great two-legged jumps, and at times he moved in immense one-legged, mud-spattering hops, and at times again, with wide-stretched, far-flung, terrible-tramping, space-destroying legs he ran.

===Chinese===
他跑得怎样、跑出多大的速度，实在难以描摹：只见他迈开双腿，一蹦一蹦地朝前蹿；有时又单腿蹦跳，一跳一大截，泥浆四溅；有时又两腿大开大合、远远抛出、可怕地踏地、仿佛要把空间踏碎，撒腿狂奔。

===Original===
He left the swallows behind as if they were asleep. He caught up on a red deer, jumped over it, and left it standing. The wind was always behind him, for he outran it every time; and he caught up in jumps and bounces on Cael of the Iron, although Cael was running well, with his fists up and his head back and his two legs flying in and out so vigorously that you could not see them because of that speedy movement.

===Chinese===
燕子被他甩在身后，仿佛还在酣睡。他追上一头赤鹿，一跃而过，把它留在原地发怔。风永远落在他背后，因为他回回都把风跑赢；他一路连蹦带跳，追上了铁凯尔——虽说凯尔跑得正欢，双拳紧攥，头颅后仰，两条腿进进出出飞舞得那么用力，快得叫人根本看不清。

===Original===
Trotting by the side of Cael, the Carl thrust a hand into the tail of his coat and pulled out a fistfull of red bones.

===Chinese===
卡尔在凯尔身旁颠着小跑，一只手伸进外衣后摆，摸出一把红艳艳的骨头。

===Original===
“Here, my heart, is a meaty bone,” said he, “for you fasted all night, poor friend, and if you pick a bit off the bone your stomach will get a rest.”

===Chinese===
“来，我的心肝，这根骨头肉多，”他说，“你饿了一整夜，可怜的朋友，啃上几口，你那肚肠也能歇口气。”

===Original===
“Keep your filth, beggarman,” the other replied, “for I would rather be hanged than gnaw on a bone that you have browsed.”

===Chinese===
“收起你的脏东西吧，叫花子，”那一位答道，“宁可叫人吊死，我也啃不下你啃过的骨头。”

===Original===
“Why don’t you run, my pulse?” said the Carl earnestly; “why don’t you try to win the race?”

===Chinese===
“你怎么不跑哇，我的脉搏？”卡尔一脸恳切地说，“你怎么不试着赢下这场比赛？”

===Original===
Cael then began to move his limbs as if they were the wings of a fly, or the fins of a little fish, or as if they were the six legs of a terrified spider.

===Chinese===
于是凯尔开始摆动四肢，仿佛那是苍蝇的翅膀，是小鱼的鳍，又仿佛是吓破了胆的蜘蛛那六条腿。

===Original===
“I am running,” he gasped.

===Chinese===
“我在跑哇。”他喘着气说。

===Original===
“But try and run like this,” the Carl admonished, and he gave a wriggling bound and a sudden outstretching and scurrying of shanks, and he disappeared from Cael’s sight in one wild spatter of big boots.

===Chinese===
“可你得照这样跑啊，”卡尔规劝道。说着他一拧身子腾地一跃，小腿猛然伸展、急急倒腾，在一阵狂野的大靴子溅踏声里，从凯尔的眼前消失了。

===Original===
Despair fell on Cael of the Iron, but he had a great heart.

===Chinese===
绝望压上了铁凯尔的心头，可他胸中自有一副大胆。

===Original===
“I will run until I burst,” he shrieked, “and when I burst, may I burst to a great distance, and may I trip that beggar-man up with my burstings and make him break his leg.”

===Chinese===
“我就是跑到炸开也要跑，”他尖声喊道，“等我炸开的时候，求老天让我炸得远远的，好用我的炸裂绊那叫花子一个跟头，摔断他一条腿。”

===Original===
He settled then to a determined, savage, implacable trot.

===Chinese===
随后他定下神，迈开一种坚决、凶狠、不肯罢休的小跑。

===Original===
He caught up on the Carl at last, for the latter had stopped to eat blackberries from the bushes on the road, and when he drew nigh, Cael began to jeer and sneer angrily at the Carl.

===Chinese===
他终于又追上了卡尔，因为那一位中途停了脚，正在路旁的灌木丛里吃黑莓；等他跑近，凯尔便冲着卡尔挖苦讥笑起来，火气冲天。

===Original===
“Who lost the tails of his coat?” he roared.

===Chinese===
“是谁把外衣的后摆跑丢啦？”他吼道。

===Original===
“Don’t ask riddles of a man that’s eating blackberries,” the Carl rebuked him.

===Chinese===
“别跟一个正吃黑莓的人打哑谜。”卡尔责备他道。

===Original===
“The dog without a tail and the coat without a tail,” cried Cael.

===Chinese===
“没尾巴的狗，没后摆的外衣。”凯尔嚷道。

===Original===
“I give it up,” the Carl mumbled.

===Chinese===
“我认输，猜不出。”卡尔咕哝道。

===Original===
“It’s yourself, beggarman,” jeered Cael.

===Chinese===
“谜底就是你自己，叫花子。”凯尔讥笑道。

===Original===
“I am myself,” the Carl gurgled through a mouthful of blackberries, “and as I am myself, how can it be myself? That is a silly riddle,” he burbled.

===Chinese===
“我是我自己呀，”卡尔满嘴黑莓，咕噜咕噜地说，“既然我是我自己，又怎么会是我自己？这谜语蠢透了。”他咕哝咕哝地说。

===Original===
“Look at your coat, tub of grease?”

===Chinese===
“瞧瞧你的外衣，油桶？”

===Original===
The Carl did so.

===Chinese===
卡尔瞧了瞧。

===Original===
“My faith,” said he, “where are the two tails of my coat?”

===Chinese===
“我的老天，”他说，“我外衣的两片后摆哪儿去了？”

===Original===
“I could smell one of them and it wrapped around a little tree thirty miles back,” said Cael, “and the other one was dishonouring a bush ten miles behind that.”

===Chinese===
“有一片我老远就闻见了，它裹在三十英里外的一棵小树上，”凯尔说，“另一片更不体面，正败坏着再往回十英里一丛灌木的名声。”

===Original===
“It is bad luck to be separated from the tails of your own coat,” the Carl grumbled. “I’ll have to go back for them. Wait here, beloved, and eat blackberries until I come back, and we’ll both start fair.”

===Chinese===
“跟自家外衣的后摆分离，是要倒霉的，”卡尔嘟囔道，“我只好回去取它们。在这儿等着，心爱的，吃吃黑莓打发时光，等我回来，咱们两个公平起跑。”

===Original===
“Not half a second will I wait,” Cael replied, and he began to run towards Ben Edair as a lover runs to his maiden or as a bee flies to his hive.

===Chinese===
“半秒钟我也不等。”凯尔答道，随即朝本·埃代尔飞奔而去，像情郎奔向他的姑娘，像蜜蜂飞回它的蜂巢。

===Original===
“I haven’t had half my share of blackberries either,” the Carl lamented as he started to run backwards for his coattails.

===Chinese===
“我那份黑莓也还没吃上一半呢。”卡尔悲叹一声，转身往回跑，去追他的外衣后摆。

===Original===
He ran determinedly on that backward journey, and as the path he had travelled was beaten out as if it had been trampled by an hundred bulls yoked neck to neck, he was able to find the two bushes and the two coattails. He sewed them on his coat.

===Chinese===
他在回头路上跑得斩钉截铁；由于他来时踩出的那道路径，就好像一百头公牛脖子挨脖子拴在一起踏过似的，所以他顺顺当当找到了那两丛灌木和那两片后摆。他把它们缝回了外衣上。

===Original===
Then he sprang up, and he took to a fit and a vortex and an exasperation of running for which no description may be found. The thumping of his big boots grew as continuous as the pattering of hailstones on a roof, and the wind of his passage blew trees down. The beasts that were ranging beside his path dropped dead from concussion, and the steam that snored from his nose blew birds into bits and made great lumps of cloud fall out of the sky.

===Chinese===
随后他一跃而起，发起一阵癫狂、一道旋涡、一场怒不可遏的奔跑，找不出任何言语可以形容。他的大靴子踏地的轰响，连绵得如同冰雹砸在屋顶上；他奔过带起的风把一棵棵大树刮倒；在他路径两旁游荡的走兽被震得纷纷倒毙；他鼻孔里鼾声般喷出的白汽，把飞鸟崩得粉碎，还震得大团大团的云块从天上跌落下来。

===Original===
He again caught up on Cael, who was running with his head down and his toes up.

===Chinese===
他又一次追上了凯尔，凯尔正低着头、翘着脚趾地跑着。

===Original===
“If you won’t try to run, my treasure,” said the Carl, “you will never get your tribute.”

===Chinese===
“你要是不肯认真跑，我的珍宝，”卡尔说，“你的贡赋可就永远到不了手了。”

===Original===
And with that he incensed and exploded himself into an eye-blinding, continuous, waggle and complexity of boots that left Cael behind him in a flash.

===Chinese===
说罢他勃然发力，把自己炸成一片耀眼的、连绵不绝、乱纷纷的一大团靴影，一闪之间把凯尔甩在了身后。

===Original===
“I will run until I burst,” sobbed Cael, and he screwed agitation and despair into his legs until he hummed and buzzed like a bluebottle on a window.

===Chinese===
“我就是跑到炸开也要跑。”凯尔抽泣道。他把焦灼与绝望一股脑拧进两条腿里，直拧得自己像窗玻璃上的一只绿头苍蝇，嗡嗡嘤嘤地响。

===Original===
Five miles from Ben Edair the Carl stopped, for he had again come among blackberries.

===Chinese===
离本·埃代尔还有五英里，卡尔停下了脚，因为他又一头撞进了黑莓丛里。

===Original===
He ate of these until he was no more than a sack of juice, and when he heard the humming and buzzing of Cael of the Iron he mourned and lamented that he could not wait to eat his fill He took off his coat, stuffed it full of blackberries, swung it on his shoulders, and went bounding stoutly and nimbly for Ben Edair.

===Chinese===
他一口气吃到自己几乎成了一口袋果汁；听见铁凯尔那嗡嗡嘤嘤的声音逼近，他悲叹一声，怨自己不能留下吃个尽兴。他脱下外衣，塞满黑莓，往肩上一抡，便迈着又壮实又轻快的步子，连蹦带跳直奔本·埃代尔去了。

### VI / VI

===Original===
It would be hard to tell of the terror that was in Fionn’s breast and in the hearts of the Fianna while they attended the conclusion of that race.

===Chinese===
那场比赛收尾的时候，芬恩胸中和勇士团众人心头悬着怎样的恐惧，实在难以尽述。

===Original===
They discussed it unendingly, and at some moment of the day a man upbraided Fionn because he had not found Caelte the son of Ronán as had been agreed on.

===Chinese===
他们翻来覆去地议论个没完；白天某一刻，有个汉子责备起芬恩来，怪他没有按约定把罗南之子卡尔特找回来。

===Original===
“There is no one can run like Caelte,” one man averred.

===Chinese===
“谁也跑不过卡尔特。”有一个人断言。

===Original===
“He covers the ground,” said another.

===Chinese===
“他是贴着地皮飞。”另一人说。

===Original===
“He is light as a feather.”

===Chinese===
“他轻得像根羽毛。”

===Original===
“Swift as a stag.”

===Chinese===
“快得像头牡鹿。”

===Original===
“Lunged like a bull.”

===Chinese===
“肺活量赛过公牛。”

===Original===
“Legged like a wolf.”

===Chinese===
“腿脚胜过恶狼。”

===Original===
“He runs!”

===Chinese===
“那样地跑！”

===Original===
These things were said to Fionn, and Fionn said these things to himself.

===Chinese===
这些话是说给芬恩听的，芬恩又把这些话说给自己听。

===Original===
With every passing minute a drop of lead thumped down into every heart, and a pang of despair stabbed up to every brain.

===Chinese===
时间每过去一分，就有一滴铅砰地坠进每个人心里，一股绝望的剧痛向上刺进每个人的脑袋。

===Original===
“Go,” said Fionn to a hawk-eyed man, “go to the top of this hill and watch for the coming of the racers.” And he sent lithe men with him so that they might run back in endless succession with the news.

===Chinese===
“去，”芬恩对一个目光如鹰的汉子说，“到山顶上去，守望赛跑的人回来。”他又派了几个腿脚利落的跟他同去，好叫他们接二连三、源源不断地跑回来报信。

===Original===
The messengers began to run through his tent at minute intervals calling “nothing,” “nothing,” “nothing,” as they paused and darted away.

===Chinese===
信使们开始每隔一分钟便穿过他的营帐跑一趟，一迭声喊着“没有”“没有”“没有”，脚下一停便又蹿了出去。

===Original===
And the words, “nothing, nothing, nothing,” began to drowse into the brains of every person present.

===Chinese===
而“没有，没有，没有”这几个字，也开始昏昏沉沉地钻进在场每个人的脑髓里。

===Original===
“What can we hope from that Carl?” a champion demanded savagely.

===Chinese===
“那个卡尔能有什么指望？”一位好汉凶巴巴地质问。

===Original===
“Nothing,” cried a messenger who stood and sped.

===Chinese===
“没有。”一个信使喊道，话音未落人已蹿了出去。

===Original===
“A clump!” cried a champion.

===Chinese===
“一块木头疙瘩！”一位好汉喊道。

===Original===
“A hog!” said another.

===Chinese===
“一头猪！”另一位说。

===Original===
“A flat-footed.”

===Chinese===
“平脚板。”

===Original===
“Little-winded.”

===Chinese===
“短气。”

===Original===
“Big-bellied.”

===Chinese===
“大肚。”

===Original===
“Lazy-boned.”

===Chinese===
“懒骨头。”

===Original===
“Pork!”

===Chinese===
“猪肉！”

===Original===
“Did you think, Fionn, that a whale could swim on land, or what did you imagine that lump could do?”

===Chinese===
“芬恩，你莫非以为鲸鱼能在陆地上游？你当那坨货色还能干出什么来？”

===Original===
“Nothing,” cried a messenger, and was sped as he spoke.

===Chinese===
“没有。”一个信使喊道，话音没落，人已经蹿没了影。

===Original===
Rage began to gnaw in Fionn’s soul, and a red haze danced and flickered before his eyes. His hands began to twitch and a desire crept over him to seize on champions by the neck, and to shake and worry and rage among them like a wild dog raging among sheep.

===Chinese===
怒火开始啃咬芬恩的灵魂，一片红雾在他眼前舞动、闪烁。他的双手抽搐起来，一股欲望悄悄爬上他心头：要掐住这些好汉的脖子，在他们中间又摇又咬又咆哮，像一条闯进羊群里发狂的恶狗。

===Original===
He looked on one, and yet he seemed to look on all at once.

===Chinese===
他望着一个人，却又像是同时望着所有人。

===Original===
“Be silent,” he growled. “Let each man be silent as a dead man.”

===Chinese===
“闭嘴，”他低吼道，“每个人都给我像死人一样闭嘴。”

===Original===
And he sat forward, seeing all, seeing none, with his mouth drooping open, and such a wildness and bristle lowering from that great glum brow that the champions shivered as though already in the chill of death, and were silent.

===Chinese===
他朝前倾坐着，看见一切，又什么都没看见；嘴巴耷拉着张开，那阴沉的大额头上压着一股狂乱与森森戾气，众好汉直打寒战，仿佛已浸在死亡的寒意里，全都噤了声。

===Original===
He rose and stalked to the tent-door.

===Chinese===
他站起身，大步走到营帐门口。

===Original===
“Where to, O Fionn?” said a champion humbly.

===Chinese===
“去哪儿，芬恩啊？”一位好汉低声下气地问。

===Original===
“To the hilltop,” said Fionn, and he stalked on.

===Chinese===
“去山顶。”芬恩说，径自大步走了下去。

===Original===
They followed him, whispering among themselves, keeping their eyes on the ground as they climbed.

===Chinese===
众人跟上去，一路窃窃私语，爬山时眼睛只盯着地面。

### VII / VII

===Original===
“What do you see?” Fionn demanded of the watcher.

===Chinese===
“你看见什么了？”芬恩问那瞭望的人。

===Original===
“Nothing,” that man replied.

===Chinese===
“什么也没有。”那人答道。

===Original===
“Look again,” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“再看看。”芬恩说。

===Original===
The eagle-eyed man lifted a face, thin and sharp as though it had been carven on the wind, and he stared forward with an immobile intentness.

===Chinese===
那鹰眼的汉子抬起一张脸，瘦削锋利，仿佛是在风上雕出来的；他目不转睛，一动不动地朝前凝望。

===Original===
“What do you see?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“你看见什么了？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“Nothing,” the man replied.

===Chinese===
“什么也没有。”那人答道。

===Original===
“I will look myself,” said Fionn, and his great brow bent forward and gloomed afar.

===Chinese===
“我亲自看。”芬恩说。他前倾起硕大的额头，朝远方阴沉沉地望过去。

===Original===
The watcher stood beside, staring with his tense face and unwinking, lidless eye.

===Chinese===
瞭望人立在旁边，绷紧了脸，用那只不眨的、没有眼睑的眼睛凝望。

===Original===
“What can you see, O Fionn?” said the watcher.

===Chinese===
“您能看见什么，芬恩啊？”瞭望人说。

===Original===
“I can see nothing,” said Fionn, and he projected again his grim, gaunt forehead. For it seemed as if the watcher stared with his whole face, aye, and with his hands; but Fionn brooded weightedly on distance with his puckered and crannied brow.

===Chinese===
“我什么也看不见。”芬恩说着，又把自己那严厉瘦削的额头朝前投了出去。因为那瞭望人仿佛是用整张脸在凝望，是啊，连手都在凝望；芬恩却用他那皱褶纵横的额头，沉甸甸地向远方凝思。

===Original===
They looked again.

===Chinese===
两人又望了一阵。

===Original===
“What can you see?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“能看见什么了？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“I see nothing,” said the watcher.

===Chinese===
“我什么也看不见。”瞭望人说。

===Original===
“I do not know if I see or if I surmise, but something moves,” said Fionn. “There is a trample,” he said.

===Chinese===
“我不知道是看见了还是在揣度，可是有什么东西在动。”芬恩说，“有一阵踏地的声响。”他说。

===Original===
The watcher became then an eye, a rigidity, an intense out-thrusting and ransacking of thin-spun distance. At last he spoke.

===Chinese===
那瞭望人顿时化作一只眼睛、一段僵直、一束朝纤细远方猛探猛搜的劲头。他终于开口了。

===Original===
“There is a dust,” he said.

===Chinese===
“有一团尘土。”他说。

===Original===
And at that the champions gazed also, straining hungrily afar, until their eyes became filled with a blue darkness and they could no longer see even the things that were close to them.

===Chinese===
众好汉一听，也跟着眺望起来，如饥似渴地朝远方绷紧了眼睛，直到眼底灌满一片蓝色的黑暗，连近在眼前的东西都看不见了。

===Original===
“I,” cried Conán triumphantly, “I see a dust.”

===Chinese===
“我，”柯南得意洋洋地喊道，“我看见一团尘土。”

===Original===
“And I,” cried another.

===Chinese===
“我也看见了。”另一位喊道。

===Original===
“And I.”

===Chinese===
“我也看见了。”

===Original===
“I see a man,” said the eagle-eyed watcher.

===Chinese===
“我看见一个人。”鹰眼的瞭望人说。

===Original===
And again they stared, until their straining eyes grew dim with tears and winks, and they saw trees that stood up and sat down, and fields that wobbled and spun round and round in a giddily swirling world.

===Chinese===
他们又瞪眼望去，瞪到眼睛酸出泪水、眨个不停，一片模糊，只见树木立起来又坐下去，田地在一个天旋地转的世界里摇摇晃晃、团团打转。

===Original===
“There *is* a man,” Conán roared.

===Chinese===
“真的有一个人！”柯南吼道。

===Original===
“A man there is,” cried another.

===Chinese===
“是有一个。”另一位喊道。

===Original===
“And he is carrying a man on his back,” said the watcher. “It is Cael of the Iron carrying the Carl on his back,” he groaned.

===Chinese===
“而且他背上还驮着一个人，”瞭望人说，“是铁凯尔背着卡尔。”他呻吟道。

===Original===
“The great pork!” a man gritted.

===Chinese===
“好大一块肥猪肉！”一个男人咬着牙说。

===Original===
“The no-good!” sobbed another.

===Chinese===
“不成器的废物！”另一个抽泣道。

===Original===
“The lean-hearted.”

===Chinese===
“干瘪心。”

===Original===
“Thick-thighed.”

===Chinese===
“粗大腿。”

===Original===
“Ramshackle.”

===Chinese===
“散了架的货。”

===Original===
“Muddleheaded.”

===Chinese===
“一脑袋浆糊。”

===Original===
“Hog!” screamed a champion.

===Chinese===
“猪猡！”一位好汉尖叫道。

===Original===
And he beat his fists angrily against a tree.

===Chinese===
说着他怒冲冲地拿双拳捶打一棵树。

===Original===
But the eagle-eyed watcher watched until his eyes narrowed and became pinpoints, and he ceased to be a man and became an optic.

===Chinese===
可那鹰眼的瞭望人还在望，望到双眼眯成了针尖，他不再是一个人，而成了一件纯然的视觉器官。

===Original===
“Wait,” he breathed, “wait until I screw into one other inch of sight.”

===Chinese===
“等等，”他轻声说，“等我把视力再拧出多一寸来。”

===Original===
And they waited, looking no longer on that scarcely perceptible speck in the distance, but straining upon the eye of the watcher as though they would penetrate it and look through it.

===Chinese===
众人便等着，不再去看远方那个若有若无的小点，而是全都绷紧了盯住瞭望人那只眼睛，仿佛要钻进它里面，透过它去看。

===Original===
“It is the Carl,” he said, “carrying something on his back, and behind him again there is a dust.”

===Chinese===
“那是卡尔，”他说，“背上驮着个什么东西；他身后又有一团尘土。”

===Original===
“Are you sure?” said Fionn in a voice that rumbled and vibrated like thunder.

===Chinese===
“你看真切了？”芬恩说，声音像雷一样滚动、震颤。

===Original===
“It is the Carl,” said the watcher, “and the dust behind him is Cael of the Iron trying to catch him up.”

===Chinese===
“是卡尔，”瞭望人说，“他身后的尘土，是铁凯尔在拼命追赶。”

===Original===
Then the Fianna gave a roar of exultation, and each man seized his neighbour and kissed him on both cheeks; and they gripped hands about Fionn, and they danced round and round in a great circle, roaring with laughter and relief, in the ecstasy which only comes where grisly fear has been and whence that bony jowl has taken itself away.

===Chinese===
勇士团顿时爆出一声欢腾，人人抓住身旁的人，在他两边脸颊上亲吻；他们围着芬恩紧紧拉起手，绕成一个大圈团团起舞，又笑又松心地吼着，沉浸在一种狂喜里——这狂喜，唯有森然的恐惧来过、又拖着那副骸骨般的嘴脸走开了的地方，才会降临。

### VIII / VIII

===Original===
The Carl of the Drab Coat came bumping and stumping and clumping into the camp, and was surrounded by a multitude that adored him and hailed him with tears.

===Chinese===
褐衣卡尔磕磕碰碰、蹬蹬踏踏、咚咚作响地进了营地，人群立刻把他团团围住，人人敬他爱他，含着热泪向他欢呼。

===Original===
“Meal!” he bawled, “meal for the love of the stars!”

===Chinese===
“麦粉！”他大声吼着，“看在星辰的份上，给我麦粉！”

===Original===
And he bawled, “Meal, meal!” until he bawled everybody into silence.

===Chinese===
他一路吼着“麦粉！麦粉！”直吼到所有人都安静下来。

===Original===
Fionn addressed him.

===Chinese===
芬恩开口问他。

===Original===
“What for the meal, dear heart?”

===Chinese===
“要麦粉做什么用，亲爱的？”

===Original===
“For the inside of my mouth,” said the Carl, “for the recesses and crannies and deep-down profundities of my stomach. Meal, meal!” he lamented.

===Chinese===
“为了我嘴巴的里子，”卡尔说，“为了我肚子里那些旮旮旯旯、坑坑洼洼和深不见底的所在。麦粉，麦粉！”他哀叹道。

===Original===
Meal was brought.

===Chinese===
麦粉端来了。

===Original===
The Carl put his coat on the ground, opened it carefully, and revealed a store of blackberries, squashed, crushed, mangled, democratic, ill-looking.

===Chinese===
卡尔把外衣摊在地上，小心翼翼地展开，露出一大堆黑莓——压烂的、挤碎的、稀烂的、彼此不分你我的、模样难看的。

===Original===
“The meal!” he groaned, “the meal!”

===Chinese===
“麦粉呢！”他呻吟道，“麦粉！”

===Original===
It was given to him.

===Chinese===
麦粉给了他。

===Original===
“What of the race, my pulse?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“赛跑怎么样了，我的脉搏？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“Wait, wait,” cried the Carl. “I die, I die for meal and blackberries.”

===Chinese===
“等等，等等，”卡尔喊道，“我要死了，我要为麦粉和黑莓死了。”

===Original===
Into the centre of the mess of blackberries he discharged a barrel of meal, and he mixed the two up and through, and round and down, until the pile of white-black, red-brown slibber-slobber reached up to his shoulders. Then he commenced to paw and impel and project and cram the mixture into his mouth, and between each mouthful he sighed a contented sigh, and during every mouthful he gurgled an oozy gurgle.

===Chinese===
他把一桶麦粉倾进那摊黑莓的正中央，又把两样东西翻来搅去、转着圈地捣到底，直到那堆黑白间杂、红褐斑驳、稀里哗啦的浆糊堆到了他的肩膀那么高。接着他动手又扒又推又扬又塞，把那混合物往嘴里送；每两口之间，心满意足地叹一口气；每一口进行之中，又咕叽咕叽地淌着黏水响。

===Original===
But while Fionn and the Fianna stared like lost minds upon the Carl, there came a sound of buzzing, as if a hornet or a queen of the wasps or a savage, steep-winged griffin was hovering about them, and looking away they saw Cael of the Iron charging on them with a monstrous extension and scurry of his legs. He had a sword in his hand, and there was nothing in his face but redness and ferocity.

===Chinese===
可正当芬恩和勇士团失魂落魄地盯着卡尔看时，一阵嗡嗡声传来，仿佛一只大黄蜂、一只黄蜂女王，或一头凶猛的、翅膀陡斜的狮鹫正在他们头顶盘旋。众人转眼望去，只见铁凯尔双腿大张、疯狂倒腾着冲将过来。他手里提着一柄剑，脸上除了通红与狰狞，什么都没有。

===Original===
Fear fell like night around the Fianna, and they stood with slack knees and hanging hands waiting for death. But the Carl lifted a pawful of his oozy slop and discharged this at Cael with such a smash that the man’s head spun off his shoulders and hopped along the ground. The Carl then picked up the head and threw it at the body with such aim and force that the neck part of the head jammed into the neck part of the body and stuck there, as good a head as ever, you would have said, but that it had got twisted the wrong way round. The Carl then lashed his opponent hand and foot.

===Chinese===
恐惧像夜幕一样罩落在勇士团四周，众人膝弯发软、双手垂落，站在那里等死。卡尔却抄起满满一大把黏糊糊的浆糊，朝凯尔猛掷过去，这一掷势大力沉，那人的脑袋竟从肩膀上旋飞出去，在地上蹦了几蹦。卡尔捡起那颗脑袋，朝那身子掷回去，又准又狠，脑袋的脖颈部分正楔进身子的脖颈部分，牢牢卡住——若不是装反了方向，你会说这还是一颗一如既往的好脑袋。随后卡尔把他的对手连手带脚捆了个结实。

===Original===
“Now, dear heart, do you still claim tribute and lordship of Ireland?” said he.

===Chinese===
“怎么样，亲爱的，你还讨不讨爱尔兰的贡赋和宗主权了？”他说。

===Original===
“Let me go home,” groaned Cael, “I want to go home.”

===Chinese===
“放我回家吧，”凯尔呻吟道，“我要回家。”

===Original===
“Swear by the sun and moon, if I let you go home, that you will send to Fionn, yearly and every year, the rent of the land of Thessaly.”

===Chinese===
“凭着太阳和月亮起誓：若我放你回家，你要年年不落地，把色萨利土地的租赋送给芬恩。”

===Original===
“I swear that,” said Cael, “and I would swear anything to get home.”

===Chinese===
“我起誓，”凯尔说，“只要能回家，起什么誓我都干。”

===Original===
The Carl lifted him then and put him sitting into his ship. Then he raised his big boot and gave the boat a kick that drove it seven leagues out into the sea, and that was how the adventure of Cael of the Iron finished.

===Chinese===
卡尔随即把他提起来，放进他那条船里坐好；接着抬起大靴子一脚，把船踢得直驶出七里格远，进了大海——铁凯尔的这桩冒险，就这样收了场。

===Original===
“Who are you, sir?” said Fionn to the Carl.

===Chinese===
“先生，您是谁？”芬恩问卡尔。

===Original===
But before answering the Carl’s shape changed into one of splendour and delight.

===Chinese===
可不等回答，卡尔的身形已化作一片辉煌与欢欣。

===Original===
“I am ruler of the Shí of Rath Cruachan,” he said.

===Chinese===
“我是拉斯克鲁亨仙丘（the Shí of Rath Cruachan）之主。”他说。

===Original===
Then Fionn mac Uail made a feast and a banquet for the jovial god, and with that the tale is ended of the King of Thessaly’s son and the Carl of the Drab Coat.

===Chinese===
随后，尤尔之子芬恩为这位快活的神明大摆宴席；至此，色萨利国王之子与褐衣卡尔的故事，也就讲完了。

## The Enchanted Cave of Cesh Corran / 克什科兰的魔窟

### I / I

===Original===
Fionn mac Uail was the most prudent chief of an army in the world, but he was not always prudent on his own account. Discipline sometimes irked him, and he would then take any opportunity that presented for an adventure; for he was not only a soldier, he was a poet also, that is, a man of science, and whatever was strange or unusual had an irresistible attraction for him.

===Chinese===
芬恩·麦克·尤尔（Fionn mac Uail）是普天之下最审慎的兵马统领，可轮到自己的事，他却未必审慎。军纪有时叫他心烦，每逢这时，冒出来的任何冒险机会他都不肯放过；因为他不但是个军人，还是个诗人——也就是说，是个有学问的人——凡是古怪稀奇的事物，对他都有一种无法抗拒的吸引力。

===Original===
Such a soldier was he that, single-handed, he could take the Fianna out of any hole they got into, but such an inveterate poet was he that all the Fianna together could scarcely retrieve him from the abysses into which he tumbled. It took him to keep the Fianna safe, but it took all the Fianna to keep their captain out of danger. They did not complain of this, for they loved every hair of Fionn’s head more than they loved their wives and children, and that was reasonable for there was never in the world a person more worthy of love than Fionn was.

===Chinese===
论战士，他是这样一号人物：单枪匹马，也能把芬尼亚勇士团（Fianna）从他们陷进去的任何窟窿里捞出来；论诗人，他又是这样一号积习成癖的人物：全体勇士团凑在一起，也难得把他从他自己栽进去的深渊里打捞回来。保全勇士团，有他一个便够；保全他们的统领远离危险，却得全团一齐上阵。对此无人抱怨，因为他们爱芬恩头上的每一根头发，胜过爱自己的妻儿；这也在情理之中，因为世上从来不曾有过比芬恩更值得爱的人。

===Original===
Goll mac Morna did not admit so much in words, but he admitted it in all his actions, for although he never lost an opportunity of killing a member of Fionn’s family (there was deadly feud between clann-Baiscne and clann-Morna), yet a call from Fionn brought Goll raging to his assistance like a lion that rages tenderly by his mate. Not even a call was necessary, for Goll felt in his heart when Fionn was threatened, and he would leave Fionn’s own brother only half-killed to fly where his arm was wanted. He was never thanked, of course, for although Fionn loved Goll he did not like him, and that was how Goll felt towards Fionn.

===Chinese===
戈尔·麦克·莫尔纳（Goll mac Morna）嘴上不肯承认这么多，行动上却全认了：虽然他从不错过任何一个杀芬恩族人的机会——巴斯克内氏族（clann-Baiscne）与莫尔纳氏族（clann-Morna）之间，结着血海世仇——可芬恩一声召唤，戈尔便会咆哮着扑来相助，像守在伴侣身旁温柔发威的狮子。其实连召唤都用不着，因为芬恩一受威胁，戈尔心里便有感应；哪怕正把芬恩的亲兄弟打到只剩半条命，他也会撂下那人，飞身赶往用得着他臂膀的地方。当然，他从没得过一声道谢，因为芬恩虽然爱戈尔，却不欢喜他——而戈尔对芬恩，怀的也是同一样心思。

---

===Original===
Fionn, with Conán the Swearer and the dogs Bran and Sceólan, was sitting on the hunting-mound at the top of Cesh Corran. Below and around on every side the Fianna were beating the coverts in Legney and Brefny, ranging the fastnesses of Glen Dallan, creeping in the nut and beech forests of Carbury, spying among the woods of Kyle Conor, and ranging the wide plain of Moy Conal.

===Chinese===
芬恩带着“起誓者”柯南（Conán the Swearer）和布兰（Bran）、斯科兰（Sceólan）两条猎犬，坐在克什科兰（Cesh Corran）顶上的猎丘上。四下里、山脚下，勇士团正在四处搜猎：他们搜过莱格尼（Legney）与布雷夫尼（Brefny）的树丛，巡行达兰峡谷（Glen Dallan）的险塞，蹑足潜行在卡伯里（Carbury）的榛林与山毛榉林间，窥伺着凯尔·康纳（Kyle Conor）的树林，还巡防着莫伊·科纳尔（Moy Conal）的辽阔平原。

===Original===
The great captain was happy: his eyes were resting on the sights he liked best﻿—the sunlight of a clear day, the waving trees, the pure sky, and the lovely movement of the earth; and his ears were filled with delectable sounds﻿—the baying of eager dogs, the clear calling of young men, the shrill whistling that came from every side, and each sound of which told a definite thing about the hunt. There was also the plunge and scurry of the deer, the yapping of badgers, and the whirr of birds driven into reluctant flight.

===Chinese===
大统领心里快活：他眼里歇着他最爱的景致——晴日的阳光、摇曳的树木、澄净的天空，还有大地那可爱的律动；他耳中灌满了悦耳的声响——猎犬急切的吠嗥、年轻人清亮的呼喊、四面八方传来的尖利呼哨，每一声都确切地报着围猎中的一件事。此外还有鹿儿的腾跃奔窜、獾子的尖声吠叫，和鸟儿被逼得不情愿起飞时那扑棱棱的翼声。

### II / II

===Original===
Now the king of the Shí of Cesh Corran, Conaran, son of Imidel, was also watching the hunt, but Fionn did not see him, for we cannot see the people of Faery until we enter their realm, and Fionn was not thinking of Faery at that moment. Conaran did not like Fionn, and, seeing that the great champion was alone, save for Conán and the two hounds Bran and Sceólan, he thought the time had come to get Fionn into his power. We do not know what Fionn had done to Conaran, but it must have been bad enough, for the king of the Shí of Cesh Cotran was filled with joy at the sight of Fionn thus close to him, thus unprotected, thus unsuspicious.

===Chinese===
且说克什科兰仙丘之王、伊米德尔之子科纳兰（Conaran），也在观看这场围猎，芬恩却看不见他——仙灵之族（Faery）的子民，我们不踏进他们的疆土便看不见，而芬恩那一刻压根没想着仙境。科纳兰不喜欢芬恩；眼见这位大英雄孤身一人，身边只有柯南和布兰、斯科兰两条猎犬，他心想把芬恩攥进手心的时辰到了。芬恩究竟对科纳兰做过什么，我们不得而知，但想必够缺德的，因为这位仙丘之王眼看芬恩这般近在咫尺、这般毫无护卫、这般不存戒心，不由得满心欢喜。

===Original===
This Conaran had four daughters. He was fond of them and proud of them, but if one were to search the Shís of Ireland or the land of Ireland, the equal of these four would not be found for ugliness and bad humour and twisted temperaments.

===Chinese===
这位科纳兰有四个女儿。他对她们又疼爱又得意，只是任你把爱尔兰的座座仙丘搜寻个遍，把爱尔兰的土地搜寻个遍，论丑陋、论坏脾气、论七拧八别的心性，也找不出堪与这四姐妹比肩的。

===Original===
Their hair was black as ink and tough as wire: it stuck up and poked out and hung down about their heads in bushes and spikes and tangles. Their eyes were bleary and red. Their mouths were black and twisted, and in each of these mouths there was a hedge of curved yellow fangs. They had long scraggy necks that could turn all the way round like the neck of a hen. Their arms were long and skinny and muscular, and at the end of each finger they had a spiked nail that was as hard as horn and as sharp as a briar. Their bodies were covered with a bristle of hair and fur and fluff, so that they looked like dogs in some parts and like cats in others, and in other parts again they looked like chickens. They had moustaches poking under their noses and woolly wads growing out of their ears, so that when you looked at them the first time you never wanted to look at them again, and if you had to look at them a second time you were likely to die of the sight.

===Chinese===
她们的头发黑得像墨，韧得像铁丝：在脑袋四周又是支棱着，又是戳出着，又是披挂着，一丛丛、一撮撮、一团团。她们的眼睛浑浊而血红。她们的嘴乌黑歪斜，每张嘴里都长着一道篱笆似的弯弯黄獠牙。她们的脖子又长又干瘦，能像母鸡的脖子一样整圈转到背后。她们的手臂又长又瘦又多筋，每根指头尖上都生着一枚尖锥似的指甲，硬得像兽角，利得像荆棘。她们周身覆着一层刚毛、粗毛和绒毛，弄得有些地方像狗，有些地方像猫，再有些地方又像小鸡。她们鼻子底下戳出两撇髭须，耳朵里长出一团团毛絮，叫人头一回看了，此生再不想看第二回；万不得已看了第二回，多半会当场丧命。

===Original===
They were called Caevóg, Cuillen, and Iaran. The fourth daughter, Iarnach, was not present at that moment, so nothing need be said of her yet.

===Chinese===
她们一个叫卡沃格（Caevóg），一个叫库伦（Cuillen），一个叫伊阿兰（Iaran）。第四个女儿伊阿娜赫（Iarnach）当时不在场，所以暂且不必说她。

===Original===
Conaran called these three to him.

===Chinese===
科纳兰把这三个唤到跟前。

===Original===
“Fionn is alone,” said he. “Fionn is alone, my treasures.”

===Chinese===
“芬恩落了单，”他说，“芬恩落了单，我的宝贝们。”

===Original===
“Ah!” said Caevóg, and her jaw crunched upwards and stuck outwards, as was usual with her when she was satisfied.

===Chinese===
“啊！”卡沃格说。她的下颌朝上一咯吱、朝外一凸——她一称心如意，照例便是这副做派。

===Original===
“When the chance comes take it,” Conaran continued, and he smiled a black, beetle-browed, unbenevolent smile.

===Chinese===
“机会一到就下手，”科纳兰接着说，同时绽出一个黑沉沉、眉头紧锁、毫无善意的笑。

===Original===
“It’s a good word,” quoth Cuillen, and she swung her jaw loose and made it waggle up and down, for that was the way she smiled.

===Chinese===
“这话说得好。”库伦道。她把下巴甩得松松垮垮，一上一下地晃荡——她笑起来，就是这么个笑法。

===Original===
“And here is the chance,” her father added.

===Chinese===
“眼下机会就到了。”她们的父亲补上一句。

===Original===
“The chance is here,” Iaran echoed, with a smile that was very like her sister’s, only that it was worse, and the wen that grew on her nose joggled to and fro and did not get its balance again for a long time.

===Chinese===
“机会到了。”伊阿兰随声附和，脸上绽出一个同她姊妹十分相像的笑，只是更糟几分；她鼻子上长的那颗瘤子跟着摇来晃去，好半天才稳住平衡。

===Original===
Then they smiled a smile that was agreeable to their own eyes, but which would have been a deadly thing for anybody else to see.

===Chinese===
随后她们齐齐绽开一个自个儿瞧着顺心的笑——旁人若有眼福一见，那便是送命的事。

===Original===
“But Fionn cannot see us,” Caevóg objected, and her brow set downwards and her chin set upwards and her mouth squeezed sidewards, so that her face looked like a badly disappointed nut.

===Chinese===
“可芬恩看不见咱们呀，”卡沃格提出异议。她的眉毛往下一沉，下巴往上一顶，嘴巴往边上一挤，整张脸活像一颗大失所望的坚果。

===Original===
“And we are worth seeing,” Cuillen continued, and the disappointment that was set in her sister’s face got carved and twisted into hers, but it was worse in her case.

===Chinese===
“咱们可是值得一看的。”库伦接着说。她姊妹脸上定下的那份失望，被刻进她脸上又拧了几拧，只是落到她脸上，越发不堪。

===Original===
“That is the truth,” said Iaran in a voice of lamentation, and her face took on a gnarl and a writhe and a solidity of ugly woe that beat the other two and made even her father marvel.

===Chinese===
“这话不假，”伊阿兰用哭丧一般的声调说。她的脸庞添了一层虬结、一层扭绞，凝成一副丑恶的悲苦，把另外两个都比了下去，连她们的父亲也为之称奇。

===Original===
“He cannot see us now,” Conaran replied, “but he will see us in a minute.”

===Chinese===
“他这会儿看不见咱们，”科纳兰答道，“可再过一分钟就看见了。”

===Original===
“Won’t Fionn be glad when he sees us!” said the three sisters.

===Chinese===
“芬恩见了咱们，还不知要多高兴呢！”三姐妹说。

===Original===
And then they joined hands and danced joyfully around their father, and they sang a song, the first line of which is:

===Chinese===
于是她们手拉着手，围着父亲欢天喜地跳起舞来，还唱了一支歌，头一句是：

===Original===
> “Fionn thinks he is safe. But who knows when the sky will fall?”

===Chinese===
> “芬恩自以为安然无恙。可谁知道天什么时候塌下来？”

===Original===
Lots of the people in the Shí learned that song by heart, and they applied it to every kind of circumstance.

===Chinese===
仙丘里许多人把这支歌背得烂熟，什么场合都要拿来套用一番。

### III / III

===Original===
By his arts Conaran changed the sight of Fionn’s eyes, and he did the same for Conán.

===Chinese===
科纳兰施起法术，改换了芬恩眼中所见，又对柯南如法炮制。

===Original===
In a few minutes Fionn stood up from his place on the mound. Everything was about him as before, and he did not know that he had gone into Faery. He walked for a minute up and down the hillock. Then, as by chance, he stepped down the sloping end of the mound and stood with his mouth open, staring. He cried out:

===Chinese===
几分钟后，芬恩从丘顶的座位上站起。周遭一切如旧，他并不知道自己已经走进了仙境。他在小丘上来回踱了一阵，随后像是无意间，顺着土丘坡缓的一头走下去，张着嘴站定，直愣愣地望着。他喊出声来：

===Original===
“Come down here, Conán, my darling.”

===Chinese===
“下来这儿，柯南，我的宝贝。”

===Original===
Conán stepped down to him.

===Chinese===
柯南步下丘来，到他身边。

===Original===
“Am I dreaming?” Fionn demanded, and he stretched out his finger before him.

===Chinese===
“我是在做梦么？”芬恩质问，一面朝身前伸出一根手指。

===Original===
“If you are dreaming,” said Conán, “I’m dreaming too. They weren’t here a minute ago,” he stammered.

===Chinese===
“你要是在做梦，”柯南说，“那我也在做梦。方才她们还不在这儿呢。”他结结巴巴地说。

===Original===
Fionn looked up at the sky and found that it was still there. He stared to one side and saw the trees of Kyle Conor waving in the distance. He bent his ear to the wind and heard the shouting of hunters, the yapping of dogs, and the clear whistles, which told how the hunt was going.

===Chinese===
芬恩抬头望天，发觉天还好好地在原处。他朝一旁望去，看见凯尔·康纳的树林在远处摇曳。他侧耳迎着风，听见猎人的呼喊、猎犬的吠叫和清亮的呼哨——这些声响正报着围猎进行得怎样。

===Original===
“Well!” said Fionn to himself.

===Chinese===
“嘿！”芬恩自语道。

===Original===
“By my hand!” quoth Conán to his own soul.

===Chinese===
“凭我的手起誓！”柯南对自己的灵魂言道。

===Original===
And the two men stared into the hillside as though what they were looking at was too wonderful to be looked away from.

===Chinese===
两人直勾勾盯住山坡，仿佛眼前的事物奇妙得叫人不忍把视线挪开。

===Original===
“Who are they?” said Fionn.

===Chinese===
“她们是谁？”芬恩说。

===Original===
“What are they?” Conán gasped.

===Chinese===
“她们是什么？”柯南倒抽一口气。

===Original===
And they stared again.

===Chinese===
两人又盯了过去。

===Original===
For there was a great hole like a doorway in the side of the mound, and in that doorway the daughters of Conaran sat spinning. They had three crooked sticks of holly set up before the cave, and they were reeling yarn off these. But it was enchantment they were weaving.

===Chinese===
原来土丘一侧开着一个大门似的窟窿，科纳兰的女儿们正坐在门洞里纺纱。她们在洞前立起三根歪歪扭扭的冬青棍，从上面抽纱绕线。可她们织的哪里是纱线，分明是妖术。

===Original===
“One could not call them handsome,” said Conán.

===Chinese===
“谁也没法管她们叫美人。”柯南说。

===Original===
“One could,” Fionn replied, “but it would not be true.”

===Chinese===
“叫倒是可以叫的，”芬恩答道，“可那不是实话。”

===Original===
“I cannot see them properly,” Fionn complained. “They are hiding behind the holly.”

===Chinese===
“我看她们看不真切，”芬恩抱怨道，“她们躲在冬青后头呢。”

===Original===
“I would be contented if I could not see them at all,” his companion grumbled.

===Chinese===
“要是能干脆看不见她们，我倒心满意足了。”他的同伴咕哝道。

===Original===
But the Chief insisted.

===Chinese===
可统领偏要弄个明白。

===Original===
“I want to make sure that it is whiskers they are wearing.”

===Chinese===
“我非要确认清楚，她们脸上长的到底是不是颊须。”

===Original===
“Let them wear whiskers or not wear them,” Conán counselled. “But let us have nothing to do with them.”

===Chinese===
“管她们长不长颊须，”柯南进言道，“咱们别同她们沾惹就是了。”

===Original===
“One must not be frightened of anything,” Fionn stated.

===Chinese===
“人什么都不许怕，”芬恩申明。

===Original===
“I am not frightened,” Conán explained. “I only want to keep my good opinion of women, and if the three yonder are women, then I feel sure I shall begin to dislike females from this minute out.”

===Chinese===
“我不是怕，”柯南解释道，“我只是想保全我对女人的好感。那边那三位倘若也算女人，那我可以肯定，打从这一分钟起，我就要开始厌恶女性了。”

===Original===
“Come on, my love,” said Fionn, “for I must find out if these whiskers are true.”

===Chinese===
“来吧，我的心肝，”芬恩说，“这颊须是真是假，我非要查个水落石出。”

===Original===
He strode resolutely into the cave. He pushed the branches of holly aside and marched up to Conaran’s daughters, with Conán behind him.

===Chinese===
他毅然大步走进洞去。他拨开冬青枝，径直朝科纳兰的女儿们走去，柯南跟在背后。

### IV / IV

===Original===
The instant they passed the holly a strange weakness came over the heroes.
